VOID OPTIMIZER 3.0.0 BUILD INFO
==============================
Target: Windows 10/11 x64
Go: 1.23.2
Build: GOOS=windows GOARCH=amd64 CGO_ENABLED=0
GUI: -H windowsgui -s -w -trimpath
Features: PC optimizer, Windows tweaks, display manager, Rust CFG/binds, Rust+
links, Steam playtime tracker, VOID Radar Rust+ direct WebSocket client.
Radar: TeamInfo death/online state, map markers, Team Chat sendTeamMessage,
local notifications and live UI feed.
Verification: Windows x64 cross-compile; targeted RustPlus unit tests passed.
Known full-suite failures: two old Rust CFG tests (water.quality allow-list and
grass quality tier expectation), unrelated to Radar.
Not performed: Windows GUI launch, live pairing, live Rust server connection,
or live Team Chat send. Newer Facepunch Companion Socket/auth-token route is not
yet implemented; direct Rust+ app.port route may not work on every server/network.

====================================================================================================
FILE: README_RU.txt
====================================================================================================
VOID OPTIMIZER 3.0.0 — WINDOWS 10/11 x64
=========================================

VOID RADAR · RUST+ TEAM CHAT
----------------------------
• Без серверного uMod/Oxide/Carbon-плагина и без администраторского доступа.
• При смерти участника команды отправляет в игровой Team Chat ник и grid:
  [VOID] Nick погиб! Квадрат: G14
• Также может сообщать об online-статусе тиммейтов и доступных Rust+ маркерах
  Cargo Ship, CH-47, Crate и Patrol Helicopter.
• Встроенный LIVE FEED и Windows-уведомления.

Требуются Rust+ pairing и членство в игровой команде. Текущий сетевой режим
использует прямой WebSocket к Rust+ app.port. Некоторые серверы/сети могут
требовать новый Facepunch Companion Socket, который пока не поддержан.
Rust+ не отдаёт все серверные события: Oil Rig и отдельные local hooks без
серверного источника недоступны.

Подробная инструкция — RUSTPLUS-SETUP-RU.txt. Пароль Steam и Steam Guard-коды
в VOID не вводи; PlayerToken не передавай другим людям.

Сохранены оптимизация Windows/ПК, Rust CFG и бинды, управление разрешением,
раздел RUST+ и Steam Player Tracker.

Сборка: исходники Go в source/. Для пересборки на Windows запусти build.bat.
GUI и живой сервер в этой среде не запускались; см. BUILD-INFO.txt.

====================================================================================================
FILE: RELEASE-NOTES-RU.txt
====================================================================================================
VOID OPTIMIZER 3.0.0 — VOID RADAR ЧЕРЕЗ RUST+
============================================

• Удалена ошибочная server-plugin/webhook-архитектура.
• Удалены файлы uMod/Oxide/Carbon plugin и инструкции установки плагина.
• VOID Radar теперь использует Rust+ Companion WebSocket API.
• Смерть тиммейта: ник + квадрат карты отправляются прямо в игровой Team Chat.
• В Team Chat также публикуются смены online-статуса и доступные события-маркеры
  Cargo Ship, CH-47, Crate и Patrol Helicopter.
• Добавлены pairing-валидация, protobuf-разбор TeamInfo/MapMarkers и grid-тесты.
• PlayerToken сохраняется локально и не выводится в журнал.

ОГРАНИЧЕНИЯ
-----------
Требуется включённый Rust+, Pair with Server и участие в команде. Текущая сборка
поддерживает прямой WebSocket на Rust+ app.port. Некоторые серверы/сети могут
требовать новый Facepunch Companion Socket, который ещё не реализован в этой
сборке. Доступны только данные, реально отдаваемые Rust+ API; Oil Rig и часть
локальных серверных событий без серверного доступа не видны.

ПРОВЕРКИ
--------
Целевые Rust+ unit tests проходят. Полный набор Go-тестов имеет две старые ошибки
в тестах Rust CFG (water.quality и grass quality tier), не связанные с VOID Radar.
Windows GUI и подключение к живому Rust+ серверу не запускались.

====================================================================================================
FILE: RUSTPLUS-SETUP-RU.txt
====================================================================================================
VOID RADAR — ПОДКЛЮЧЕНИЕ ЧЕРЕЗ RUST+
===================================

ЧТО БУДЕТ ПРОИСХОДИТЬ
---------------------
Когда VOID обнаружит, что участник твоей Rust-команды погиб, он отправит
сообщение в обычный игровой Team Chat:

    [VOID] Nick погиб! Квадрат: G14

Также могут отправляться сообщения о входе/выходе тиммейта и о маркерах
Cargo Ship, CH-47, Crate и Patrol Helicopter, если сервер отдаёт их Rust+.

ПОДГОТОВКА PAIRING
------------------
1. Установи Node.js LTS, если он ещё не установлен.
2. Запусти файл VOID-RUSTPLUS-PAIR.bat из полного ZIP-комплекта.
3. При первом запуске откроется окно Chrome для регистрации Rust Companion.
   Авторизуйся через Steam только на официальной странице Steam. Не вводи пароль
   Steam или Steam Guard-коды в консоль или в VOID.
4. После регистрации консоль будет ждать pairing-уведомление.
5. Запусти Rust, открой меню Rust+ и нажми Pair with Server для нужного сервера.
6. В консоли появятся значения. Перенеси их в VOID:
   • ip          → поле «АДРЕС СЕРВЕРА»
   • port        → поле «RUST+ APP PORT»
   • playerId    → поле «STEAMID64 / PLAYER ID»
   • playerToken → поле «PLAYER TOKEN»
7. В VOID открой VOID RADAR, вставь четыре значения и нажми
   «СОХРАНИТЬ И ПОДКЛЮЧИТЬ».
8. Оставь VOID запущенным и убедись, что состоишь в игровой команде.

ВАЖНО: port — это Rust+ app.port из Pairing, а не игровой порт подключения
(часто 28015). Не подставляй игровой порт наугад.

ПОВТОРНОЕ ПОДКЛЮЧЕНИЕ
--------------------
Pairing нужно получить для каждого сервера отдельно. Сохранённые значения VOID
оставляет локально. При повторной привязке сервера получи новые данные через
Pair with Server и обнови поля.

БЕЗОПАСНОСТЬ
------------
PlayerToken — секретный токен привязки. Не отправляй его никому и не публикуй
скриншоты, где он виден. Не передавай Steam-пароль, Steam Guard-коды или файлы
профиля кому-либо. Если токен стал известен другим, перепривяжи сервер через
Rust+.

ОГРАНИЧЕНИЯ
-----------
• На сервере должен работать Rust+ Companion, и pairing должен быть доступен.
• Ты должен состоять в игровой команде, чтобы получать TeamInfo и отправлять
  сообщения в Team Chat.
• Эта версия подключается напрямую к Rust+ app.port. Facepunch постепенно
  переводит Rust+ на Companion Socket; некоторые серверы или сети уже могут
  требовать новый маршрут, который эта сборка пока не поддерживает.
• Rust+ может отдать только часть событий. Cargo/CH-47/Crate/Patrol Heli доступны
  как map markers, но Oil Rig/Harbor/Hack и другие серверные хуки не гарантируются.
• VOID не читает память Rust, не внедряет код в игру и не обходит античит.

STEAM PLAYER TRACKER (ОТДЕЛЬНО)
-------------------------------
Steam Web API Key в разделе RUST+ нужен только для просмотра публичных игровых
часов профилей. Он не нужен для VOID Radar и отправки Team Chat сообщений.

FILE: add_icon_resource.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Add the app's multi-size .ico as native Windows EXE icon resources.

Usage: python add_icon_resource.py VOID-OPTIMIZER.exe assets/void-icon.ico
This script patches a freshly built PE32+ Windows executable in place.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import math
import os
import struct
import sys
from pathlib import Path


def align(value: int, alignment: int) -> int:
    return (value + alignment - 1) // alignment * alignment


def directory_header(id_count: int) -> bytes:
    return struct.pack("<IIHHHH", 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, id_count)


def resource_entry(resource_id: int, offset: int, is_directory: bool) -> bytes:
    target = offset | (0x80000000 if is_directory else 0)
    return struct.pack("<II", resource_id, target)


def make_resource_blob(ico: bytes, section_rva: int) -> bytes:
    if len(ico) < 6:
        raise ValueError("ICO file is truncated")
    reserved, kind, count = struct.unpack_from("<HHH", ico, 0)
    if reserved != 0 or kind != 1 or count < 1:
        raise ValueError("Invalid ICO header")
    images = []
    entries = []
    for index in range(count):
        off = 6 + index * 16
        if off + 16 > len(ico):
            raise ValueError("ICO directory is truncated")
        width, height, colors, reserved_byte, planes, bit_count, size, image_off = struct.unpack_from("<BBBBHHII", ico, off)
        if image_off + size > len(ico):
            raise ValueError("ICO image data is outside the file")
        images.append(ico[image_off:image_off + size])
        entries.append((width, height, colors, reserved_byte, planes, bit_count, size, index + 1))

    root_off = 0
    icon_type_off = 32
    group_type_off = icon_type_off + 16 + count * 8
    icon_lang_start = group_type_off + 16 + 8
    group_lang_off = icon_lang_start + count * 24
    data_entries_off = group_lang_off + 24
    payload_off = align(data_entries_off + (count + 1) * 16, 4)

    image_offsets = []
    cursor = payload_off
    for image in images:
        cursor = align(cursor, 4)
        image_offsets.append(cursor)
        cursor += len(image)
    group_icon = bytearray(struct.pack("<HHH", 0, 1, count))
    for width, height, colors, reserved_byte, planes, bit_count, size, resource_id in entries:
        group_icon += struct.pack("<BBBBHHIH", width, height, colors, reserved_byte, planes, bit_count, size, resource_id)
    group_data_off = align(cursor, 4)
    total_size = group_data_off + len(group_icon)
    blob = bytearray(total_size)

    # Root: RT_ICON (3) and RT_GROUP_ICON (14).
    blob[root_off:root_off + 16] = directory_header(2)
    blob[root_off + 16:root_off + 24] = resource_entry(3, icon_type_off, True)
    blob[root_off + 24:root_off + 32] = resource_entry(14, group_type_off, True)

    # RT_ICON -> icon ID -> language 0x409 -> data entry.
    blob[icon_type_off:icon_type_off + 16] = directory_header(count)
    for i in range(count):
        lang_off = icon_lang_start + i * 24
        blob[icon_type_off + 16 + i * 8:icon_type_off + 24 + i * 8] = resource_entry(i + 1, lang_off, True)
        blob[lang_off:lang_off + 16] = directory_header(1)
        blob[lang_off + 16:lang_off + 24] = resource_entry(0x409, data_entries_off + i * 16, False)
        data_off = image_offsets[i]
        blob[data_entries_off + i * 16:data_entries_off + (i + 1) * 16] = struct.pack("<IIII", section_rva + data_off, len(images[i]), 0, 0)
        blob[data_off:data_off + len(images[i])] = images[i]

    # RT_GROUP_ICON -> group ID 1 -> language 0x409 -> group data entry.
    blob[group_type_off:group_type_off + 16] = directory_header(1)
    blob[group_type_off + 16:group_type_off + 24] = resource_entry(1, group_lang_off, True)
    blob[group_lang_off:group_lang_off + 16] = directory_header(1)
    blob[group_lang_off + 16:group_lang_off + 24] = resource_entry(0x409, data_entries_off + count * 16, False)
    blob[data_entries_off + count * 16:data_entries_off + (count + 1) * 16] = struct.pack("<IIII", section_rva + group_data_off, len(group_icon), 0, 0)
    blob[group_data_off:group_data_off + len(group_icon)] = group_icon
    return bytes(blob)


def patch_exe(exe_path: Path, ico_path: Path) -> None:
    data = bytearray(exe_path.read_bytes())
    ico = ico_path.read_bytes()
    if data[:2] != b"MZ":
        raise ValueError("Not an MZ executable")
    pe_off = struct.unpack_from("<I", data, 0x3C)[0]
    if data[pe_off:pe_off + 4] != b"PE\0\0":
        raise ValueError("Invalid PE signature")
    coff = pe_off + 4
    machine, number_sections = struct.unpack_from("<HH", data, coff)
    if machine != 0x8664:
        raise ValueError("Expected an x64 executable")
    optional_size = struct.unpack_from("<H", data, coff + 16)[0]
    optional = coff + 20
    magic = struct.unpack_from("<H", data, optional)[0]
    if magic != 0x20B:
        raise ValueError("Expected a PE32+ optional header")
    section_alignment = struct.unpack_from("<I", data, optional + 32)[0]
    file_alignment = struct.unpack_from("<I", data, optional + 36)[0]
    size_of_headers = struct.unpack_from("<I", data, optional + 60)[0]
    num_dirs = struct.unpack_from("<I", data, optional + 108)[0]
    if num_dirs < 3:
        raise ValueError("PE has no resource data-directory slot")
    resource_dir_entry = optional + 112 + 2 * 8
    old_rva, old_size = struct.unpack_from("<II", data, resource_dir_entry)

    section_table = optional + optional_size
    sections = []
    max_va_end = 0
    for i in range(number_sections):
        off = section_table + i * 40
        name = bytes(data[off:off + 8]).rstrip(b"\0").decode("ascii", "replace")
        virtual_size, virtual_address, raw_size, raw_ptr = struct.unpack_from("<IIII", data, off + 8)
        sections.append((name, virtual_size, virtual_address, raw_size, raw_ptr))
        max_va_end = max(max_va_end, virtual_address + max(virtual_size, raw_size))

    # Recent Go toolchains may already emit a .rsrc section containing a
    # generic icon group. Replace that icon-only tree in place when possible.
    if old_rva or old_size:
        if not old_rva or not old_size:
            raise ValueError("PE resource directory is malformed")
        target = None
        for item in sections:
            name, virtual_size, virtual_address, raw_size, raw_ptr = item
            span = max(virtual_size, raw_size)
            if virtual_address <= old_rva < virtual_address + span:
                target = item
                break
        if target is None:
            raise ValueError("Existing resource directory is not inside a section")
        name, virtual_size, virtual_address, raw_size, raw_ptr = target
        if name != ".rsrc":
            raise ValueError(f"Existing resource directory is in unexpected section {name!r}")
        resource_blob = make_resource_blob(ico, old_rva)
        if len(resource_blob) > raw_size:
            raise ValueError("New icon resources do not fit in the existing .rsrc section")
        new_bytes = resource_blob + b"\0" * (raw_size - len(resource_blob))
        data[raw_ptr:raw_ptr + raw_size] = new_bytes
        section_header = section_table + sections.index(target) * 40
        struct.pack_into("<I", data, section_header + 8, len(resource_blob))
        struct.pack_into("<II", data, resource_dir_entry, old_rva, len(resource_blob))
        exe_path.write_bytes(data)
        return

    new_header = section_table + number_sections * 40
    if new_header + 40 > size_of_headers:
        raise ValueError("Not enough spare section-header space in PE headers")

    section_va = align(max_va_end, section_alignment)
    # First pass builds the resource tree. Its payload entries are relative to this RVA.
    resource_blob = make_resource_blob(ico, section_va)
    raw_ptr = align(len(data), file_alignment)
    raw_size = align(len(resource_blob), file_alignment)
    data.extend(b"\0" * (raw_ptr - len(data)))
    data.extend(resource_blob)
    data.extend(b"\0" * (raw_size - len(resource_blob)))

    # Add a read-only initialized-data section.
    section_name = b".rsrc\0\0\0"
    header = bytearray(40)
    header[0:8] = section_name
    struct.pack_into("<IIIIIIHHI", header, 8,
                     len(resource_blob), section_va, raw_size, raw_ptr,
                     0, 0, 0, 0, 0x40000040)
    data[new_header:new_header + 40] = header
    struct.pack_into("<H", data, coff + 2, number_sections + 1)
    struct.pack_into("<I", data, optional + 8,
                     struct.unpack_from("<I", data, optional + 8)[0] + raw_size)
    struct.pack_into("<I", data, optional + 56, align(section_va + len(resource_blob), section_alignment))
    struct.pack_into("<II", data, resource_dir_entry, section_va, len(resource_blob))
    exe_path.write_bytes(data)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) != 3:
        raise SystemExit("Usage: python add_icon_resource.py VOID-OPTIMIZER.exe assets/void-icon.ico")
    patch_exe(Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2]))
    print(f"Added native Windows icon resource to {sys.argv[1]}")

FILE: background.go
====================================================================================================
//go:build windows

package main

import (
	"bytes"
	"image"
	_ "image/png"
	"runtime"
	"unsafe"
)

const biRGB = 0

var cardFillDC uintptr
var cardFillBitmap uintptr
var cardFillColor uint32

type bitmapInfoHeader struct {
	Size          uint32
	Width         int32
	Height        int32
	Planes        uint16
	BitCount      uint16
	Compression   uint32
	SizeImage     uint32
	XPelsPerMeter int32
	YPelsPerMeter int32
	ClrUsed       uint32
	ClrImportant  uint32
}

type bitmapInfo struct {
	Header bitmapInfoHeader
	Colors [1]uint32
}

type blendFunction struct {
	Operation           byte
	Flags               byte
	SourceConstantAlpha byte
	AlphaFormat         byte
}

func createBackgroundBitmap() {
	if len(embeddedBackground) == 0 || app.BackgroundDC != 0 {
		return
	}
	img, _, err := image.Decode(bytes.NewReader(embeddedBackground))
	if err != nil || img == nil {
		return
	}
	bounds := img.Bounds()
	w, h := bounds.Dx(), bounds.Dy()
	if w < 1 || h < 1 || w > 4096 || h > 4096 {
		return
	}
	info := bitmapInfo{Header: bitmapInfoHeader{Size: uint32(unsafe.Sizeof(bitmapInfoHeader{})), Width: int32(w), Height: -int32(h), Planes: 1, BitCount: 32, Compression: biRGB}}
	var bits uintptr
	bitmap, _, _ := pCreateDIBSection.Call(0, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&info)), 0, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&bits)), 0, 0)
	runtime.KeepAlive(&info)
	if bitmap == 0 || bits == 0 {
		if bitmap != 0 {
			pDeleteObject.Call(bitmap)
		}
		return
	}
	pixels := unsafe.Slice((*byte)(unsafe.Pointer(bits)), w*h*4)
	for y := 0; y < h; y++ {
		for x := 0; x < w; x++ {
			r, g, b, a := img.At(bounds.Min.X+x, bounds.Min.Y+y).RGBA()
			// Keep the decorative art deliberately translucent so every control
			// and text label remains legible over it. AlphaBlend needs premultiplied BGRA.
			// SourceConstantAlpha is set to 255 when drawing, so this per-pixel
			// opacity is the only global dimming factor. Keep the artwork clearly
			// visible while leaving room for the translucent cards and readable UI.
			const opacity = uint32(100)
			a8 := uint8((a >> 8) * opacity / 100)
			r8 := uint8((r >> 8) * opacity / 100)
			g8 := uint8((g >> 8) * opacity / 100)
			b8 := uint8((b >> 8) * opacity / 100)
			i := (y*w + x) * 4
			pixels[i+0], pixels[i+1], pixels[i+2], pixels[i+3] = b8, g8, r8, a8
		}
	}
	dc, _, _ := pCreateCompatibleDC.Call(0)
	if dc == 0 {
		pDeleteObject.Call(bitmap)
		return
	}
	pSelectObject.Call(dc, bitmap)
	app.BackgroundDC = dc
	app.BackgroundBitmap = bitmap
	app.BackgroundWidth = w
	app.BackgroundHeight = h
	runtime.KeepAlive(img)
	runtime.KeepAlive(pixels)
}

func drawBackgroundArt(hdc uintptr, r rect) {
	if app.BackgroundDC == 0 || app.BackgroundBitmap == 0 || app.BackgroundWidth <= 0 || app.BackgroundHeight <= 0 {
		return
	}
	w, h := int(r.Right-r.Left), int(r.Bottom-r.Top)
	if w <= 0 || h <= 0 {
		return
	}
	blend := blendFunction{Operation: 0, Flags: 0, SourceConstantAlpha: 255, AlphaFormat: 1}
	pAlphaBlend.Call(hdc,
		uintptr(r.Left), uintptr(r.Top), uintptr(w), uintptr(h),
		app.BackgroundDC, 0, 0, uintptr(app.BackgroundWidth), uintptr(app.BackgroundHeight),
		uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&blend)))
	runtime.KeepAlive(&blend)
}

// drawCardFill paints a translucent card surface over the embedded artwork.
// The cached 1x1 premultiplied-alpha DIB is stretched by AlphaBlend, avoiding
// a new GDI allocation for every card on every repaint.
func drawCardFill(hdc uintptr, r rect) bool {
	if cardFillDC == 0 || cardFillColor != colCard {
		if cardFillDC != 0 {
			pDeleteDC.Call(cardFillDC)
			cardFillDC = 0
		}
		if cardFillBitmap != 0 {
			pDeleteObject.Call(cardFillBitmap)
			cardFillBitmap = 0
		}
		info := bitmapInfo{Header: bitmapInfoHeader{Size: uint32(unsafe.Sizeof(bitmapInfoHeader{})), Width: 1, Height: -1, Planes: 1, BitCount: 32, Compression: biRGB}}
		var bits uintptr
		bitmap, _, _ := pCreateDIBSection.Call(0, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&info)), 0, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&bits)), 0, 0)
		runtime.KeepAlive(&info)
		if bitmap == 0 || bits == 0 {
			if bitmap != 0 {
				pDeleteObject.Call(bitmap)
			}
			return false
		}
		// COLORREF is 0x00BBGGRR. Windows AlphaBlend expects premultiplied BGRA.
		const alpha = uint32(165) // about 65% opaque; reveal the full-screen artwork more clearly
		r8 := uint32(colCard & 0xFF)
		g8 := uint32((colCard >> 8) & 0xFF)
		b8 := uint32((colCard >> 16) & 0xFF)
		pixel := unsafe.Slice((*byte)(unsafe.Pointer(bits)), 4)
		pixel[0] = byte(b8 * alpha / 255)
		pixel[1] = byte(g8 * alpha / 255)
		pixel[2] = byte(r8 * alpha / 255)
		pixel[3] = byte(alpha)
		dc, _, _ := pCreateCompatibleDC.Call(0)
		if dc == 0 {
			pDeleteObject.Call(bitmap)
			return false
		}
		pSelectObject.Call(dc, bitmap)
		cardFillDC, cardFillBitmap, cardFillColor = dc, bitmap, colCard
		runtime.KeepAlive(pixel)
	}
	w, h := int(r.Right-r.Left), int(r.Bottom-r.Top)
	if w <= 0 || h <= 0 {
		return false
	}
	blend := blendFunction{Operation: 0, Flags: 0, SourceConstantAlpha: 255, AlphaFormat: 1}
	ok, _, _ := pAlphaBlend.Call(hdc,
		uintptr(r.Left), uintptr(r.Top), uintptr(w), uintptr(h),
		cardFillDC, 0, 0, 1, 1, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&blend)))
	runtime.KeepAlive(&blend)
	return ok != 0
}

func releaseCardFillSurface() {
	if cardFillDC != 0 {
		pDeleteDC.Call(cardFillDC)
		cardFillDC = 0
	}
	if cardFillBitmap != 0 {
		pDeleteObject.Call(cardFillBitmap)
		cardFillBitmap = 0
	}
	cardFillColor = 0
}

  pause
  exit /b 1
)
set GOOS=windows
set GOARCH=amd64

go build -ldflags="-H windowsgui -s -w" -trimpath -o VOID-OPTIMIZER-v3.0.0.exe .
if errorlevel 1 (
  echo Build failed.
  pause
  exit /b 1
)
where py >nul 2>nul
if not errorlevel 1 (
  py -3 add_icon_resource.py VOID-OPTIMIZER-v3.0.0.exe assets\void-icon.ico
  if errorlevel 1 echo Warning: EXE built, but Explorer icon embedding failed.
) else (
  echo Python 3 not found: window icon still loads, Explorer may show default icon.
)
echo Built: %~dp0VOID-OPTIMIZER-v3.0.0.exe

FILE: display.go

//go:build windows

package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"runtime"
	"sort"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"syscall"
	"unsafe"
)

const (
	enumCurrentSettings = 0xFFFFFFFF
	cdsTest             = 0x00000002
	dmBitsPerPel        = 0x00040000
	dmPelsWidth         = 0x00080000
	dmPelsHeight        = 0x00100000
	dmDisplayFrequency  = 0x00400000
)

type displayMode struct {
	Width, Height, Refresh, Bits int
}

type devModeW struct {
	DeviceName         [32]uint16
	SpecVersion        uint16
	DriverVersion      uint16
	Size               uint16
	DriverExtra        uint16
	Fields             uint32
	PositionX          int32
	PositionY          int32
	DisplayOrientation uint32
	DisplayFixedOutput uint32
	Color              uint16
	Duplex             uint16
	YResolution        uint16
	TTOption           uint16
	Collate            uint16
	FormName           [32]uint16
	LogPixels          uint16
	BitsPerPel         uint32
	PelsWidth          uint32
	PelsHeight         uint32
	DisplayFlags       uint32
	DisplayFrequency   uint32
	ICMMethod          uint32
	ICMIntent          uint32
	MediaType          uint32
	DitherType         uint32
	Reserved1          uint32
	Reserved2          uint32
	PanningWidth       uint32
	PanningHeight      uint32
}

var (
	pEnumDisplaySettings     = user32.NewProc("EnumDisplaySettingsW")
	pChangeDisplaySettingsEx = user32.NewProc("ChangeDisplaySettingsExW")
)

func modeToDevMode(mode displayMode) devModeW {
	// Change only geometry. Forcing the current bit depth can make an otherwise
	// supported resolution fail on HDR, 10-bit, virtual and hybrid display paths.
	dm := devModeW{Size: uint16(unsafe.Sizeof(devModeW{})), Fields: dmPelsWidth | dmPelsHeight}
	dm.PelsWidth = uint32(mode.Width)
	dm.PelsHeight = uint32(mode.Height)
	if mode.Refresh > 0 {
		dm.Fields |= dmDisplayFrequency
		dm.DisplayFrequency = uint32(mode.Refresh)
	}
	return dm
}

func readDisplayMode(index uint32) (displayMode, bool) {
	var dm devModeW
	dm.Size = uint16(unsafe.Sizeof(dm))
	ok, _, _ := pEnumDisplaySettings.Call(0, uintptr(index), uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&dm)))
	if ok == 0 || dm.PelsWidth == 0 || dm.PelsHeight == 0 {
		return displayMode{}, false
	}
	return displayMode{Width: int(dm.PelsWidth), Height: int(dm.PelsHeight), Refresh: int(dm.DisplayFrequency), Bits: int(dm.BitsPerPel)}, true
}

func refreshDisplayModes() {
	current, ok := readDisplayMode(enumCurrentSettings)
	if ok {
		app.CurrentDisplayMode = current
	}
	modes := make([]displayMode, 0, 64)
	seen := map[string]bool{}
	for i := uint32(0); i < 4096; i++ {
		m, found := readDisplayMode(i)
		if !found {
			break
		}
		if m.Width < 800 || m.Height < 600 || m.Width > 7680 || m.Height > 4320 || m.Refresh < 20 || m.Refresh > 500 {
			continue
		}
		key := fmt.Sprintf("%dx%d@%d", m.Width, m.Height, m.Refresh)
		if !seen[key] {
			seen[key] = true
			modes = append(modes, m)
		}
	}
	if ok {
		key := fmt.Sprintf("%dx%d@%d", current.Width, current.Height, current.Refresh)
		if !seen[key] {
			modes = append(modes, current)
		}
	}
	sort.Slice(modes, func(i, j int) bool {
		if modes[i].Width != modes[j].Width {
			return modes[i].Width < modes[j].Width
		}
		if modes[i].Height != modes[j].Height {
			return modes[i].Height < modes[j].Height
		}
		return modes[i].Refresh > modes[j].Refresh
	})
	app.MonitorModes = modes
	app.MonitorIndex = 0
	for i, m := range modes {
		if ok && m.Width == current.Width && m.Height == current.Height && m.Refresh == current.Refresh {
			app.MonitorIndex = i
			break
		}
	}
}

func displayModeLabel(m displayMode) string {
	if m.Width == 0 || m.Height == 0 {
		return "Режим не определён"
	}
	if m.Refresh > 0 {
		return fmt.Sprintf("%d × %d  •  %d Гц", m.Width, m.Height, m.Refresh)
	}
	return fmt.Sprintf("%d × %d", m.Width, m.Height)
}

func applyDisplayMode(mode displayMode) {
	if mode.Width < 800 || mode.Height < 600 || mode.Width > 7680 || mode.Height > 4320 {
		app.Status = "Недопустимое разрешение. Допустимый диапазон: 800×600 — 7680×4320."
		if app.Language == "en" {
			app.Status = "Invalid resolution. Allowed range: 800×600 to 7680×4320."
		}
		return
	}
	dm := modeToDevMode(mode)
	// Some display drivers report a mode at a nominal refresh rate but reject
	// applying it explicitly. First try the requested refresh; if the driver
	// rejects it, let Windows select the refresh rate for the chosen geometry.
	result, _, _ := pChangeDisplaySettingsEx.Call(0, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&dm)), 0, cdsTest, 0)
	if int32(result) != 0 && dm.Fields&dmDisplayFrequency != 0 {
		dm.Fields &^= dmDisplayFrequency
		dm.DisplayFrequency = 0
		result, _, _ = pChangeDisplaySettingsEx.Call(0, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&dm)), 0, cdsTest, 0)
	}
	if int32(result) != 0 {
		app.Status = fmt.Sprintf("Драйвер монитора не поддерживает режим %s (код %d). Текущий экран не изменён.", displayModeLabel(mode), int32(result))
		if app.Language == "en" {
			app.Status = fmt.Sprintf("The display driver rejected %s (code %d). The current mode was not changed.", displayModeLabel(mode), int32(result))
		}
		runtime.KeepAlive(&dm)
		return
	}
	old := app.CurrentDisplayMode
	result, _, _ = pChangeDisplaySettingsEx.Call(0, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&dm)), 0, 0, 0)
	if int32(result) != 0 && dm.Fields&dmDisplayFrequency != 0 {
		// Retry once without a forced refresh rate; geometry remains the same.
		dm.Fields &^= dmDisplayFrequency
		dm.DisplayFrequency = 0
		testResult, _, _ := pChangeDisplaySettingsEx.Call(0, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&dm)), 0, cdsTest, 0)
		if int32(testResult) == 0 {
			result, _, _ = pChangeDisplaySettingsEx.Call(0, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&dm)), 0, 0, 0)
		}
	}
	runtime.KeepAlive(&dm)
	if int32(result) != 0 {
		app.Status = fmt.Sprintf("Windows не смогла применить режим (код %d).", int32(result))
		if app.Language == "en" {
			app.Status = fmt.Sprintf("Windows could not apply the display mode (code %d).", int32(result))
		}
		return
	}
	if old.Width > 0 && old.Height > 0 && (old.Width != mode.Width || old.Height != mode.Height || old.Refresh != mode.Refresh) {
		app.PreviousDisplayMode = old
		app.HasPreviousDisplayMode = true
	}
	app.CurrentDisplayMode = mode
	refreshDisplayModes()
	app.Status = "Разрешение применено: " + displayModeLabel(app.CurrentDisplayMode) + ". Если картинка отображается некорректно, используй «Вернуть предыдущее»."
	if app.Language == "en" {
		app.Status = "Resolution applied: " + displayModeLabel(app.CurrentDisplayMode) + ". Use Restore Previous if the display does not look right."
	}
}

func applyCustomDisplayMode() {
	if app.DisplayWidthEdit == 0 || app.DisplayHeightEdit == 0 {
		app.Status = "Поля пользовательского разрешения не созданы."
		if app.Language == "en" {
			app.Status = "Custom resolution fields are unavailable."
		}
		return
	}
	readEdit := func(hwnd uintptr) (int, error) {
		buf := make([]uint16, 32)
		pGetWindowText.Call(hwnd, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&buf[0])), uintptr(len(buf)))
		return strconv.Atoi(strings.TrimSpace(syscall.UTF16ToString(buf)))
	}
	width, errW := readEdit(app.DisplayWidthEdit)
	height, errH := readEdit(app.DisplayHeightEdit)
	if errW != nil || errH != nil {
		app.Status = "Введи ширину и высоту целыми числами, например 1920 и 1080."
		if app.Language == "en" {
			app.Status = "Enter the width and height as whole numbers, for example 1920 and 1080."
		}
		return
	}
	if width < 800 || width > 7680 || height < 600 || height > 4320 {
		app.Status = "Разрешение вне допустимого диапазона 800×600 — 7680×4320."
		if app.Language == "en" {
			app.Status = "Resolution is outside the allowed range of 800×600 to 7680×4320."
		}
		return
	}
	mode := app.CurrentDisplayMode
	mode.Width, mode.Height = width, height
	applyDisplayMode(mode)
}

func addDisplayEdits() {
	createEdit := func(hwnd *uintptr, x int, cue string) {
		if *hwnd != 0 {
			return
		}
		class, _ := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString("EDIT")
		style := wsChild | wsVisible | wsBorder | esAutoHScroll
		h, _, _ := pCreateWindowEx.Call(wsExClientEdge, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(class)), 0, uintptr(style), uintptr(x), 555, 145, 36, app.HWND, 0, app.Instance, 0)
		runtime.KeepAlive(class)
		*hwnd = h
		if h != 0 {
			app.Controls = append(app.Controls, h)
			placeholder, _ := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString(cue)
			pSendMessage.Call(h, 0x1501, 1, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(placeholder)))
			runtime.KeepAlive(placeholder)
		}
	}
	createEdit(&app.DisplayWidthEdit, 520, "Ширина")
	createEdit(&app.DisplayHeightEdit, 680, "Высота")
}

func paintDisplay(hdc uintptr, c rect) {
	topTitle, topBody := "АВТОМАТИЧЕСКОЕ ОБНАРУЖЕНИЕ МОНИТОРА", "VOID читает активное разрешение, частоту обновления и список режимов, которые сообщает драйвер дисплея."
	section, custom, safe := "ТЕКУЩИЙ РЕЖИМ И СПИСОК", "СВОЁ РАЗРЕШЕНИЕ", "БЕЗОПАСНОСТЬ"
	currentLabel, countLabel := "Сейчас: ", "Доступных режимов: %d"
	chooseHint := "Выбирай только режимы, которые реально поддерживает монитор. Windows сначала проверяет совместимость и только затем меняет картинку."
	widthLabel, heightLabel := "Ширина (px)", "Высота (px)"
	customHint := "Например: 1920 × 1080. Пользовательский режим будет применён только если его принимает драйвер."
	safeHint := "Не поддерживаемые монитором разрешения не применяются. Настройка меняет текущий режим Windows, а не разгоняет панель и не меняет её физические характеристики."
	if app.Language == "en" {
		topTitle, topBody = "AUTOMATIC DISPLAY DETECTION", "VOID reads the active resolution, refresh rate and display modes exposed by the monitor driver."
		section, custom, safe = "CURRENT MODE AND AVAILABLE MODES", "CUSTOM RESOLUTION", "SAFETY"
		currentLabel, countLabel = "Current: ", "Available modes: %d"
		chooseHint = "Choose only a mode supported by the display. Windows checks compatibility before changing the screen."
		widthLabel, heightLabel = "Width (px)", "Height (px)"
		customHint = "Example: 1920 × 1080. A custom mode is applied only when the display driver accepts it."
		safeHint = "Unsupported resolutions are rejected. This changes the active Windows mode; it does not overclock the panel or alter its physical capabilities."
	}
	card(hdc, rect{260, 125, c.Right - 26, 225}, topTitle, topBody)
	current := displayModeLabel(app.CurrentDisplayMode)
	selected := "Нет поддерживаемых режимов"
	if app.Language == "en" {
		selected = "No supported modes found"
	}
	if len(app.MonitorModes) > 0 && app.MonitorIndex >= 0 && app.MonitorIndex < len(app.MonitorModes) {
		selected = displayModeLabel(app.MonitorModes[app.MonitorIndex])
	}
	card(hdc, rect{260, 245, c.Right - 26, 455}, section, "")
	drawText(hdc, currentLabel+current, rect{285, 295, c.Right - 50, 328}, 13, true, dtLeft|dtSingleLine|dtNoPrefix, uint32(colGreen))
	drawText(hdc, fmt.Sprintf(countLabel, len(app.MonitorModes)), rect{285, 335, 500, 360}, 9, false, dtLeft|dtSingleLine|dtNoPrefix, uint32(colMuted))
	drawText(hdc, selected, rect{590, 292, 805, 340}, 13, true, dtCenter|dtVCenter|dtWordBreak|dtNoPrefix, uint32(colText))
	drawText(hdc, chooseHint, rect{285, 365, c.Right - 55, 420}, 9, false, dtLeft|dtWordBreak|dtNoPrefix, uint32(colMuted))
	card(hdc, rect{260, 480, c.Right - 26, 680}, custom, "")
	drawText(hdc, widthLabel, rect{520, 525, 665, 550}, 9, true, dtLeft|dtSingleLine|dtNoPrefix, uint32(colText))
	drawText(hdc, heightLabel, rect{680, 525, 825, 550}, 9, true, dtLeft|dtSingleLine|dtNoPrefix, uint32(colText))
	drawText(hdc, customHint, rect{285, 648, c.Right - 50, 675}, 7, false, dtLeft|dtWordBreak|dtNoPrefix, uint32(colMuted))
	card(hdc, rect{260, 705, c.Right - 26, 800}, safe, safeHint)
	status := app.Status
	if status == "" {
		status = "Монитор определён автоматически."
		if app.Language == "en" {
			status = "Display detected automatically."
		}
	}
	drawText(hdc, status, rect{285, 815, c.Right - 50, 850}, 8, true, dtLeft|dtWordBreak|dtNoPrefix, uint32(colPurple))
}

FILE: hardware_profile.go

package main

import "strings"

type hardwareInfo struct {
	CPU         string  `json:"CPU"`
	GPU         string  `json:"GPU"`
	RAMGB       float64 `json:"RAMGB"`
	RAMType     string  `json:"RAMType"`
	Disks       string  `json:"Disks"`
	OS          string  `json:"OS"`
	LogicalCPUs int     `json:"LogicalCPUs"`
	GPUMemoryGB float64 `json:"GPUMemoryGB"`
}

// rustHardwareRecommendation is a conservative local recommendation. It only
// chooses client-side graphics settings; it never overclocks hardware or changes
// driver/security settings.
type rustHardwareRecommendation struct {
	Quality      int
	DrawDistance int
	Tier         string
	Reason       string
}

func recommendRustHardware(h hardwareInfo) rustHardwareRecommendation {
	gpu := strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(h.GPU))
	ram, cores, vram := h.RAMGB, h.LogicalCPUs, h.GPUMemoryGB
	hasAny := func(parts ...string) bool {
		for _, part := range parts {
			if strings.Contains(gpu, part) {
				return true
			}
		}
		return false
	}

	// WMI returns every adapter in one string on hybrid laptops. Previously,
	// the mere presence of Intel UHD/Iris caused an RTX laptop to be marked LOW.
	// Count an integrated adapter only when no discrete adapter is also listed.
	discreteGPU := hasAny(
		"nvidia", "geforce", "rtx ", "gtx ", "radeon rx", "amd radeon rx",
		"intel arc", "arc a", "arc b",
	)
	integratedGPU := hasAny(
		"intel uhd", "intel hd", "iris xe", "radeon graphics", "vega 6",
		"vega 7", "vega 8", "radeon 610m", "radeon 660m", "radeon 680m",
		"radeon 740m", "radeon 760m", "radeon 780m", "basic display",
	) && !discreteGPU

	modernDiscrete := hasAny(
		"rtx 20", "rtx 30", "rtx 40", "rtx 50", "gtx 16",
		"rx 5", "rx 6", "rx 7", "rx 9", "intel arc", "arc a", "arc b",
	)
	highEnd := hasAny(
		"rtx 3060 ti", "rtx 3070", "rtx 3080", "rtx 3090",
		"rtx 4060 ti", "rtx 4070", "rtx 4080", "rtx 4090",
		"rtx 5060 ti", "rtx 5070", "rtx 5080", "rtx 5090",
		"rx 6700", "rx 6750", "rx 6800", "rx 6900", "rx 7700",
		"rx 7800", "rx 7900", "rx 9070", "arc b580",
	)

	// Unknown or incomplete hardware stays conservative. Do not infer that an
	// absent WMI VRAM value means zero memory.
	if (ram > 0 && ram < 12) || integratedGPU || (cores > 0 && cores <= 4) || (vram > 0 && vram < 3.5) {
		return rustHardwareRecommendation{
			Quality: 0, DrawDistance: 900, Tier: "LOW",
			Reason: "Небольшой объём RAM, мало потоков CPU или слабая/встроенная GPU — приоритет FPS и стабильности.",
		}
	}
	// AdapterRAM is often capped by WMI at 4 GB. For recognized upper-mid/high
	// GPUs, the model name is more reliable than that reported number.
	if ram >= 24 && cores >= 8 && (vram >= 8 || highEnd) {
		return rustHardwareRecommendation{
			Quality: 3, DrawDistance: 2200, Tier: "HIGH",
			Reason: "Достаточно RAM и потоков CPU, обнаружена производительная дискретная GPU — выбран расширенный профиль без максимальных тяжёлых теней.",
		}
	}
	if ram >= 12 && cores >= 6 && (modernDiscrete || vram >= 4) {
		return rustHardwareRecommendation{
			Quality: 1, DrawDistance: 1500, Tier: "BALANCED",
			Reason: "Дискретная GPU и достаточные ресурсы CPU/RAM — снижены дорогие эффекты при сохранении нормальной дальности.",
		}
	}
	return rustHardwareRecommendation{
		Quality: 0, DrawDistance: 1200, Tier: "SAFE",
		Reason: "Не хватает достоверных данных для тяжёлого профиля — применяются осторожные настройки.",
	}
}

func validRustCustomKey(key string) bool {
	key = strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(key))
	if key == "" || len(key) > 12 {
		return false
	}
	allowed := map[string]bool{
		"q": true, "w": true, "e": true, "r": true, "t": true, "y": true,
		"u": true, "i": true, "o": true, "p": true, "a": true, "s": true,
		"0": true, "1": true, "2": true, "3": true, "4": true, "5": true, "6": true, "7": true, "8": true, "9": true, "space": true,
		"d": true, "f": true, "g": true, "h": true, "j": true, "k": true,
		"l": true, "z": true, "x": true, "c": true, "v": true, "b": true,
		"n": true, "m": true, "mouse1": true, "mouse2": true, "mouse3": true,
		"mouse4": true, "mouse5": true,
		"f1": true, "f2": true, "f3": true, "f4": true, "f5": true, "f6": true,
		"f7": true, "f8": true, "f9": true, "f10": true, "f11": true, "f12": true,
		"leftalt": true, "rightalt": true, "leftshift": true, "rightshift": true,
		"leftctrl": true, "rightctrl": true, "capslock": true, "tab": true,
		"home": true, "end": true, "insert": true, "delete": true,
	}
	return allowed[key]
}

package main

import "testing"

func TestRecommendRustHardwareConservativeAndTiers(t *testing.T) {
	low := recommendRustHardware(hardwareInfo{CPU: "Old CPU", GPU: "Intel UHD Graphics", RAMGB: 8, LogicalCPUs: 4})
	if low.Tier != "LOW" || low.Quality != 0 || low.DrawDistance >= 1200 {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected low recommendation: %+v", low)
	}
	balanced := recommendRustHardware(hardwareInfo{GPU: "NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3060", RAMGB: 16, LogicalCPUs: 8})
	if balanced.Tier != "BALANCED" || balanced.DrawDistance < 1400 {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected balanced recommendation: %+v", balanced)
	}
	high := recommendRustHardware(hardwareInfo{GPU: "NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4090", RAMGB: 64, LogicalCPUs: 24, GPUMemoryGB: 24})
	if high.Tier != "HIGH" || high.Quality < 2 || high.DrawDistance < 2000 {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected high recommendation: %+v", high)
	}
	unknown := recommendRustHardware(hardwareInfo{})
	if unknown.Tier != "SAFE" || unknown.Quality != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("unknown hardware should get conservative defaults: %+v", unknown)
	}
}

func TestValidRustCustomKey(t *testing.T) {
	for _, key := range []string{"q", "Mouse4", "F12", "LeftAlt", "tab"} {
		if !validRustCustomKey(key) {
			t.Fatalf("key should be accepted: %s", key)
		}
	}
	for _, key := range []string{"", "bad key", "bind q forward", "../x", ";quit"} {
		if validRustCustomKey(key) {
			t.Fatalf("key should be rejected: %q", key)
		}
	}
}

func TestRecommendRustHardwareRecognizesCommon12GBRadeonWithoutWMIEnoughVRAM(t *testing.T) {
	got := recommendRustHardware(hardwareInfo{
		CPU: "Intel Xeon E5-2690 v4", GPU: "AMD Radeon RX 6700 XT",
		RAMGB: 32, LogicalCPUs: 28, GPUMemoryGB: 4,
	})
	if got.Tier != "HIGH" || got.DrawDistance < 2000 {
		t.Fatalf("RX 6700 XT system should not be misclassified due to WMI VRAM cap: %+v", got)
	}
}

func TestRecommendRustHardwareDoesNotMisclassifyHybridLaptop(t *testing.T) {
	got := recommendRustHardware(hardwareInfo{
		CPU: "Intel Core i7", GPU: "Intel UHD Graphics / NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3060 Laptop GPU",
		RAMGB: 16, LogicalCPUs: 12, GPUMemoryGB: 4,
	})
	if got.Tier != "BALANCED" {
		t.Fatalf("hybrid laptop with a discrete RTX GPU should not be treated as integrated-only: %+v", got)
	}
}

func TestRecommendRustHardwareRecognizesStrongGPUDespiteWMI4GBCap(t *testing.T) {
	got := recommendRustHardware(hardwareInfo{
		CPU: "AMD Ryzen 7", GPU: "NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4070 Laptop GPU",
		RAMGB: 32, LogicalCPUs: 16, GPUMemoryGB: 4,
	})
	if got.Tier != "HIGH" {
		t.Fatalf("strong discrete GPU should use HIGH despite WMI VRAM cap: %+v", got)
	}
}

//go:build windows

package main

import (
	"crypto/sha256"
	_ "embed"
	"encoding/binary"
	"encoding/hex"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"math"
	"net/http"
	"net/url"
	"os"

type gdiSize struct{ Cx, Cy int32 }
type rect struct{ Left, Top, Right, Bottom int32 }
type fileTime struct{ Low, High uint32 }
type systemPowerStatus struct {
	ACLineStatus, BatteryFlag, BatteryLifePercent, SystemStatusFlag byte
	BatteryLifeTime, BatteryFullLifeTime                            uint32
}
type notifyIconData struct {
	CbSize           uint32
	HWnd             uintptr
	UID              uint32
	UFlags           uint32
	CallbackMessage  uint32
	HIcon            uintptr

	State            uint32
	StateMask        uint32
	Info             [256]uint16
	TimeoutOrVersion uint32
	InfoTitle        [64]uint16
	InfoFlags        uint32
	GuidItem         [16]byte
	BalloonIcon      uintptr
}
type msg struct {
	Hwnd     uintptr
	Message  uint32
	_        uint32
	WParam   uintptr
	LParam   uintptr
	Time     uint32
	Pt       point
	LPrivate uint32
}
type paintStruct struct {
	HDC         uintptr
	Erase       int32
	Paint       rect
	Restore     int32
	IncUpdate   int32
	RGBReserved [32]byte
}

	Size       uint32
	Style      uint32

	WndExtra   int32
	Instance   uintptr

	MenuName   *uint16

}

	ItemID     uint32
	ItemAction uint32
	ItemState  uint32
	_          uint32
	HwndItem   uintptr
	HDC        uintptr

}

// Embedded artwork is extracted to the user's LocalAppData on launch, so the EXE stays self-contained.
//
//go:embed assets/void-banner.bmp
var embeddedBanner []byte

//go:embed assets/void-banner-blue.bmp
var embeddedBannerBlue []byte

//go:embed assets/void-icon.ico
var embeddedIcon []byte

//go:embed assets/void-cursor-purple.cur
var embeddedCursorPurple []byte

//go:embed assets/void-cursor-blue.cur
var embeddedCursorBlue []byte

//go:embed assets/void-background.png
var embeddedBackground []byte

var app = &appState{Page: "overview", Status: "Готов к работе.", CPUPercent: "—", RAMPercent: "—", Quality: 0, DrawDistance: 1500, FOV: 90, Contrast: 100, Gamma: 100, RedGain: 100, GreenGain: 100, BlueGain: 100, Language: "ru", Theme: "purple", TweaksBackup: map[string]regSnapshot{}, Buttons: map[int]buttonInfo{}, CustomLootKey: "mouse3", ViewmodelKey: "f3", BindKeys: defaultRustBindKeys()}

func main() {
	runtime.LockOSThread()
	pSetProcessDPIAware.Call()
	inst, _, _ := pGetModuleHandle.Call(0)
	app.Instance = inst
	prepareAssets()
	loadPrefs()
	app.RadarEventCh = make(chan VoidRadarEvent, 64)
	app.RustRadarPairing = loadRustPlusPairing()
	if strings.TrimSpace(app.Login) == "" {
		app.Page = "login"
	}
	applyTheme()
	loadTweaksBackup()
	if app.TweaksBackupError != "" {
		app.Status = app.TweaksBackupError
	}
	className, _ := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString("VOIDOptimizerWindow")
	proc := syscall.NewCallback(windowProc)
	wc := wndClassEx{Size: uint32(unsafe.Sizeof(wndClassEx{})), Style: 0x0002 | 0x0001, WndProc: proc, Instance: inst, Icon: app.AppIcon, IconSm: app.AppIcon, Cursor: app.ActiveCursor, ClassName: className}
	regResult, _, _ := pRegisterClassEx.Call(uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&wc)))
	runtime.KeepAlive(&wc)
	runtime.KeepAlive(className)
	if regResult == 0 {
		return
	}
	title, _ := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString("VOID OPTIMIZER")
	// Frameless themed window: the app draws its own title controls and supports
	// native resize hit-tests plus a custom maximize/restore button.
	windowStyle := wsPopup | wsClipChildren
	winX, winY, winW, winH := initialWindowBounds()
	hwnd, _, _ := pCreateWindowEx.Call(0, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(className)), uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(title)), uintptr(windowStyle), uintptr(winX), uintptr(winY), uintptr(winW), uintptr(winH), 0, 0, inst, 0)
	runtime.KeepAlive(className)
	runtime.KeepAlive(title)
	if hwnd == 0 {
		return
	}
	app.HWND = hwnd
	// The window is fully custom-drawn. Ask DWM not to paint its remaining
	// system border, which otherwise appears as a thin white strip on Win11.
	setDWMColorAttribute(hwnd, 34, 0xFFFFFFFE) // DWMWA_BORDER_COLOR = NONE
	setDWMColorAttribute(hwnd, 35, uint32(colBg))
	if app.AppIcon != 0 {
		pSendMessage.Call(hwnd, wmSetIcon, 1, app.AppIcon)
		pSendMessage.Call(hwnd, wmSetIcon, 0, app.AppIcon)
	}
	captureGamma()
	app.RustFolder = findRust()
	app.RustRunning = rustRunning()
	refreshDisplayModes()
	app.Autostart = autostartEnabled()
	app.HardwareCh = make(chan hardwareInfo, 1)
	go func(ch chan hardwareInfo) { ch <- detectHardware() }(app.HardwareCh)
	startUpdateCheck()
	showButtons()
	pSetTimer.Call(hwnd, 1, 5000, 0)
	pShowWindow.Call(hwnd, swShow)
	pUpdateWindow.Call(hwnd)
	var m msg
	for {
		r, _, _ := pGetMessage.Call(uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&m)), 0, 0, 0)
		if int32(r) <= 0 {
			break
		}
		pTranslateMessage.Call(uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&m)))
		pDispatchMessage.Call(uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&m)))
	}
}

func windowProc(hwnd uintptr, message uint32, wParam, lParam uintptr) uintptr {
	switch message {
	case wmCreate:
		return 0
	case wmNCCalcSize, wmNCPaint:
		// Fully consume non-client drawing/calculation: Windows must not add
		// a caption, resize frame, shadow stripe or themed white border.
		return 0
	case wmNCActivate:
		return 1
	case wmSetCursor:
		if uint32(lParam&0xFFFF) == htClient {
			cursor := app.ActiveCursor
			if cursor == 0 {
				cursor, _, _ = pLoadCursor.Call(0, 32512) // IDC_ARROW fallback
				app.ActiveCursor = cursor
			}
			if cursor != 0 {
				pSetCursor.Call(cursor)
				return 1
			}
		}
	case wmNCHitTest:
		sx := int(int16(uint16(lParam)))
		sy := int(int16(uint16(lParam >> 16)))
		pt := point{X: int32(sx), Y: int32(sy)}
		pScreenToClient.Call(hwnd, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&pt)))
		var cr rect
		pGetClientRect.Call(hwnd, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&cr)))
		maximized, _, _ := pIsZoomed.Call(hwnd)
		if app.Fullscreen {
			return htClient
		}
		// Keep the outer 8 physical pixels client-visible for the custom resize handler.
		if maximized == 0 && (pt.X < 8 || pt.X >= cr.Right-8 || pt.Y < 8 || pt.Y >= cr.Bottom-8) {
			return htClient
		}
		logicalX, logicalY := deviceToCanvas(int(pt.X), int(pt.Y))
		if logicalY >= 0 && logicalY < 38 && logicalX < 1320-390 {
			return htCaption
		}
		return htClient
	case wmKeyDown, wmSysKeyDown:
		if app.CapturingBind != "" {
			if wParam == 0x1B { // ESC cancels key capture.
				app.CapturingBind = ""
				app.Status = "Назначение клавиши отменено."
				showButtons()
				return 0
			}
			key := rustKeyFromVirtualKey(uint32(wParam))
			if key == "" || !validRustCustomKey(key) {
				app.Status = "Эту клавишу нельзя использовать. Нажми другую клавишу или Esc для отмены."
				invalidate()
				return 0
			}
			assignRustBindKey(app.CapturingBind, key)
			return 0
		}
		if wParam == 0x7A { // F11 toggles true monitor-sized fullscreen.
			toggleFullscreen()
			return 0
		}
	case wmCtlColorEdit:
		if (app.LoginEdit != 0 && lParam == app.LoginEdit) || (app.UpdateURLEdit != 0 && lParam == app.UpdateURLEdit) || (app.BindKeyEdit != 0 && lParam == app.BindKeyEdit) || (app.ViewmodelBindKeyEdit != 0 && lParam == app.ViewmodelBindKeyEdit) || (app.DisplayWidthEdit != 0 && lParam == app.DisplayWidthEdit) || (app.DisplayHeightEdit != 0 && lParam == app.DisplayHeightEdit) || (app.RustAPIKeyEdit != 0 && lParam == app.RustAPIKeyEdit) || (app.RustIDsEdit != 0 && lParam == app.RustIDsEdit) || (app.RustRadarServerEdit != 0 && lParam == app.RustRadarServerEdit) || (app.RustRadarPortEdit != 0 && lParam == app.RustRadarPortEdit) || (app.RustRadarPlayerIDEdit != 0 && lParam == app.RustRadarPlayerIDEdit) || (app.RustRadarTokenEdit != 0 && lParam == app.RustRadarTokenEdit) {
			pSetTextColor.Call(wParam, uintptr(uint32(colText)))
			pSetBkColor.Call(wParam, uintptr(uint32(colCard)))
			if app.LoginBrush == 0 {
				app.LoginBrush, _, _ = pCreateSolidBrush.Call(uintptr(uint32(colCard)))
			}
			return app.LoginBrush
		}
		return 0
	case wmEraseBkgnd:
		return 1
	case wmPaint:
		paint(hwnd)
		return 0
	case wmAppQuit:
		pDestroyWindow.Call(hwnd)
		return 0
	case wmCommand:
		// The login edit is the only native child control. All buttons are
		// painted on the parent surface to avoid themed white corners/flicker.
		return 0
	case wmTimer:
		oldCPU, oldRAM := app.CPUPercent, app.RAMPercent
		refreshMetrics()
		for app.RadarEventCh != nil {
			select {
			case event := <-app.RadarEventCh:
				app.RadarEvents = append([]VoidRadarEvent{event}, app.RadarEvents...)
				if len(app.RadarEvents) > 16 {
					app.RadarEvents = app.RadarEvents[:16]
				}
				app.Status = "VOID Radar: " + event.Title
				if event.Type == "connected" {
					app.RustRadarConnected = true
				}
				if event.Type == "disconnected" || event.Type == "connection_error" {
					app.RustRadarConnected = false
				}
				if event.Type == "teammate_death" || event.Type == "world_event" {
					showVoidRadarNotification(event)
				}
				if app.Page == "void_radar" || app.Page == "rust_plus" {
					invalidate()
				}
			default:
				goto radarQueueDrained
			}
		}
	radarQueueDrained:
		if app.ScanCh != nil {
			select {
			case result := <-app.ScanCh:
				app.Preview = result.Files
				app.Scanned = true
				app.ScanCh = nil
				app.Busy = false
				app.Status = result.Status
				invalidate()
			default:
			}
		}
		if app.CleanCh != nil {
			select {
			case result := <-app.CleanCh:
				app.CleanCh = nil
				app.Busy = false
				app.Preview = nil
				app.Scanned = false
				app.Status = result.Status
				invalidate()
			default:
			}
		}
		if app.HardwareCh != nil {
			select {
			case info := <-app.HardwareCh:
				app.Hardware = info
				app.HardwareReady = info.CPU != "" || info.GPU != "" || info.RAMGB > 0
				app.HardwareCh = nil
				if app.Page == "overview" || app.Page == "settings" {
					if app.HardwareReady {
						app.Status = "Компоненты ПК обнаружены автоматически."
					} else {
						app.Status = "Не удалось получить сведения о комплектующих через Windows."
					}
					invalidate()
				}
			default:
			}
		}
		if app.RustMonitorCh != nil {
			select {
			case result := <-app.RustMonitorCh:
				app.RustMonitorCh = nil
				app.RustMonitorBusy = false
				if result.Err != nil {
					app.RustMonitorError = result.Err.Error()
					app.Status = "Мониторинг Steam: " + result.Err.Error()
				} else {
					app.RustMonitorError = ""
					if len(app.RustMonitorPlayers) > 0 {
						for _, event := range rustPlayerTransitions(app.RustMonitorPlayers, result.Players) {
							app.RustMonitorEvents = append([]string{time.Now().Format("15:04:05") + " · " + event}, app.RustMonitorEvents...)
						}
						if len(app.RustMonitorEvents) > 8 {
							app.RustMonitorEvents = app.RustMonitorEvents[:8]
						}
					}
					app.RustMonitorPlayers = result.Players
					app.RustMonitorLastUpdate = time.Now()
					app.Status = fmt.Sprintf("Мониторинг обновлён: %d профилей. Следующее обновление примерно через 2 минуты.", len(result.Players))
				}
				if app.Page == "rust_plus" {
					invalidate()
				}
			default:
			}
		}
		if app.Page == "rust_plus" && !app.RustMonitorBusy && validSteamAPIKey(app.RustSteamAPIKey) && len(app.RustTrackedIDs) > 0 && !app.RustMonitorNextCheck.IsZero() && time.Now().After(app.RustMonitorNextCheck) {
			startRustMonitor()
		}
		if app.UpdateCh == nil && !app.UpdateAvailable && !app.NextUpdateCheck.IsZero() && time.Now().After(app.NextUpdateCheck) {
			startUpdateCheck()
		}
		if app.UpdateCh != nil {
			select {
			case result := <-app.UpdateCh:
				app.UpdateCh = nil
				app.UpdateStatus = result.Status
				if result.Available {
					app.UpdateAvailable = true
					app.UpdateVersion = result.Manifest.Version
					app.UpdateURL = result.Manifest.DownloadURL
					app.UpdateSHA256 = result.Manifest.SHA256
					app.UpdateNotes = result.Manifest.Notes
					// Automatic mode: when a valid publisher feed reports a newer release,
					// download, verify and stage it without asking the user to click Update.
					startUpdateInstall()
				}
				if result.InstallReady {
					pPostMessage.Call(app.HWND, wmAppQuit, 0, 0)
				} else if app.UpdateInstalling {
					app.UpdateInstalling = false
				}
				invalidate()
			default:
			}
		}
		wasRunning := app.RustRunning
		if app.Page == "rust" {
			app.RustRunning = rustRunning()
		}
		if wasRunning != app.RustRunning {
			if app.RustRunning {
				app.Status = "Rust обнаружен: игра запущена. VOID работает только в режиме наблюдения."
			} else {
				app.Status = "Rust закрыт. Можно безопасно применить или откатить CFG."
			}
			if app.Page == "rust" {
				invalidate()
			}
		}
		if oldCPU != app.CPUPercent || oldRAM != app.RAMPercent {
			invalidateFooter()
		}
		return 0
	case wmMouseWheel:
		delta := int(int16(uint16(wParam >> 16)))
		step := -72 // positive wheel delta moves content up
		if delta < 0 {
			step = 72
		}
		switch app.Page {
		case "tweaks":
			app.TweakScroll += step
			app.TweakScroll = clampInt(app.TweakScroll, 0, tweakScrollMax())
			showButtons()
			invalidate()
		case "rust_binds":
			app.BindScroll += step
			app.BindScroll = clampInt(app.BindScroll, 0, bindScrollMax())
			showButtons()
			invalidate()
		}
		return 0
	case wmMButtonDown:
		if app.CapturingBind != "" {
			assignRustBindKey(app.CapturingBind, "mouse3")
			return 0
		}
	case wmXButtonDown:
		if app.CapturingBind != "" {
			button := uint16(wParam >> 16)
			if button == 1 {
				assignRustBindKey(app.CapturingBind, "mouse4")
			} else if button == 2 {
				assignRustBindKey(app.CapturingBind, "mouse5")
			}
			return 1
		}
	case wmLButtonDown:
		x, y := int(int16(uint16(lParam))), int(int16(uint16(lParam>>16)))
		if beginManualResize(x, y) {
			pSetCapture.Call(hwnd)
			return 0
		}
		logicalX, logicalY := deviceToCanvas(x, y)
		if id := buttonAt(logicalX, logicalY); id != 0 {
			app.PressedButton = id
			app.HotButton = id
			pSetCapture.Call(hwnd)
			invalidate()
			return 0
		}
		logicalX, logicalY = deviceToCanvas(x, y)
		handleMouse(logicalX, logicalY, true)
		if app.Dragging != "" {
			pSetCapture.Call(hwnd)
		}
		return 0
	case wmMouseMove:
		if app.ResizeMode != "" {
			updateManualResize(int(int16(uint16(lParam))), int(int16(uint16(lParam>>16))))
			return 0
		}
		if app.Dragging != "" {
			lx, ly := deviceToCanvas(int(int16(uint16(lParam))), int(int16(uint16(lParam>>16))))
			handleMouse(lx, ly, true)
			return 0
		}
		x, y := int(int16(uint16(lParam))), int(int16(uint16(lParam>>16)))
		lx, ly := deviceToCanvas(x, y)
		newHot := buttonAt(lx, ly)
		if newHot != app.HotButton {
			old := app.HotButton
			app.HotButton = newHot
			invalidateButton(old)
			invalidateButton(newHot)
		}
		if !app.TrackingMouse {
			track := trackMouseEvent{Size: uint32(unsafe.Sizeof(trackMouseEvent{})), Flags: 0x00000002, HWND: hwnd, HoverTime: 0xFFFFFFFF}
			pTrackMouseEvent.Call(uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&track)))
			app.TrackingMouse = true
			runtime.KeepAlive(&track)
		}
		return 0
	case wmMouseLeave:
		app.TrackingMouse = false
		if app.PressedButton == 0 && app.HotButton != 0 {
			old := app.HotButton
			app.HotButton = 0
			invalidateButton(old)
		}
		return 0
	case wmLButtonUp:
		x, y := int(int16(uint16(lParam))), int(int16(uint16(lParam>>16)))
		if app.ResizeMode != "" {
			app.ResizeMode = ""
			pReleaseCapture.Call()
			return 0
		}
		if app.PressedButton != 0 {
			pressed := app.PressedButton
			app.PressedButton = 0
			lx, ly := deviceToCanvas(x, y)
			app.HotButton = buttonAt(lx, ly)
			pReleaseCapture.Call()
			invalidate()
			if app.HotButton == pressed {
				handleCommand(pressed)
			}
			return 0
		}
		if app.Dragging != "" {
			lx, ly := deviceToCanvas(x, y)
			handleMouse(lx, ly, true)
			app.Dragging = ""
		}
		pReleaseCapture.Call()
		return 0
	case wmCancelMode, wmCaptureChanged:
		app.Dragging = ""
		app.ResizeMode = ""
		app.PressedButton = 0
		return 0
	case wmSize:
		positionWindowChrome()
		refreshButtons()
		invalidate()
		return 0
	case wmDestroy:
		pKillTimer.Call(hwnd, 1)
		if app.RustRadarClient != nil {
			app.RustRadarClient.Stop()
			app.RustRadarClient = nil
		}
		if app.RadarTrayAdded {
			removeVoidRadarTrayIcon()
		}
		restoreGamma()
		releaseCardFillSurface()
		if app.BackgroundDC != 0 {
			pDeleteDC.Call(app.BackgroundDC)
			app.BackgroundDC = 0
		}
		if app.BackgroundBitmap != 0 {
			pDeleteObject.Call(app.BackgroundBitmap)
			app.BackgroundBitmap = 0
		}
		if app.BannerBitmap != 0 {
			pDeleteObject.Call(app.BannerBitmap)
			app.BannerBitmap = 0
		}
		if app.BannerBitmapBlue != 0 {
			pDeleteObject.Call(app.BannerBitmapBlue)
			app.BannerBitmapBlue = 0
		}
		if app.LoginBrush != 0 {
			pDeleteObject.Call(app.LoginBrush)
			app.LoginBrush = 0
		}
		if app.AppIcon != 0 {
			pDestroyIcon.Call(app.AppIcon)
			app.AppIcon = 0
		}
		if app.CursorPurple != 0 {
			pDestroyCursor.Call(app.CursorPurple)
			app.CursorPurple = 0
		}
		if app.CursorBlue != 0 {
			pDestroyCursor.Call(app.CursorBlue)
			app.CursorBlue = 0
		}
		pPostQuitMessage.Call(0)
		return 0
	}
	r, _, _ := pDefWindowProc.Call(hwnd, uintptr(message), wParam, lParam)
	return r
}

type trackMouseEvent struct {
	Size      uint32
	Flags     uint32
	HWND      uintptr
	HoverTime uint32
}

func initialWindowBounds() (x, y, w, h int) {
	screenW, _, _ := pGetSystemMetrics.Call(0) // SM_CXSCREEN
	screenH, _, _ := pGetSystemMetrics.Call(1) // SM_CYSCREEN
	w, h = 1480, 940
	if screenW > 0 {
		preferred := int(float64(screenW) * 0.90)
		if preferred > w {
			w = preferred
		}
		if w > int(screenW)-40 {
			w = int(screenW) - 40
		}
	}
	if screenH > 0 {
		preferred := int(float64(screenH) * 0.90)
		if preferred > h {
			h = preferred
		}
		if h > int(screenH)-100 {
			h = int(screenH) - 100
		}
	}
	if w < 960 {
		w = maxInt(800, int(screenW)-20)
	}
	if h < 620 {
		h = maxInt(560, int(screenH)-60)
	}
	x = maxInt(0, (int(screenW)-w)/2)
	y = maxInt(0, (int(screenH)-h)/2)
	return
}

func setDWMColorAttribute(hwnd uintptr, attribute uint32, color uint32) {
	if hwnd == 0 {
		return
	}
	value := color
	pDwmSetWindowAttribute.Call(hwnd, uintptr(attribute), uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&value)), unsafe.Sizeof(value))
	runtime.KeepAlive(&value)
}

func beginManualResize(x, y int) bool {
	maximized, _, _ := pIsZoomed.Call(app.HWND)
	if app.Fullscreen || maximized != 0 {
		return false
	}
	w, h := clientSize()
	const edge = 8
	mode := ""
	left, right, top, bottom := x < edge, x >= w-edge, y < edge, y >= h-edge
	switch {
	case top && left:
		mode = "top-left"
	case top && right:
		mode = "top-right"
	case bottom && left:
		mode = "bottom-left"
	case bottom && right:
		mode = "bottom-right"
	case left:
		mode = "left"
	case right:
		mode = "right"
	case top:
		mode = "top"
	case bottom:
		mode = "bottom"
	default:
		return false
	}
	var wr rect
	pGetWindowRect.Call(app.HWND, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&wr)))
	pt := point{X: int32(x), Y: int32(y)}
	pClientToScreen.Call(app.HWND, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&pt)))
	app.ResizeMode = mode
	app.ResizeStartX, app.ResizeStartY = int(pt.X), int(pt.Y)
	app.ResizeStartRect = wr
	return true
}

func updateManualResize(x, y int) {
	if app.ResizeMode == "" {
		return
	}
	pt := point{X: int32(x), Y: int32(y)}
	pClientToScreen.Call(app.HWND, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&pt)))
	dx, dy := int(pt.X)-app.ResizeStartX, int(pt.Y)-app.ResizeStartY
	r := app.ResizeStartRect
	left, top, right, bottom := int(r.Left), int(r.Top), int(r.Right), int(r.Bottom)
	const minW, minH = 960, 620
	mode := app.ResizeMode
	if strings.Contains(mode, "left") {
		left += dx
	}
	if strings.Contains(mode, "right") {
		right += dx
	}
	if strings.Contains(mode, "top") {
		top += dy
	}
	if strings.Contains(mode, "bottom") {
		bottom += dy
	}
	if right-left < minW {
		if strings.Contains(mode, "left") {
			left = right - minW
		} else {
			right = left + minW
		}
	}
	if bottom-top < minH {
		if strings.Contains(mode, "top") {
			top = bottom - minH
		} else {
			bottom = top + minH
		}
	}
	pSetWindowPos.Call(app.HWND, 0, uintptr(left), uintptr(top), uintptr(right-left), uintptr(bottom-top), swpNoZOrder|swpNoActivate)
}

func hasButton(id int) bool {
	_, ok := app.Buttons[id]
	return ok
}

func clientSize() (int, int) {
	var c rect
	if app.HWND != 0 {
		pGetClientRect.Call(app.HWND, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&c)))
	}
	return int(c.Right - c.Left), int(c.Bottom - c.Top)
}

func canvasScaleAndOffset(w, h int) (float64, int, int) {
	if w < 1 || h < 1 {
		return 1, 0, 0
	}
	// Stretch the fixed logical canvas to the full client area. This avoids
	// letterbox gutters and keeps the navigation/sidebar sized to the maximized window.
	return 1, 0, 0
}

func setCanvasTransform(hdc uintptr, w, h int) {
	scale, ox, oy := canvasScaleAndOffset(w, h)
	if scale <= 0 {
		return
	}
	pSetMapMode.Call(hdc, 8) // MM_ANISOTROPIC
	windowExt := gdiSize{1320, 900}
	viewExt := gdiSize{int32(w), int32(h)}
	origin := point{int32(ox), int32(oy)}
	pSetWindowExtEx.Call(hdc, uintptr(windowExt.Cx), uintptr(windowExt.Cy), 0)
	pSetViewportExtEx.Call(hdc, uintptr(viewExt.Cx), uintptr(viewExt.Cy), 0)
	pSetViewportOrgEx.Call(hdc, uintptr(origin.X), uintptr(origin.Y), 0)
	runtime.KeepAlive(&windowExt)
	runtime.KeepAlive(&viewExt)
	runtime.KeepAlive(&origin)
}

func deviceToCanvas(x, y int) (int, int) {
	w, h := clientSize()
	if w < 1 || h < 1 {
		return x, y
	}
	return int(math.Round(float64(x) * 1320 / float64(w))), int(math.Round(float64(y) * 900 / float64(h)))
}

func canvasRectToDevice(x, y, w, h int) (int, int, int, int) {
	cw, ch := clientSize()
	if cw < 1 || ch < 1 {
		return x, y, w, h
	}
	left := int(math.Round(float64(x) * float64(cw) / 1320))
	top := int(math.Round(float64(y) * float64(ch) / 900))
	width := int(math.Round(float64(w) * float64(cw) / 1320))
	height := int(math.Round(float64(h) * float64(ch) / 900))
	return left, top, width, height
}

func positionWindowChrome() {
	if app.HWND == 0 {
		return
	}
	w, h := 1320, 900
	setBounds := func(id, x, y, bw, bh int) {
		if info, ok := app.Buttons[id]; ok {
			info.Bounds = rect{int32(x), int32(y), int32(x + bw), int32(y + bh)}
			app.Buttons[id] = info
		}
	}
	setBounds(idWindowMinimize, w-142, 8, 38, 28)
	setBounds(idWindowMaximize, w-98, 8, 38, 28)
	setBounds(idWindowClose, w-54, 8, 38, 28)
	setBounds(idSettings, 16, h-62, 194, 40)
	setBounds(idCheckUpdates, w-390, 8, 220, 28)
	setBounds(idUpdate, w-390, 8, 220, 28)
	setBounds(idDetect, w-520, 170, 170, 40)
	setBounds(idBrowse, w-338, 170, 230, 40)
	setBounds(idLangRU, w-485, 170, 165, 42)
	setBounds(idLangEN, w-305, 170, 165, 42)
	setBounds(idThemePurple, w-485, 315, 165, 42)
	setBounds(idThemeBlue, w-305, 315, 165, 42)
	setBounds(idAutostart, w-390, 455, 330, 42)
	setBounds(idSaveUpdateURL, w-220, 660, 180, 36)
	setBounds(idRescanHardware, w-390, 780, 330, 36)
	if app.UpdateURLEdit != 0 {
		x, y, cw, ch := canvasRectToDevice(280, 660, maxInt(360, w-530), 34)
		pSetWindowPos.Call(app.UpdateURLEdit, 0, uintptr(x), uintptr(y), uintptr(cw), uintptr(ch), swpNoZOrder|swpNoActivate)
	}
	if app.LoginEdit != 0 {
		x, y, cw, ch := canvasRectToDevice((w-390)/2, 430, 390, 42)
		pSetWindowPos.Call(app.LoginEdit, 0, uintptr(x), uintptr(y), uintptr(cw), uintptr(ch), swpNoZOrder|swpNoActivate)
	}
	if app.DisplayWidthEdit != 0 {
		x, y, cw, ch := canvasRectToDevice(520, 555, 145, 36)
		pSetWindowPos.Call(app.DisplayWidthEdit, 0, uintptr(x), uintptr(y), uintptr(cw), uintptr(ch), swpNoZOrder|swpNoActivate)
	}
	if app.DisplayHeightEdit != 0 {
		x, y, cw, ch := canvasRectToDevice(680, 555, 145, 36)
		pSetWindowPos.Call(app.DisplayHeightEdit, 0, uintptr(x), uintptr(y), uintptr(cw), uintptr(ch), swpNoZOrder|swpNoActivate)
	}
	if app.RustAPIKeyEdit != 0 {
		x, y, cw, ch := canvasRectToDevice(280, 368, 330, 34)
		pSetWindowPos.Call(app.RustAPIKeyEdit, 0, uintptr(x), uintptr(y), uintptr(cw), uintptr(ch), swpNoZOrder|swpNoActivate)
	}
	if app.RustIDsEdit != 0 {
		x, y, cw, ch := canvasRectToDevice(280, 437, 485, 50)
		pSetWindowPos.Call(app.RustIDsEdit, 0, uintptr(x), uintptr(y), uintptr(cw), uintptr(ch), swpNoZOrder|swpNoActivate)
	}
	if app.RustRadarServerEdit != 0 {
		x, y, cw, ch := canvasRectToDevice(280, 235, 450, 34)
		pSetWindowPos.Call(app.RustRadarServerEdit, 0, uintptr(x), uintptr(y), uintptr(cw), uintptr(ch), swpNoZOrder|swpNoActivate)
	}
	if app.RustRadarPortEdit != 0 {
		x, y, cw, ch := canvasRectToDevice(750, 235, 150, 34)
		pSetWindowPos.Call(app.RustRadarPortEdit, 0, uintptr(x), uintptr(y), uintptr(cw), uintptr(ch), swpNoZOrder|swpNoActivate)
	}
	if app.RustRadarPlayerIDEdit != 0 {
		x, y, cw, ch := canvasRectToDevice(280, 300, 450, 34)
		pSetWindowPos.Call(app.RustRadarPlayerIDEdit, 0, uintptr(x), uintptr(y), uintptr(cw), uintptr(ch), swpNoZOrder|swpNoActivate)
	}
	if app.RustRadarTokenEdit != 0 {
		x, y, cw, ch := canvasRectToDevice(750, 300, 360, 34)
		pSetWindowPos.Call(app.RustRadarTokenEdit, 0, uintptr(x), uintptr(y), uintptr(cw), uintptr(ch), swpNoZOrder|swpNoActivate)
	}
}

func showButtons() {
	// Only the first-run login field is a native child control. Destroy it
	// after login; all action/navigation buttons live in the custom canvas.
	if app.Page != "login" && app.LoginEdit != 0 {
		pDestroyWindow.Call(app.LoginEdit)
		app.LoginEdit = 0
	}
	if app.Page != "settings" && app.UpdateURLEdit != 0 {
		pDestroyWindow.Call(app.UpdateURLEdit)
		app.UpdateURLEdit = 0
	}
	if app.Page != "rust_binds" && app.BindKeyEdit != 0 {
		pDestroyWindow.Call(app.BindKeyEdit)
		app.BindKeyEdit = 0
	}
	if app.Page != "rust_binds" && app.ViewmodelBindKeyEdit != 0 {
		pDestroyWindow.Call(app.ViewmodelBindKeyEdit)
		app.ViewmodelBindKeyEdit = 0
	}
	if app.Page != "rust_plus" {
		if app.RustAPIKeyEdit != 0 {
			pDestroyWindow.Call(app.RustAPIKeyEdit)
			app.RustAPIKeyEdit = 0
		}
		if app.RustIDsEdit != 0 {
			pDestroyWindow.Call(app.RustIDsEdit)
			app.RustIDsEdit = 0
		}
	}
	if app.Page != "void_radar" {
		for _, h := range []uintptr{app.RustRadarServerEdit, app.RustRadarPortEdit, app.RustRadarPlayerIDEdit, app.RustRadarTokenEdit} {
			if h != 0 {
				pDestroyWindow.Call(h)
			}
		}
		app.RustRadarServerEdit, app.RustRadarPortEdit, app.RustRadarPlayerIDEdit, app.RustRadarTokenEdit = 0, 0, 0, 0
	}
	if app.Page != "display" {
		if app.DisplayWidthEdit != 0 {
			pDestroyWindow.Call(app.DisplayWidthEdit)
			app.DisplayWidthEdit = 0
		}
		if app.DisplayHeightEdit != 0 {
			pDestroyWindow.Call(app.DisplayHeightEdit)
			app.DisplayHeightEdit = 0
		}
	}
	app.Controls = nil
	app.Buttons = make(map[int]buttonInfo)
	app.HotButton = 0
	app.PressedButton = 0
	w, h := 1320, 900
	if app.Page != "login" {
		nav := []struct {
			id   int
			text string
			y    int
		}{
			{idOverview, tx("ГЛАВНАЯ"), 155}, {idClean, tx("ОЧИСТКА"), 205}, {idBoost, tx("ОПТИМИЗАЦИЯ ПК"), 255},
			{idRust, tx("ОПТИМИЗАЦИЯ RUST"), 305}, {idRustBinds, tx("БИНДЫ RUST"), 355}, {idColors, tx("ЦВЕТА МОНИТОРА"), 405},
			{idDisplay, tx("РАЗРЕШЕНИЕ ЭКРАНА"), 455}, {idTweaks, tx("ТВИКИ WINDOWS"), 505}, {idRustPlus, tx("RUST+"), 555}, {idVoidRadar, tx("VOID RADAR"), 605},
		}
		for _, n := range nav {
			primary := (n.id == idOverview && app.Page == "overview") || (n.id == idClean && app.Page == "clean") || (n.id == idBoost && app.Page == "boost") || (n.id == idRust && app.Page == "rust") || (n.id == idRustBinds && app.Page == "rust_binds") || (n.id == idColors && app.Page == "colors") || (n.id == idTweaks && app.Page == "tweaks") || (n.id == idDisplay && app.Page == "display") || (n.id == idRustPlus && app.Page == "rust_plus") || (n.id == idVoidRadar && app.Page == "void_radar")
			addButton(n.id, n.text, 16, n.y, 194, 42, primary)
		}
		addButton(idSettings, tx("НАСТРОЙКИ"), 16, h-62, 194, 40, app.Page == "settings")
	}
	// Themed window chrome and a built-in update control are always present.
	addButton(idWindowMinimize, "—", w-142, 8, 38, 28, false)
	addButton(idWindowMaximize, "□", w-98, 8, 38, 28, false)
	addButton(idWindowClose, "×", w-54, 8, 38, 28, false)
	if app.UpdateAvailable {
		label := "ОБНОВИТЬ · v" + app.UpdateVersion
		if app.Language == "en" {
			label = "UPDATE · v" + app.UpdateVersion
		}
		addButton(idUpdate, label, w-390, 8, 220, 28, true)
	} else {
		label := "ПРОВЕРИТЬ ОБНОВЛЕНИЯ"
		if app.Language == "en" {
			label = "CHECK FOR UPDATES"
		}
		addButton(idCheckUpdates, label, w-390, 8, 220, 28, false)
	}
	if app.Page == "login" {
		addLoginControls()
	} else {
		switch app.Page {
		case "overview":
			label := "АВТООПТИМИЗАЦИЯ ПК"
			if app.Language == "en" {
				label = "AUTO-OPTIMIZE PC"
			}
			addButton(idHomeOptimize, label, 985, 177, 280, 42, true)
		case "clean":
			addButton(idScan, "СКАНИРОВАТЬ", 280, 250, 160, 42, true)
			addButton(idCleanNow, "УДАЛИТЬ НАЙДЕННОЕ", 454, 250, 220, 42, false)
		case "boost":
			addButton(idApply, "БЫСТРАЯ ОПТИМИЗАЦИЯ", 280, 235, 225, 42, true)
			addButton(idRestore, "ОТКАТИТЬ НАСТРОЙКИ", 520, 235, 215, 42, false)
		case "rust":
			addButton(idDetect, "ОБНОВИТЬ ПОИСК", w-520, 170, 170, 40, true)
			addButton(idBrowse, "ВЫБРАТЬ RUSTCLIENT.EXE", w-338, 170, 230, 40, false)
			addButton(idLow, "МАКС. FPS", 280, 555, 140, 40, app.RustProfileID == 0)
			addButton(idPvp, "PVP", 432, 555, 140, 40, app.RustProfileID == 1)
			addButton(idSurvival, "ВЫЖИВАНИЕ", 584, 555, 140, 40, app.RustProfileID == 2)
			addButton(idQuality, "ДАЛЬНОСТЬ 2000", 736, 555, 160, 40, app.RustProfileID == 3)
			addButton(idRustAuto, "АВТО ПОД ЖЕЛЕЗО", 910, 555, 190, 40, app.RustProfileID == idRustAuto)
			addButton(idOpenRust, "ОТКРЫТЬ ПАПКУ ИГРЫ", 280, 780, 210, 38, false)
			addButton(idApplyRustCFG, "ПРИМЕНИТЬ CFG", 510, 780, 180, 38, true)
			addButton(idRestoreRustCFG, "ОТКАТИТЬ CFG", 706, 780, 160, 38, false)
		case "rust_binds":
			for i := range rustUtilityBindSpecs() {
				y := 205 + i*68 - app.BindScroll
				if y >= 185 && y <= 815 {
					label := "ИЗМЕНИТЬ"
					if app.CapturingBind == rustUtilityBindSpecs()[i].ID {
						label = "НАЖМИ КЛАВИШУ"
					}
					if app.Language == "en" {
						label = "CHANGE KEY"
						if app.CapturingBind == rustUtilityBindSpecs()[i].ID {
							label = "PRESS A KEY"
						}
					}
					addButton(idBindAssignBase+i, label, 1110, y+15, 150, 30, app.CapturingBind == rustUtilityBindSpecs()[i].ID)
				}
			}
			applyLabel, restoreLabel := "УСТАНОВИТЬ БИНДЫ", "ВОССТАНОВИТЬ ОРИГИНАЛ"
			if app.Language == "en" {
				applyLabel, restoreLabel = "APPLY BINDS", "RESTORE ORIGINAL"
			}
			actionY := 205 + len(rustUtilityBindSpecs())*68 + 78 - app.BindScroll
			if actionY >= 190 && actionY <= 845 {
				addButton(idApplyBinds, applyLabel, 280, actionY, 210, 34, true)
				addButton(idRestoreBinds, restoreLabel, 500, actionY, 210, 34, false)
			}
		case "colors":
			labels := []string{"ПУСТЫНЯ", "ЛЕС", "ЗИМА", "НОЧНАЯ ВИДИМОСТЬ", "ЗАКАТ", "СБРОС"}
			if app.Language == "en" {
				labels = []string{"DESERT", "FOREST", "WINTER", "NIGHT VISIBILITY", "DUSK", "RESET"}
			}
			addButton(idColorWarm, labels[0], 280, 790, 125, 38, false)
			addButton(idColorNeutral, labels[1], 413, 790, 110, 38, false)
			addButton(idColorCool, labels[2], 531, 790, 110, 38, false)
			addButton(idColorNightVision, labels[3], 649, 790, 175, 38, true)
			addButton(idColorDusk, labels[4], 832, 790, 110, 38, false)
			addButton(idColorReset, labels[5], 950, 790, 110, 38, false)
		case "tweaks":
			for i, item := range tweakCardCatalog() {
				y := 150 + i*58 - app.TweakScroll
				if y >= 125 && y <= 820 {
					addButton(item.id, tweakButtonLabel(item.id), 1120, y+12, 145, 28, tweakEnabled(item.id))
				}
			}
			actionY := 150 + len(tweakCardCatalog())*58 + 184 - app.TweakScroll
			if actionY >= 125 && actionY <= 845 {
				addButton(idTweakRestore, "ОТКАТИТЬ ВСЕ ТВИКИ", 280, actionY, 220, 38, false)
				addButton(idDNSFlush, "ОЧИСТИТЬ DNS-КЭШ", 520, actionY, 210, 38, false)
			}
		case "void_radar":
			addRustPlusRadarEdits()
			addButton(idRustRadarConnect, "СОХРАНИТЬ И ПОДКЛЮЧИТЬ", 280, 350, 245, 42, true)
			addButton(idRustRadarDisconnect, "ОТКЛЮЧИТЬ", 540, 350, 170, 42, false)
		case "rust_plus":
			addRustMonitorEdits()
			connectLabel, mapLabel := "ПОДКЛЮЧИТЬ RUST+", "ОТКРЫТЬ RUST+ WEB"
			keyLabel, saveLabel, refreshLabel := "ГДЕ ВЗЯТЬ STEAM KEY?", "СОХРАНИТЬ И НАЧАТЬ", "ОБНОВИТЬ СЕЙЧАС"
			deathLabel, botMapLabel, changesLabel := "ОТКРЫТЬ VOID RADAR", "МОНИТОРИНГ СОБЫТИЙ", "ИЗМЕНЕНИЯ RUST+"
			if app.Language == "en" {
				connectLabel, mapLabel = "PAIR RUST+", "OPEN RUST+ WEB"
				keyLabel, saveLabel, refreshLabel = "GET STEAM API KEY", "SAVE & START", "REFRESH NOW"
				deathLabel, botMapLabel, changesLabel = "OPEN VOID RADAR", "EVENT MONITOR", "RUST+ CHANGES"
			}
			addButton(idRustPlusConnect, connectLabel, 850, 178, 190, 42, true)
			addButton(idRustPlusMap, mapLabel, 1048, 178, 210, 42, false)
			addButton(idRustAPIHelp, keyLabel, 625, 365, 140, 34, false)
			addButton(idRustPlusSave, saveLabel, 280, 503, 220, 38, true)
			addButton(idRustPlusRefresh, refreshLabel, 510, 503, 190, 38, false)
			addButton(idRustPlusOpenRadar, deathLabel, 282, 770, 235, 38, false)
			addButton(idRustPlusEventMonitor, botMapLabel, 530, 770, 220, 38, true)
			addButton(idRustPlusChangelog, changesLabel, 765, 770, 205, 38, false)
		case "display":
			refreshLabel, applyLabel, restoreLabel, customLabel := "ОБНОВИТЬ МОНИТОР", "ПРИМЕНИТЬ РАЗРЕШЕНИЕ", "ВЕРНУТЬ ПРЕДЫДУЩЕЕ", "ПРИМЕНИТЬ СВОЁ"
			if app.Language == "en" {
				refreshLabel, applyLabel, restoreLabel, customLabel = "RESCAN DISPLAY", "APPLY RESOLUTION", "RESTORE PREVIOUS", "APPLY CUSTOM"
			}
			addButton(idMonitorRefresh, refreshLabel, 280, 165, 190, 38, false)
			addButton(idMonitorPrev, "‹", 520, 292, 52, 42, false)
			addButton(idMonitorNext, "›", 810, 292, 52, 42, false)
			addButton(idMonitorApply, applyLabel, 280, 390, 250, 42, true)
			addButton(idMonitorRestore, restoreLabel, 550, 390, 230, 42, false)
			addDisplayEdits()
			addButton(idMonitorCustom, customLabel, 760, 600, 190, 42, true)
		case "settings":
			addButton(idLangRU, "РУССКИЙ", w-485, 170, 165, 42, app.Language == "ru")
			addButton(idLangEN, "ENGLISH", w-305, 170, 165, 42, app.Language == "en")
			addButton(idThemePurple, "VOID PURPLE", w-485, 315, 165, 42, app.Theme == "purple")
			addButton(idThemeBlue, "ARCTIC BLUE", w-305, 315, 165, 42, app.Theme == "blue")
			addButton(idAutostart, autostartButtonLabel(), w-390, 455, 330, 42, app.Autostart)
			addButton(idSaveUpdateURL, "СОХРАНИТЬ URL", w-220, 660, 180, 36, true)
			addButton(idRescanHardware, rescanButtonLabel(), w-390, 780, 330, 36, false)
			addUpdateURLEdit()
		}
	}
	positionWindowChrome()
	refreshButtons()
}

func addLoginControls() {
	if app.LoginEdit == 0 {
		class, _ := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString("EDIT")
		style := wsChild | wsVisible | wsBorder | esAutoHScroll
		h, _, _ := pCreateWindowEx.Call(wsExClientEdge, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(class)), 0, uintptr(style), 465, 430, 390, 42, app.HWND, uintptr(idLoginEdit), app.Instance, 0)
		runtime.KeepAlive(class)
		app.LoginEdit = h
		if h != 0 {
			app.Controls = append(app.Controls, h)
		}
	}
	addButton(idLoginContinue, "ПРОДОЛЖИТЬ", 570, 500, 180, 44, true)
	pSetFocus.Call(app.LoginEdit)
}

func addRustPlusRadarEdits() {
	makeEdit := func(slot *uintptr, x, y, w, h int, placeholder string, password bool) {
		if *slot != 0 {
			return
		}
		class, _ := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString("EDIT")
		style := wsChild | wsVisible | wsBorder | esAutoHScroll
		if password {
			style |= esPassword
		}
		hwnd, _, _ := pCreateWindowEx.Call(wsExClientEdge, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(class)), 0, uintptr(style), uintptr(x), uintptr(y), uintptr(w), uintptr(h), app.HWND, uintptr(idRustRadarConnect), app.Instance, 0)
		runtime.KeepAlive(class)
		*slot = hwnd
		if hwnd != 0 {
			app.Controls = append(app.Controls, hwnd)
			text := ""
			switch slot {
			case &app.RustRadarServerEdit:
				text = app.RustRadarPairing.Server
			case &app.RustRadarPortEdit:
				if app.RustRadarPairing.Port > 0 {
					text = strconv.Itoa(app.RustRadarPairing.Port)
				}
			case &app.RustRadarPlayerIDEdit:
				text = app.RustRadarPairing.PlayerID
			case &app.RustRadarTokenEdit:
				text = app.RustRadarPairing.PlayerToken
			}
			if text != "" {
				v, _ := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString(text)
				pSetWindowText.Call(hwnd, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(v)))
				runtime.KeepAlive(v)
			}
			ph, _ := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString(placeholder)
			pSendMessage.Call(hwnd, 0x1501, 1, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(ph)))
			runtime.KeepAlive(ph)
		}
	}
	makeEdit(&app.RustRadarServerEdit, 280, 235, 450, 34, "IP / адрес сервера из Rust+ pairing", false)
	makeEdit(&app.RustRadarPortEdit, 750, 235, 150, 34, "Rust+ app.port", false)
	makeEdit(&app.RustRadarPlayerIDEdit, 280, 300, 450, 34, "SteamID64 / playerId", false)
	makeEdit(&app.RustRadarTokenEdit, 750, 300, 360, 34, "PlayerToken (секретный)", true)
}

func addRustMonitorEdits() {
	if app.RustAPIKeyEdit == 0 {
		class, _ := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString("EDIT")
		style := wsChild | wsVisible | wsBorder | esAutoHScroll | esPassword
		h, _, _ := pCreateWindowEx.Call(wsExClientEdge, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(class)), 0, uintptr(style), 280, 368, 330, 34, app.HWND, uintptr(idRustPlusSave), app.Instance, 0)
		runtime.KeepAlive(class)
		app.RustAPIKeyEdit = h
		if h != 0 {
			app.Controls = append(app.Controls, h)
			if app.RustSteamAPIKey != "" {
				value, _ := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString(app.RustSteamAPIKey)
				pSetWindowText.Call(h, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(value)))
				runtime.KeepAlive(value)
			}
			placeholder, _ := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString("Steam Web API Key (не Rust+ ключ)")
			pSendMessage.Call(h, 0x1501, 1, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(placeholder)))
			runtime.KeepAlive(placeholder)
		}
	}
	if app.RustIDsEdit == 0 {
		class, _ := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString("EDIT")
		style := wsChild | wsVisible | wsBorder | esMultiline | esAutoVScroll | wsVScroll
		h, _, _ := pCreateWindowEx.Call(wsExClientEdge, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(class)), 0, uintptr(style), 280, 437, 485, 50, app.HWND, uintptr(idRustPlusSave), app.Instance, 0)
		runtime.KeepAlive(class)
		app.RustIDsEdit = h
		if h != 0 {
			app.Controls = append(app.Controls, h)
			value, _ := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString(strings.Join(app.RustTrackedIDs, "\r\n"))
			pSetWindowText.Call(h, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(value)))
			runtime.KeepAlive(value)
			placeholder, _ := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString("SteamID64 — по одному на строку (до 7)")
			pSendMessage.Call(h, 0x1501, 1, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(placeholder)))
			runtime.KeepAlive(placeholder)
		}
	}
}

func addUpdateURLEdit() {
	if app.UpdateURLEdit != 0 {
		return
	}
	class, _ := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString("EDIT")
	style := wsChild | wsVisible | esAutoHScroll
	h, _, _ := pCreateWindowEx.Call(0, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(class)), 0, uintptr(style), 280, 660, 520, 34, app.HWND, uintptr(idSaveUpdateURL), app.Instance, 0)
	runtime.KeepAlive(class)
	app.UpdateURLEdit = h
	if h != 0 {
		app.Controls = append(app.Controls, h)
		value, _ := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString(app.UpdateFeedURL)
		pSetWindowText.Call(h, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(value)))
		runtime.KeepAlive(value)
		placeholder, _ := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString("https://your-site.example/update.json")
		pSendMessage.Call(h, 0x1501, 1, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(placeholder))) // EM_SETCUEBANNER
		runtime.KeepAlive(placeholder)
	}
}
