	if _, e := os.Stat(filepath.Join(app.RustFolder, "RustClient.exe")); e != nil {
		return "", "", "", fmt.Errorf("в выбранной папке не найден RustClient.exe")
	}
	// Rust installs commonly keep keys.cfg under cfg\, while the Facepunch
	// wiki also documents the install-root location. Prefer an existing cfg file,
	// then an existing root file; create the cfg location only when neither exists.
	cfgDir := filepath.Join(app.RustFolder, "cfg")
	cfgKeys := filepath.Join(cfgDir, "keys.cfg")
	rootKeys := filepath.Join(app.RustFolder, "keys.cfg")
	if _, e := os.Stat(cfgKeys); e == nil {
		keys = cfgKeys
	} else if !os.IsNotExist(e) {
		return "", "", "", e
	} else if _, e := os.Stat(rootKeys); e == nil {
		keys = rootKeys
	} else if !os.IsNotExist(e) {
		return "", "", "", e
	} else {
		if e := os.MkdirAll(cfgDir, 0755); e != nil {
			return "", "", "", e
		}
		keys = cfgKeys
	}
	return keys, keys + ".void-backup", keys + ".void-no-original", nil
}

func applyRustUtilityBinds() {
	if rustRunning() {
		app.Status = "Закрой Rust полностью перед установкой биндов. keys.cfg не изменён."
		return
	}
	keys, backup, absent, err := rustKeysPaths()
	if err != nil {
		app.Status = err.Error()
		return
	}
	original, readErr := os.ReadFile(keys)
	if readErr != nil && !os.IsNotExist(readErr) {
		app.Status = "Не удалось прочитать keys.cfg: " + readErr.Error()
		return
	}
	_, backupErr := os.Stat(backup)
	_, absentErr := os.Stat(absent)
	if backupErr != nil && !os.IsNotExist(backupErr) {
		app.Status = "Не удалось проверить резервную копию keys.cfg: " + backupErr.Error()
		return
	}
	if absentErr != nil && !os.IsNotExist(absentErr) {
		app.Status = "Не удалось проверить маркер keys.cfg: " + absentErr.Error()
		return
	}
	if os.IsNotExist(backupErr) && os.IsNotExist(absentErr) {
		if readErr == nil {
			if err := os.WriteFile(backup, original, 0600); err != nil {
				app.Status = "Не удалось создать резервную копию keys.cfg: " + err.Error()
				return
			}
		} else if err := os.WriteFile(absent, []byte("keys.cfg did not exist before VOID applied utility binds\r\n"), 0600); err != nil {
			app.Status = "Не удалось создать маркер отката: " + err.Error()
			return
		}
	}
	if readErr != nil {
		original = nil
	}
	keysMap := normalizedRustBindKeys(app.BindKeys)
	if err := validateRustBindKeys(keysMap); err != nil {
		app.Status = "Не удалось установить бинды: " + err.Error()
		return
	}
	managedDefaults := map[string]bool{}
	for _, spec := range rustUtilityBindSpecs() {
		managedDefaults[spec.DefaultKey] = true
	}
	assigned := map[string]bool{}
	for _, key := range keysMap {
		assigned[key] = true
	}
	for _, line := range strings.Split(string(original), "\n") {
		key := rustBindKey(line)
		if key == "" || managedDefaults[key] || (key == "mouse4" && (strings.Contains(strings.ToLower(line), "hoverloot") || strings.Contains(strings.ToLower(line), "oldloot"))) {
			continue
		}
		if assigned[key] {
			app.Status = "Клавиша " + strings.ToUpper(key) + " уже занята в keys.cfg. Выбери другую — существующий бинд сохранён."
			return
		}
	}
	if err := atomicWriteFile(keys, []byte(mergeRustUtilityBindsWithMap(string(original), keysMap))); err != nil {
		app.Status = "Не удалось записать keys.cfg: " + err.Error()
		return
	}
	app.BindKeys = keysMap
	app.CustomLootKey = keysMap["loot"]
	app.ViewmodelKey = keysMap["viewmodel"]
	if err := savePrefs(); err != nil {
		app.Status = "Бинды записаны, но настройки клавиш не удалось сохранить: " + err.Error()
		return
	}
	app.RustBindsBackupReady = true
	app.Page = "rust_binds"
	app.Status = fmt.Sprintf("Установлено %d настраиваемых QoL-биндов. Исходный keys.cfg сохранён; перезапусти Rust.", len(rustUtilityBindSpecs()))
	if app.Language == "en" {
		app.Status = fmt.Sprintf("%d customizable QoL binds applied. Original keys.cfg backed up; restart Rust.", len(rustUtilityBindSpecs()))
	}
}

func restoreRustUtilityBinds() {
	if rustRunning() {
		app.Status = "Закрой Rust полностью перед откатом биндов. keys.cfg не изменён."
		return
	}
	keys, backup, absent, err := rustKeysPaths()
	if err != nil {
		app.Status = err.Error()
		return
	}
	data, backupErr := os.ReadFile(backup)
	if backupErr == nil {
		if err := atomicWriteFile(keys, data); err != nil {
			app.Status = "Не удалось восстановить keys.cfg: " + err.Error()
			return
		}
		if err := os.Remove(backup); err != nil {
			app.Status = "Файл восстановлен, но резервная копия не удалена: " + err.Error()
			return
		}
		_ = os.Remove(absent)
	} else if os.IsNotExist(backupErr) {
		if _, markerErr := os.Stat(absent); markerErr == nil {
			if err := os.Remove(keys); err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {
				app.Status = "Не удалось удалить созданный VOID keys.cfg: " + err.Error()
				return
			}
			if err := os.Remove(absent); err != nil {
				app.Status = "Файл удалён, но маркер не удалён: " + err.Error()
				return
			}
		} else {
			app.Status = "Резервная копия keys.cfg не найдена — откатывать нечего."
			return
		}
	} else {
		app.Status = "Не удалось прочитать резервную копию keys.cfg: " + backupErr.Error()
		return
	}
	app.RustBindsBackupReady = false
	app.Page = "rust_binds"
	app.Status = "Исходный keys.cfg восстановлен."
	if app.Language == "en" {
		app.Status = "Original keys.cfg restored."
	}
}

func selectRustHardwareProfile() bool {
	if !app.HardwareReady {
		app.Status = "Сначала дождись определения CPU, GPU и RAM или запусти повторное обнаружение на главной."
		if app.Language == "en" {
			app.Status = "Wait for CPU, GPU and RAM detection, or rescan hardware on Home first."
		}
		app.Page = "rust"
		return false
	}
	rec := recommendRustHardware(app.Hardware)
	app.RustProfileID = idRustAuto
	app.Quality = rec.Quality
	app.DrawDistance = rec.DrawDistance
	app.FOV = 90
	app.VSync = false
	if app.Language == "en" {
		app.Status = "Hardware profile " + rec.Tier + " selected: " + rec.Reason + " VOID will write client.cfg only while Rust is closed."
	} else {
		app.Status = "Автопрофиль " + rec.Tier + " подобран по железу: " + rec.Reason + " Сейчас VOID попробует сохранить профиль в client.cfg (только если Rust закрыт)."
	}
	app.Page = "rust"
	return true
}

func selectRustPreset(id int, name string, q, dist, fov int, vsync bool) {
	app.RustProfileID = id
	app.Quality, app.DrawDistance, app.FOV, app.VSync = q, dist, fov, vsync
	if app.Language == "en" {
		app.Status = "Selected " + name + " community-based profile. Press APPLY CFG to write it while Rust is closed."
	} else {
		app.Status = "Выбран профиль «" + name + "». Нажми «ПРИМЕНИТЬ CFG» при закрытом Rust, чтобы записать его с резервной копией."
	}
	app.Page = "rust"
}

func rustProfileName(id int) string {
	if id == idRustAuto {
		if app.Language == "en" {
			return "SMART HARDWARE PROFILE"
		}
		return "АВТОПРОФИЛЬ ПОД ЖЕЛЕЗО"
	}
	switch id {
	case idLow:
		if app.Language == "en" {
			return "MAX FPS"
		}
		return "МАКС. FPS"
	case idPvp:
		return "PVP"
	case idSurvival:
		if app.Language == "en" {
			return "BALANCED"
		}
		return "БАЛАНС"
	case idQuality:
		if app.Language == "en" {
			return "DRAW DISTANCE 2000"
		}
		return "ДАЛЬНОСТЬ 2000"
	default:
		if app.Language == "en" {
			return "PVP BASELINE"
		}
		return "БАЗОВЫЙ PVP"
	}
}

func handleMouse(x, y int, down bool) {
	if app.Page == "colors" {
		const left, right = 280, 840
		switch {
		case y >= 264 && y <= 288 && x >= left && x <= right:
			app.Dragging = "brightness"
			app.Brightness = mapSlider(x, left, right, -20, 20)
		case y >= 344 && y <= 368 && x >= left && x <= right:
			app.Dragging = "contrast"
			app.Contrast = mapSlider(x, left, right, 70, 130)
		case y >= 424 && y <= 448 && x >= left && x <= right:
			app.Dragging = "gamma"
			app.Gamma = mapSlider(x, left, right, 70, 130)
		case y >= 504 && y <= 528 && x >= left && x <= right:
			app.Dragging = "red"
			app.RedGain = mapSlider(x, left, right, 70, 130)
		case y >= 584 && y <= 608 && x >= left && x <= right:
			app.Dragging = "green"
			app.GreenGain = mapSlider(x, left, right, 70, 130)
		case y >= 664 && y <= 688 && x >= left && x <= right:
			app.Dragging = "blue"
			app.BlueGain = mapSlider(x, left, right, 70, 130)
		}
	}
	if app.Dragging != "" {
		const left, right = 280, 840
		switch app.Dragging {
		case "brightness":
			app.Brightness = mapSlider(x, left, right, -20, 20)
		case "contrast":
			app.Contrast = mapSlider(x, left, right, 70, 130)
		case "gamma":
			app.Gamma = mapSlider(x, left, right, 70, 130)
		case "red":
			app.RedGain = mapSlider(x, left, right, 70, 130)
		case "green":
			app.GreenGain = mapSlider(x, left, right, 70, 130)
		case "blue":
			app.BlueGain = mapSlider(x, left, right, 70, 130)
		}
		setGamma()
		invalidate()
	}
}

func mapSlider(x, x1, x2, min, max int) int {
	if x < x1 {
		x = x1
	}
	if x > x2 {
		x = x2
	}
	return min + int(math.Round(float64(x-x1)/float64(x2-x1)*float64(max-min)))
}
func vsyncText() string {
	if app.VSync {
		return "V-SYNC: ON"
	}
	return "V-SYNC: OFF"
}

func scanTemp() {
	language := app.Language
	if app.Busy {
		app.Status = "Подожди завершения текущей операции."
		if language == "en" {
			app.Status = "Please wait for the current operation to finish."
		}
		invalidate()
		return
	}
	app.Busy = true
	app.Scanned = false
	app.Preview = nil
	app.Status = "Сканирование временных папок выполняется в фоне..."
	if language == "en" {
		app.Status = "Scanning temporary folders in the background..."
	}
	ch := make(chan scanResult, 1)
	app.ScanCh = ch
	go func() {
		files := scanTempWorker()
		var bytes int64
		for _, f := range files {
			bytes += f.Size
		}
		status := fmt.Sprintf("Найдено файлов: %d • доступно к удалению около %s. Фильтр: старше 1 часа.", len(files), humanBytesForLanguage(bytes, language))
		if language == "en" {
			status = fmt.Sprintf("Found %d files • about %s can be freed. Filter: files older than 1 hour.", len(files), humanBytesForLanguage(bytes, language))
		}
		ch <- scanResult{Files: files, Status: status}
	}()
	invalidate()
}

// isReparsePoint fails closed: if Windows cannot inspect a path, cleanup skips it.
// This prevents traversal into junctions/mount points inside a Temp directory.
func isReparsePoint(path string) bool {
	p, err := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString(path)
	if err != nil {
		return true
	}
	attrs, _, _ := pGetFileAttributes.Call(uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(p)))
	runtime.KeepAlive(p)
	if uint32(attrs) == 0xFFFFFFFF { // INVALID_FILE_ATTRIBUTES
		return true
	}
	const fileAttributeReparsePoint = 0x0400
	return uint32(attrs)&fileAttributeReparsePoint != 0
}

func scanTempWorker() []cleanupFile {
	files := []cleanupFile{}
	seen := map[string]bool{}
	roots := tempRoots()
	cutoff := time.Now().Add(-time.Hour)
	for _, root := range roots {
		rootInfo, err := os.Lstat(root)
		if err != nil || !rootInfo.IsDir() || rootInfo.Mode()&os.ModeSymlink != 0 || isReparsePoint(root) {
			continue
		}
		_ = filepath.WalkDir(root, func(path string, d os.DirEntry, walkErr error) error {
			if walkErr != nil {
				return nil
			}
			if d.IsDir() {
				if path != root && (d.Type()&os.ModeSymlink != 0 || isReparsePoint(path)) {
					return filepath.SkipDir
				}
				return nil
			}
			if d.Type()&os.ModeSymlink != 0 || isReparsePoint(path) || seen[strings.ToLower(path)] || !withinTempRoots(path, roots) {
				return nil
			}
			info, e := d.Info()
			if e == nil && info.Mode().IsRegular() && info.ModTime().Before(cutoff) {
				seen[strings.ToLower(path)] = true
				files = append(files, cleanupFile{path, info.Size()})
			}
			return nil
		})
	}
	return files
}

func tempRoots() []string {
	roots := []string{os.TempDir(), os.Getenv("TEMP"), os.Getenv("TMP")}
	if local := os.Getenv("LOCALAPPDATA"); local != "" {
		roots = append(roots, filepath.Join(local, "Temp"))
	}
	unique := make([]string, 0, len(roots))
	seen := map[string]bool{}
	for _, root := range roots {
		if root == "" {
			continue
		}
		abs, err := filepath.Abs(root)
		if err != nil {
			continue
		}
		abs = filepath.Clean(abs)
		key := strings.ToLower(abs)
		if !seen[key] {
			seen[key] = true
			unique = append(unique, abs)
		}
	}
	return unique
}

func withinTempRoots(path string, roots []string) bool {
	abs, err := filepath.Abs(path)
	if err != nil {
		return false
	}
	abs = filepath.Clean(abs)
	for _, root := range roots {
		r := strings.TrimRight(filepath.Clean(root), `\/`)
		if strings.EqualFold(abs, r) {
			return false
		}
		if strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(abs), strings.ToLower(r+string(os.PathSeparator))) {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

func safeTempFilePath(path string, roots []string) bool {
	abs, err := filepath.Abs(path)
	if err != nil {
		return false
	}
	abs = filepath.Clean(abs)
	for _, root := range roots {
		rootAbs, err := filepath.Abs(root)
		if err != nil {
			continue
		}
		rootAbs = filepath.Clean(rootAbs)
		rel, err := filepath.Rel(rootAbs, abs)
		if err != nil || rel == "." || rel == ".." || strings.HasPrefix(rel, ".."+string(os.PathSeparator)) || filepath.IsAbs(rel) {
			continue
		}
		current := rootAbs
		if isReparsePoint(current) {
			return false
		}
		parts := strings.Split(rel, string(os.PathSeparator))
		for i, part := range parts {
			if part == "" || part == "." || part == ".." {
				return false
			}
			current = filepath.Join(current, part)
			if isReparsePoint(current) {
				return false
			}
			if i < len(parts)-1 {
				info, statErr := os.Lstat(current)
				if statErr != nil || !info.IsDir() || info.Mode()&os.ModeSymlink != 0 {
					return false
				}
			}
		}
		return true
	}
	return false
}

func cleanTemp() {
	language := app.Language
	if app.Busy {
		app.Status = "Подожди завершения текущей операции."
		if language == "en" {
			app.Status = "Please wait for the current operation to finish."
		}
		invalidate()
		return
	}
	if !app.Scanned {
		app.Status = "Сначала нажми «Сканировать», чтобы увидеть список временных файлов."
		if language == "en" {
			app.Status = "Scan first to review the list of temporary files."
		}
		invalidate()
		return
	}
	if len(app.Preview) == 0 {
		app.Status = "Сканирование завершено: старых временных файлов для удаления не найдено."
		if language == "en" {
			app.Status = "Scan complete: no old temporary files were found."
		}
		invalidate()
		return
	}
	files := append([]cleanupFile(nil), app.Preview...)
	ch := make(chan cleanResult, 1)
	app.CleanCh = ch
	app.Busy = true
	app.Status = "Удаление найденных временных файлов выполняется в фоне..."
	if language == "en" {
		app.Status = "Deleting selected temporary files in the background..."
	}
	go func() {
		var bytes int64
		count, skipped, failed := 0, 0, 0
		cutoff := time.Now().Add(-time.Hour)
		roots := tempRoots()
		for _, f := range files {
			if !safeTempFilePath(f.Path, roots) {
				skipped++
				continue
			}
			info, err := os.Lstat(f.Path)
			if err != nil || !info.Mode().IsRegular() || info.Mode()&os.ModeSymlink != 0 || !info.ModTime().Before(cutoff) {
				skipped++
				continue
			}
			if err := os.Remove(f.Path); err == nil {
				count++
				bytes += info.Size()
			} else {
				failed++
			}
		}
		status := fmt.Sprintf("Готово: удалено %d файлов • освобождено %s • пропущено %d • ошибок доступа %d.", count, humanBytesForLanguage(bytes, language), skipped, failed)
		if language == "en" {
			status = fmt.Sprintf("Done: deleted %d files • freed %s • skipped %d • access errors %d.", count, humanBytesForLanguage(bytes, language), skipped, failed)
		}
		ch <- cleanResult{Count: count, Bytes: bytes, Skipped: skipped, Failed: failed, Status: status}
	}()
	invalidate()
}

func humanBytes(v int64) string {
	return humanBytesForLanguage(v, "ru")
}

func humanBytesForLanguage(v int64, language string) string {
	units := []string{"Б", "КБ", "МБ", "ГБ"}
	if language == "en" {
		units = []string{"B", "KB", "MB", "GB"}
	}
	x := float64(v)
	i := 0
	for x >= 1024 && i < len(units)-1 {
		x /= 1024
		i++
	}
	return fmt.Sprintf("%.1f %s", x, units[i])
}

func writeFileAtomic(path string, data []byte, perm os.FileMode) error {
	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(path), 0755); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	tmp, err := os.CreateTemp(filepath.Dir(path), ".void-*.tmp")
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	tmpName := tmp.Name()
	defer os.Remove(tmpName)
	if err = tmp.Chmod(perm); err != nil {
		tmp.Close()
		return err
	}
	if _, err = tmp.Write(data); err != nil {
		tmp.Close()
		return err
	}
	if err = tmp.Sync(); err != nil {
		tmp.Close()
		return err
	}
	if err = tmp.Close(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if runtime.GOOS == "windows" {
		src, e1 := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString(tmpName)
		dst, e2 := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString(path)
		if e1 != nil {
			return e1
		}
		if e2 != nil {
			return e2
		}
		r, _, callErr := pMoveFileEx.Call(uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(src)), uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(dst)), 0x1|0x8) // REPLACE_EXISTING | WRITE_THROUGH
		runtime.KeepAlive(src)
		runtime.KeepAlive(dst)
		if r == 0 {
			if callErr != syscall.Errno(0) {
				return callErr
			}
			return fmt.Errorf("MoveFileExW failed replacing %s", path)
		}
		return nil
	}
	return os.Rename(tmpName, path)
}

func quickOptimizationTweaks() []int {
	return []int{idTweakGame, idTweakDVR, idTweakTrans, idTweakFX, idTweakAnim, idTweakMenu, idTweakOverlay, idTweakRustGPU, idTweakTaskbarAnim, idTweakAeroPeek, idTweakListAlpha, idTweakDragFull}
}

func containsTweakID(ids []int, target int) bool {
	for _, id := range ids {
		if id == target {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

func validQuickOptimizationTweaks(ids []int) bool {
	allowed := map[int]bool{}
	for _, id := range quickOptimizationTweaks() {
		allowed[id] = true
	}
	seen := map[int]bool{}
	for _, id := range ids {
		if !allowed[id] || seen[id] {
			return false
		}
		seen[id] = true
	}
	return true
}

func saveOptimizerState(path string, state optimizerState) error {
	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(state, "", "  ")
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return writeFileAtomic(path, data, 0600)
}

func runningOnACPower() (onAC, known bool) {
	var status systemPowerStatus
	result, _, _ := pGetSystemPowerStatus.Call(uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&status)))
	runtime.KeepAlive(&status)
	if result == 0 || status.ACLineStatus == 255 {
		return false, false
	}
	return status.ACLineStatus == 1, true
}

func applyBoost() {
	if app.TweaksBackupError != "" {
		app.Status = app.TweaksBackupError + " Сначала исправь файл backup; схема питания не изменена."
		if app.Language == "en" {
			app.Status = app.TweaksBackupError + " Fix the backup file first; the power plan was not changed."
		}
		return
	}

	statePath := restoreStatePath()
	st := optimizerState{BoostTweaks: []int{}}
	data, statErr := os.ReadFile(statePath)
	if statErr == nil {
		if json.Unmarshal(data, &st) != nil || st.PreviousPowerPlan == "" || !regexp.MustCompile(`(?i)^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$`).MatchString(st.PreviousPowerPlan) {
			app.Status = "Файл резервной копии схемы питания повреждён. Ничего не изменено; проверь backup вручную."
			return
		}
		// Older releases did not store the exact list. Preserve their previous
		// rollback behaviour for Game Mode and Game DVR only.
		if st.BoostTweaks == nil {
			st.BoostTweaks = []int{}
			for _, id := range []int{idTweakGame, idTweakDVR} {
				for _, change := range changesForTweak(id) {
					if _, ok := app.TweaksBackup[changeKey(change)]; ok {
						st.BoostTweaks = append(st.BoostTweaks, id)
						break
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if !validQuickOptimizationTweaks(st.BoostTweaks) {
			app.Status = "Список твиков в backup повреждён. Ничего не изменено."
			return
		}
	} else if !os.IsNotExist(statErr) {
		app.Status = "Не удалось прочитать backup схемы питания: " + statErr.Error()
		return
	} else {
		out, err := runHidden("powercfg.exe", "/getactivescheme")
		if err != nil {
			app.Status = "Не удалось прочитать текущую схему питания: " + strings.TrimSpace(out)
			return
		}
		re := regexp.MustCompile(`(?i)([0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12})`)
		if m := re.FindStringSubmatch(out); len(m) > 1 {
			st.PreviousPowerPlan = m[1]
		}
		if st.PreviousPowerPlan == "" {
			app.Status = "Не удалось определить текущую схему питания — ничего не изменено."
			return
		}
	}

	// Save a valid recovery record before touching power settings or registry values.
	if err := saveOptimizerState(statePath, st); err != nil {
		app.Status = "Не удалось сохранить backup — параметры не изменены: " + err.Error()
		return
	}
	// Avoid forcing a high-power plan while a laptop is running from battery.
	// If a previous VOID run had already switched plans, put the saved user plan
	// back before continuing; the rollback journal remains intact.
	onAC, powerStatusKnown := runningOnACPower()
	powerPlanNote := "схема питания не изменялась (состояние питания неизвестно)"
	if powerStatusKnown && onAC {
		powerOut, powerErr := runHidden("powercfg.exe", "/setactive", "SCHEME_MIN")
		if powerErr != nil {
			app.Status = "Схему High Performance включить не удалось: " + strings.TrimSpace(powerOut)
			return
		}
		powerPlanNote = "High Performance включена от сети"
	} else {
		if _, powerErr := runHidden("powercfg.exe", "/setactive", st.PreviousPowerPlan); powerErr != nil {
			app.Status = "Не удалось сохранить исходную схему питания: " + powerErr.Error()
			return
		}
		if powerStatusKnown {
			powerPlanNote = "работа от батареи — исходная схема питания сохранена"
		}
	}

	applied := 0
	skipped := 0
	for _, tweakID := range quickOptimizationTweaks() {
		changes := changesForTweak(tweakID)
		if len(changes) == 0 {
			skipped++
			continue
		} // Rust GPU preference is optional when no install is found.
		if tweakEnabled(tweakID) {
			continue
		} // Never claim or journal a value that was already set before VOID.
		if !containsTweakID(st.BoostTweaks, tweakID) {
			st.BoostTweaks = append(st.BoostTweaks, tweakID)
			if err := saveOptimizerState(statePath, st); err != nil {
				app.Status = "Не удалось обновить журнал отката: " + err.Error()
				if app.Language == "en" {
					app.Status = "Could not update the rollback journal: " + err.Error()
				}
				return
			}
		}
		if err := applyTweak(tweakID); err != nil {
			app.Status = "Оптимизация остановлена на одном из твиков: " + err.Error() + ". Остальные изменения не скрыты; используй откат при необходимости."
			if app.Language == "en" {
				app.Status = "Optimization stopped on a tweak: " + err.Error() + ". Any completed changes remain backed up for rollback."
			}
			return
		}
		applied++
	}
	msg := fmt.Sprintf("Готово: %s; применено %d твиков. Rust GPU-настройка пропущена, если игра не найдена. Перезапусти Rust для применения GPU-предпочтения. Для возврата нажми «Откатить настройки». Необязательных шагов пропущено: %d.", powerPlanNote, applied, skipped)
	if app.Language == "en" {
		msg = fmt.Sprintf("Done: %s; %d tweaks applied. Rust GPU preference is skipped if the game is not found. Restart Rust for the GPU preference to take effect. Press Restore Settings to roll back. Optional steps skipped: %d.", powerPlanNote, applied, skipped)
	}
	app.Status = msg
}

func restoreBoost() {
	statePath := restoreStatePath()
	data, err := os.ReadFile(statePath)
	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
		app.Status = "Backup схемы питания не найден — VOID ещё не менял её."
		if app.Language == "en" {
			app.Status = "Power-plan backup not found; VOID has not changed it."
		}
		return
	}
	if err != nil {
		app.Status = "Не удалось прочитать backup схемы питания: " + err.Error()
		return
	}
	var st optimizerState
	guidPattern := regexp.MustCompile(`(?i)^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$`)
	if json.Unmarshal(data, &st) != nil || !guidPattern.MatchString(st.PreviousPowerPlan) {
		app.Status = "Не удалось прочитать backup схемы питания: GUID схемы повреждён."
		return
	}

	// Legacy backup compatibility: releases before 1.7.0 tracked Game Mode and DVR
	// implicitly. New backup files always include the explicit list.
	if st.BoostTweaks == nil {
		st.BoostTweaks = []int{}
		for _, id := range []int{idTweakGame, idTweakDVR} {
			for _, change := range changesForTweak(id) {
				if _, ok := app.TweaksBackup[changeKey(change)]; ok {
					st.BoostTweaks = append(st.BoostTweaks, id)
					break
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if !validQuickOptimizationTweaks(st.BoostTweaks) {
		app.Status = "Список твиков в backup повреждён. Откат заблокирован."
		return
	}
	if _, err := runHidden("powercfg.exe", "/setactive", st.PreviousPowerPlan); err != nil {
		app.Status = "Не удалось восстановить прежнюю схему питания."
		return
	}

	failedTweaks := 0
	for _, tweakID := range st.BoostTweaks {
		changes := changesForTweak(tweakID)
		if len(changes) == 0 {
			failedTweaks++
			continue
		}
		snapshots := make([]regSnapshot, len(changes))
		completeBackup := true
		hasAnyBackup := false
		for i, change := range changes {
			snapshot, ok := app.TweaksBackup[changeKey(change)]
			if !ok {
				completeBackup = false
				continue
			}
			hasAnyBackup = true
			snapshots[i] = snapshot
		}
		if !hasAnyBackup && !tweakEnabled(tweakID) {
			continue // An earlier restore attempt already completed this tweak.
		}
		if !completeBackup || !hasAnyBackup {
			failedTweaks++
			continue
		}
		restoreFailed := false
		for i, change := range changes {
			if err := restoreReg(change.Key, change.Name, snapshots[i]); err != nil {
				restoreFailed = true
				break
			}
		}
		if restoreFailed {
			failedTweaks++
			continue
		}
		next := cloneTweaksBackup(app.TweaksBackup)
		for _, change := range changes {
			delete(next, changeKey(change))
		}
		if err := saveTweaksBackupMap(next); err != nil {
			failedTweaks++
			continue
		}
		app.TweaksBackup = next
	}
	if failedTweaks > 0 {
		app.Status = fmt.Sprintf("Схема питания восстановлена, но %d твик(ов) не удалось вернуть. Backup сохранён для повтора.", failedTweaks)
		if app.Language == "en" {
			app.Status = fmt.Sprintf("Power plan restored, but %d tweak(s) could not be restored. Backups remain for retry.", failedTweaks)
		}
		return
	}
	if err := os.Remove(statePath); err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {
		app.Status = "Параметры восстановлены, но файл состояния не удалось удалить: " + err.Error()
		return
	}
	app.Status = "Прежняя схема питания и параметры быстрой оптимизации восстановлены."
	if app.Language == "en" {
		app.Status = "Previous power plan and quick-optimization settings have been restored."
	}
}

func appDataRoot() string {
	if root := os.Getenv("LOCALAPPDATA"); root != "" {
		return root
	}
	if root, err := os.UserConfigDir(); err == nil && root != "" {
		return root
	}
	return os.TempDir()
}
func restoreStatePath() string {
	return filepath.Join(appDataRoot(), "VOID Optimizer", "restore-state.json")
}
func queryDword(name string) (bool, int) {
	out, err := runHidden("reg.exe", "query", `HKCU\Software\Microsoft\GameBar`, "/v", name)
	if err != nil {
		return false, 0
	}
	re := regexp.MustCompile(`(?i)REG_DWORD\s+0x([0-9a-f]+)`)
	m := re.FindStringSubmatch(out)
	if len(m) < 2 {
		return false, 0
	}
	v, _ := strconv.ParseInt(m[1], 16, 32)
	return true, int(v)
}

func findRust() string {
	roots := map[string]bool{}
	for _, p := range []string{filepath.Join(os.Getenv("ProgramFiles(x86)"), "Steam"), filepath.Join(os.Getenv("ProgramFiles"), "Steam")} {
		if p != "" {
			roots[p] = true
		}
	}
	for _, item := range []struct {
		key   string
		value string
	}{{`HKCU\Software\Valve\Steam`, "SteamPath"}, {`HKLM\SOFTWARE\WOW6432Node\Valve\Steam`, "InstallPath"}, {`HKLM\SOFTWARE\Valve\Steam`, "InstallPath"}} {
		out, err := runHidden("reg.exe", "query", item.key, "/v", item.value)
		if err == nil {
			lines := strings.Split(out, "\n")
			for _, line := range lines {
				if strings.Contains(line, item.value) {
					f := strings.Fields(line)
					if len(f) >= 3 {
						roots[strings.TrimSpace(strings.Join(f[2:], " "))] = true
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	all := map[string]bool{}
	for r := range roots {
		all[r] = true
		b, e := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(r, "steamapps", "libraryfolders.vdf"))
		if e == nil {
			re := regexp.MustCompile(`"path"\s+"([^"]+)"`)
			for _, m := range re.FindAllStringSubmatch(string(b), -1) {
				if len(m) > 1 {
					all[strings.ReplaceAll(m[1], `\\`, `\`)] = true
				}
			}
		}
	}
	for r := range all {
		candidate := filepath.Join(r, "steamapps", "common", "Rust")
		if _, e := os.Stat(filepath.Join(candidate, "RustClient.exe")); e == nil {
			return candidate
		}
	}
	return ""
}
func rustRunning() bool {
	h, _, _ := pCreateToolhelpSnap.Call(th32csSnap, 0)
	if h == uintptr(^uintptr(0)) {
		return true // fail closed: show read-only/running state if process enumeration fails
	}
	defer pCloseHandle.Call(h)
	var pe processEntry32
	pe.Size = uint32(unsafe.Sizeof(pe))
	r, _, _ := pProcess32First.Call(h, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&pe)))
	if r == 0 {
		return true
	}
	for r != 0 {
		name := strings.ToLower(syscall.UTF16ToString(pe.ExeFile[:]))
		if name == "rustclient.exe" || name == "rust.exe" {
			return true
		}
		r, _, _ = pProcess32Next.Call(h, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&pe)))
	}
	return false
}
func browseRust() {
	buf := make([]uint16, 32768)
	filterWords := utf16.Encode([]rune("Rust client executable\x00RustClient.exe\x00\x00"))
	filter := &filterWords[0]
	title, _ := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString("Выбери RustClient.exe")
	of := openFileName{Size: uint32(unsafe.Sizeof(openFileName{})), Owner: app.HWND, Filter: filter, FilterIndex: 1, File: &buf[0], MaxFile: uint32(len(buf)), Title: title, Flags: ofFileMust | ofPathMust | ofNoChangeDir}
	r, _, _ := pGetOpenFileName.Call(uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&of)))
	runtime.KeepAlive(&of)
	runtime.KeepAlive(title)
	runtime.KeepAlive(filterWords)
	runtime.KeepAlive(buf)
	if r != 0 {
		selected := syscall.UTF16ToString(buf)
		if strings.EqualFold(filepath.Base(selected), "RustClient.exe") {
			app.RustFolder = filepath.Dir(selected)
			app.Status = "Rust выбран: " + app.RustFolder
		} else {
			app.Status = "Выбери именно RustClient.exe."
		}
	}
}

func captureGamma() {
	dc, _, _ := pGetDC.Call(0)
	if dc == 0 {
		return
	}
	defer pReleaseDC.Call(0, dc)
	r, _, _ := pGetDeviceGammaRamp.Call(dc, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&app.GammaOriginal[0])))
	runtime.KeepAlive(&app.GammaOriginal)
	app.GammaAvailable = r != 0
}
func setGamma() {
	if !app.GammaAvailable {
		return
	}
	var ramp [768]uint16
	contrast := float64(app.Contrast) / 100.0
	gamma := float64(app.Gamma) / 100.0
	brightness := float64(app.Brightness) / 100.0
	gains := []float64{float64(app.RedGain) / 100.0, float64(app.GreenGain) / 100.0, float64(app.BlueGain) / 100.0}
	for ch := 0; ch < 3; ch++ {
		for i := 0; i < 256; i++ {
			x := float64(app.GammaOriginal[ch*256+i]) / 65535.0
			x = (x-0.5)*contrast + 0.5 + brightness*0.35
			if x < 0 {
				x = 0
			}
			if x > 1 {
				x = 1
			}
			x = math.Pow(x, 1.0/gamma)
			x *= gains[ch]
			if x < 0 {
				x = 0
			}
			if x > 1 {
				x = 1
			}
			v := int(math.Round(x * 65535))
			if v < 0 {
				v = 0
			}
			if v > 65535 {
				v = 65535
			}
			ramp[ch*256+i] = uint16(v)
		}
	}
	dc, _, _ := pGetDC.Call(0)
	if dc == 0 {
		return
	}
	r, _, _ := pSetDeviceGammaRamp.Call(dc, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&ramp[0])))
	app.GammaSetFailed = r == 0
	runtime.KeepAlive(&ramp)
	pReleaseDC.Call(0, dc)
}

func resetGamma() {
	if !app.GammaAvailable {
		return
	}
	dc, _, _ := pGetDC.Call(0)
	if dc == 0 {
		return
	}
	r, _, _ := pSetDeviceGammaRamp.Call(dc, uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&app.GammaOriginal[0])))
	app.GammaSetFailed = r == 0
	runtime.KeepAlive(&app.GammaOriginal)
	pReleaseDC.Call(0, dc)
}
func restoreGamma() { resetGamma() }

func autostartButtonLabel() string {
	if app.Language == "en" {
		if app.Autostart {
			return "DISABLE AUTOSTART"
		}
		return "ENABLE AUTOSTART"
	}
	if app.Autostart {
		return "ОТКЛЮЧИТЬ АВТОЗАПУСК"
	}
	return "ВКЛЮЧИТЬ АВТОЗАПУСК"
}
func rescanButtonLabel() string {
	if app.Language == "en" {
		return "RESCAN PC COMPONENTS"
	}
	return "ПОВТОРНО ОПРЕДЕЛИТЬ ПК"
}

func autostartEnabled() bool {
	out, err := runHidden("reg.exe", "query", `HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run`, "/v", "VOIDOptimizer")
	return err == nil && strings.Contains(strings.ToLower(out), "voidoptimizer")
}

func setAutostart(enable bool) error {
	const key = `HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run`
	if enable {
		exe, err := os.Executable()
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		exe, err = filepath.Abs(exe)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		_, err = runHidden("reg.exe", "add", key, "/v", "VOIDOptimizer", "/t", "REG_SZ", "/d", `"`+exe+`"`, "/f")
		return err
	}
	_, err := runHidden("reg.exe", "delete", key, "/v", "VOIDOptimizer", "/f")
	if err != nil && !autostartEnabled() {
		return nil
	}
	return err
}

func detectHardware() hardwareInfo {
	ps := `$ErrorActionPreference='SilentlyContinue'; [Console]::OutputEncoding=[System.Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false); $cpus=@(Get-CimInstance Win32_Processor); $cpu=($cpus | Select-Object -First 1 -ExpandProperty Name); $threads=0; foreach($p in $cpus){$threads += [int]$p.NumberOfLogicalProcessors}; $gpus=@(Get-CimInstance Win32_VideoController | Where-Object {$_.Name}); $gpu=(($gpus | Select-Object -ExpandProperty Name) -join ' / '); $vram=0.0; foreach($g in $gpus){if($g.AdapterRAM -and ([double]$g.AdapterRAM/1GB) -gt $vram){$vram=[double]$g.AdapterRAM/1GB}}; $mods=@(Get-CimInstance Win32_PhysicalMemory); $bytes=0.0; foreach($m in $mods){$bytes += [double]$m.Capacity}; $types=@($mods | ForEach-Object {switch ([int]$_.SMBIOSMemoryType) {24 {'DDR3'} 26 {'DDR4'} 34 {'DDR5'} default {'RAM'}}} | Select-Object -Unique); $disk=((Get-CimInstance Win32_DiskDrive | Select-Object -ExpandProperty Model) -join ' / '); $os=(Get-CimInstance Win32_OperatingSystem).Caption; [pscustomobject]@{CPU=$cpu;GPU=$gpu;RAMGB=[math]::Round($bytes/1GB,0);RAMType=($types -join '/');Disks=$disk;OS=$os;LogicalCPUs=$threads;GPUMemoryGB=[math]::Round($vram,1)} | ConvertTo-Json -Compress`
	out, err := runHidden("powershell.exe", "-NoLogo", "-NoProfile", "-NonInteractive", "-ExecutionPolicy", "Bypass", "-Command", ps)
	if err != nil {
		return hardwareInfo{}
	}
	var info hardwareInfo
	if json.Unmarshal([]byte(strings.TrimSpace(out)), &info) != nil {
		return hardwareInfo{}
	}
	return info
}

func readUpdateFeedURL() (string, error) {
	return effectiveUpdateFeedURL(app.UpdateFeedURL, publisherUpdateFeedURL)
}

func httpsOnlyRedirect(req *http.Request, via []*http.Request) error {
	if req.URL == nil || req.URL.Scheme != "https" || req.URL.Host == "" || req.URL.User != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("update redirects must remain on HTTPS")
	}
	if len(via) >= 5 {
		return fmt.Errorf("too many update redirects")
	}
	return nil
}

func fetchUpdateManifest(feed string) (updateManifest, error) {
	client := &http.Client{Timeout: 12 * time.Second, CheckRedirect: httpsOnlyRedirect}
	req, err := http.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, feed, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return updateManifest{}, err
	}
	req.Header.Set("User-Agent", "VOID-Optimizer/"+appVersion)
	resp, err := client.Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		return updateManifest{}, err
	}
	defer resp.Body.Close()
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		return updateManifest{}, fmt.Errorf("update server returned HTTP %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if resp.ContentLength > 64*1024 {
		return updateManifest{}, fmt.Errorf("update manifest is too large")
	}
	body, err := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(resp.Body, 64*1024+1))
	if len(body) > 64*1024 {
		return updateManifest{}, fmt.Errorf("update manifest is too large")
	}
	if err != nil {
		return updateManifest{}, err
	}
	var manifest updateManifest
	if err := json.Unmarshal(body, &manifest); err != nil {
		return updateManifest{}, fmt.Errorf("invalid update manifest: %w", err)
	}
	if _, ok := versionParts(manifest.Version); !ok {
		return updateManifest{}, fmt.Errorf("manifest version must use x.y.z format")
	}
	download, err := url.Parse(manifest.DownloadURL)
	if err != nil || download.Scheme != "https" || download.Host == "" || download.User != nil {
		return updateManifest{}, fmt.Errorf("download URL must be HTTPS")
	}
	if len(manifest.SHA256) != 64 {
		return updateManifest{}, fmt.Errorf("manifest SHA-256 must contain 64 hexadecimal characters")
	}
	if _, err := hex.DecodeString(manifest.SHA256); err != nil {
		return updateManifest{}, fmt.Errorf("manifest SHA-256 is invalid")
	}
	if len(manifest.Notes) > 2000 {
		manifest.Notes = manifest.Notes[:2000]
	}
	return manifest, nil
}

func startUpdateCheck() {
	if app.UpdateCh != nil {
		return
	}
	app.NextUpdateCheck = time.Now().Add(24 * time.Hour)
	language := app.Language
	app.UpdateStatus = "Автообновление: проверка версии..."
	if app.Language == "en" {
		app.UpdateStatus = "Auto-update: checking version..."
	}
	ch := make(chan updateResult, 1)
	app.UpdateCh = ch
	go func() {
		feed, err := readUpdateFeedURL()
		if err != nil {
			msg := "Автообновление не настроено: добавь HTTPS-ссылку на update.json в разделе «Настройки»."
			if language == "en" {
				msg = "Auto-update is not configured: add an HTTPS update.json URL in Settings."
			}
			ch <- updateResult{Status: msg}
			return
		}
		manifest, err := fetchUpdateManifest(feed)
		if err != nil {
			msg := "Не удалось проверить обновления: " + err.Error()
			if language == "en" {
				msg = "Update check failed: " + err.Error()
			}
			ch <- updateResult{Status: msg}
			return
		}
		if isNewerVersion(manifest.Version, appVersion) {
			msg := "Найдена новая версия " + manifest.Version + ". Начинаем автоматическое обновление."
			if language == "en" {
				msg = "Version " + manifest.Version + " found. Starting automatic update."
			}
			ch <- updateResult{Available: true, Manifest: manifest, Status: msg}
			return
		}
		msg := "Установлена последняя версия " + appVersion + "."
		if language == "en" {
			msg = "You have the latest version " + appVersion + "."
		}
		ch <- updateResult{Status: msg}
	}()
}

func startUpdateInstall() {
	if app.UpdateInstalling || app.UpdateURL == "" || app.UpdateSHA256 == "" {
		app.UpdateStatus = "Сейчас обновление недоступно или уже выполняется."
		invalidate()
		return
	}
	app.UpdateInstalling = true
	msg := "Скачивание и проверка обновления..."
	if app.Language == "en" {
		msg = "Downloading and verifying update..."
	}
	app.UpdateStatus = msg
	ch := make(chan updateResult, 1)
	app.UpdateCh = ch
	manifest := updateManifest{Version: app.UpdateVersion, DownloadURL: app.UpdateURL, SHA256: app.UpdateSHA256, Notes: app.UpdateNotes}
	language := app.Language
	go func() {
		result := downloadAndStageUpdate(manifest, language)
		ch <- result
	}()
	invalidate()
}

func downloadAndStageUpdate(manifest updateManifest, language string) updateResult {
	client := &http.Client{Timeout: 2 * time.Minute, CheckRedirect: httpsOnlyRedirect}
	req, err := http.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, manifest.DownloadURL, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return updateResult{Status: "Update download failed: " + err.Error()}
	}
	req.Header.Set("User-Agent", "VOID-Optimizer-Updater/"+appVersion)
	resp, err := client.Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		return updateResult{Status: "Update download failed: " + err.Error()}
	}
	defer resp.Body.Close()
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		return updateResult{Status: fmt.Sprintf("Update server returned HTTP %d", resp.StatusCode)}
	}
	if resp.ContentLength > 120*1024*1024 {
		return updateResult{Status: "Update file exceeds the 120 MB safety limit."}
	}
	target, err := os.Executable()
	if err != nil {
		return updateResult{Status: "Cannot locate current EXE: " + err.Error()}
	}
	target, err = filepath.Abs(target)
	if err != nil {
		return updateResult{Status: "Cannot resolve current EXE path: " + err.Error()}
	}
	tmp, err := os.CreateTemp(filepath.Dir(target), ".void-update-*.exe")
	if err != nil {
		return updateResult{Status: "Cannot create update file beside the EXE: " + err.Error()}
	}
	tmpPath := tmp.Name()
	cleanup := func() { _ = tmp.Close(); _ = os.Remove(tmpPath) }
	h := sha256.New()
	n, copyErr := io.Copy(io.MultiWriter(tmp, h), io.LimitReader(resp.Body, 120*1024*1024+1))
	if copyErr != nil {
		cleanup()
		return updateResult{Status: "Update download failed: " + copyErr.Error()}
	}
	if n < 2 || n > 120*1024*1024 {
		cleanup()
		return updateResult{Status: "Update file size is invalid or exceeds 120 MB."}
	}
	if err := tmp.Sync(); err != nil {
		cleanup()
		return updateResult{Status: "Cannot flush downloaded update: " + err.Error()}
	}
	if err := tmp.Close(); err != nil {
		_ = os.Remove(tmpPath)
		return updateResult{Status: "Cannot close downloaded update: " + err.Error()}
	}
	if !strings.EqualFold(hex.EncodeToString(h.Sum(nil)), manifest.SHA256) {
		_ = os.Remove(tmpPath)
		return updateResult{Status: "Update SHA-256 verification failed. The current app was not changed."}
	}
	f, err := os.Open(tmpPath)
	if err != nil {
		_ = os.Remove(tmpPath)
		return updateResult{Status: "Cannot verify update executable: " + err.Error()}
	}
	var signature [2]byte
	_, err = f.Read(signature[:])
	_ = f.Close()
	if err != nil || signature != [2]byte{'M', 'Z'} {
		_ = os.Remove(tmpPath)
		return updateResult{Status: "Downloaded file is not a Windows executable."}
	}
	backup := target + ".previous.exe"
	script, err := os.CreateTemp(os.TempDir(), "void-update-*.ps1")
	if err != nil {
		_ = os.Remove(tmpPath)
		return updateResult{Status: "Cannot create updater script: " + err.Error()}
	}
	psLiteral := func(value string) string { return "'" + strings.ReplaceAll(value, "'", "''") + "'" }
	body := fmt.Sprintf(`$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$waitForPid = %d
$target = %s
$newFile = %s
$backup = %s
$deadline = [DateTime]::UtcNow.AddSeconds(120)
$updated = $false
try {
    while (Get-Process -Id $waitForPid -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue) {
        if ([DateTime]::UtcNow -ge $deadline) { throw 'Timed out waiting for the app to close.' }
        Start-Sleep -Seconds 1
    }
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $target -PathType Leaf)) { throw 'Current executable was not found.' }
    Copy-Item -LiteralPath $target -Destination $backup -Force
    try {
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $newFile -Destination $target -Force
        Start-Process -FilePath $target
        $updated = $true
    } catch {
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $backup -Destination $target -Force
        throw
    }
} catch {
    if (-not $updated -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath $backup -PathType Leaf)) {
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $backup -Destination $target -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $target -PathType Leaf) { Start-Process -FilePath $target }
    }
} finally {
    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $newFile -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $PSCommandPath -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
}
`, os.Getpid(), psLiteral(target), psLiteral(tmpPath), psLiteral(backup))
	if _, err := script.WriteString(body); err != nil {
		_ = script.Close()
		_ = os.Remove(script.Name())
		_ = os.Remove(tmpPath)
		return updateResult{Status: "Cannot write updater script: " + err.Error()}
	}
	scriptPath := script.Name()
	if err := script.Close(); err != nil {
		_ = os.Remove(scriptPath)
		_ = os.Remove(tmpPath)
		return updateResult{Status: "Cannot finish updater script: " + err.Error()}
	}
	cmd := exec.Command(systemCommandPath("powershell.exe"), "-NoLogo", "-NoProfile", "-NonInteractive", "-ExecutionPolicy", "Bypass", "-WindowStyle", "Hidden", "-File", scriptPath)
	cmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{HideWindow: true}
	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
		_ = os.Remove(scriptPath)
		_ = os.Remove(tmpPath)
		return updateResult{Status: "Cannot launch updater: " + err.Error()}
	}
	msg := "Обновление проверено по SHA-256. VOID закроется, установщик заменит EXE и запустит новую версию. Резервная копия сохранится рядом."
	if language == "en" {
		msg = "SHA-256 verified. VOID will close, replace the EXE and launch the new version. A backup will be kept beside it."
	}
	return updateResult{Status: msg, InstallReady: true}
}

func clampInt(value, minValue, maxValue int) int {
	if value < minValue {
		return minValue
	}
	if value > maxValue {
		return maxValue
	}
	return value
}

func tweakScrollMax() int {
	// Single-column readable list + warning, status and rollback controls.
	contentBottom := 150 + len(tweakCardCatalog())*58 + 184 + 42
	return maxInt(0, contentBottom-822)
}

func bindScrollMax() int {
	contentBottom := 205 + len(rustUtilityBindSpecs())*68 + 112
	return maxInt(0, contentBottom-822)
}

func drawScrollBar(hdc uintptr, x, top, bottom, offset, maxOffset int) {
	if maxOffset <= 0 {
		return
	}
	fill(hdc, rect{int32(x), int32(top), int32(x + 3), int32(bottom)}, uint32(colCardLine))
	track := bottom - top
	thumbHeight := track * track / (track + maxOffset)
	if thumbHeight < 36 {
		thumbHeight = 36
	}
	thumbTop := top + (track-thumbHeight)*offset/maxOffset
	fill(hdc, rect{int32(x - 1), int32(thumbTop), int32(x + 4), int32(thumbTop + thumbHeight)}, uint32(colPurple))
}

func rustKeyFromVirtualKey(vk uint32) string {
	switch {
	case vk >= 'A' && vk <= 'Z':
		return strings.ToLower(string(rune(vk)))
	case vk >= '0' && vk <= '9':
		return string(rune(vk))
	case vk >= 0x70 && vk <= 0x7B:
		return fmt.Sprintf("f%d", vk-0x6F)
	}
	switch vk {
	case 0x20:
		return "space"
	case 0x09:
		return "tab"
	case 0x14:
		return "capslock"
	case 0x12:
		return "leftalt"
	case 0x10:
		return "leftshift"
	case 0x11:
		return "leftctrl"
	case 0xA4:
		return "leftalt"
	case 0xA5:
		return "rightalt"
	case 0xA0:
		return "leftshift"
	case 0xA1:
		return "rightshift"
	case 0xA2:
		return "leftctrl"
	case 0xA3:
		return "rightctrl"
	case 0x24:
		return "home"
	case 0x23:
		return "end"
	case 0x2D:
		return "insert"
	case 0x2E:
		return "delete"
	}
	return ""
}

func assignRustBindKey(id, key string) {
	key = strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(key))
	candidate := normalizedRustBindKeys(app.BindKeys)
	if _, exists := candidate[id]; !exists {
		app.CapturingBind = ""
		return
	}
	for otherID, otherKey := range candidate {
		if otherID != id && strings.EqualFold(otherKey, key) {
			app.Status = "Клавиша " + strings.ToUpper(key) + " уже назначена для «" + otherID + ". Выбери другую."
			app.CapturingBind = ""
			showButtons()
			return
		}
	}
	previous := candidate[id]
	candidate[id] = key
	app.CapturingBind = ""
	app.BindKeys = candidate
	app.CustomLootKey = candidate["loot"]
	app.ViewmodelKey = candidate["viewmodel"]
	if err := savePrefs(); err != nil {
		app.BindKeys[id] = previous
		app.CustomLootKey = app.BindKeys["loot"]
		app.ViewmodelKey = app.BindKeys["viewmodel"]
		app.Status = "Не удалось сохранить клавишу: " + err.Error()
	} else {
		app.Status = "Сохранено: " + strings.ToUpper(key) + ". Нажми «Установить бинды» после настройки всех клавиш."
		if app.Language == "en" {
			app.Status = "Saved: " + strings.ToUpper(key) + ". Press Apply Binds when all keys are configured."
		}
	}
	showButtons()
}

func prefsPath() string {
	return filepath.Join(appDataRoot(), "VOID Optimizer", "settings.json")
}
func tweaksBackupPath() string {
	return filepath.Join(appDataRoot(), "VOID Optimizer", "tweaks-backup.json")
}
func loadPrefs() {
	b, err := os.ReadFile(prefsPath())
	if err != nil {
		return
	}
	var p prefsState
	if json.Unmarshal(b, &p) != nil {
		return
	}
	if p.Language == "ru" || p.Language == "en" {
		app.Language = p.Language
	}
	if p.Theme == "purple" || p.Theme == "blue" {
		app.Theme = p.Theme
	}
	app.Login = strings.TrimSpace(p.Login)
	app.UpdateFeedURL = strings.TrimSpace(p.UpdateFeedURL)
	app.RustTrackedIDs = normalizeSteamIDs(p.RustTrackedIDs)
	app.BindKeys = normalizedRustBindKeys(p.BindKeys)
	// Migrate key customizations saved by releases before the per-bind editor.
	if p.BindKeys == nil {
		if validRustCustomKey(p.CustomLootKey) {
			app.BindKeys["loot"] = strings.ToLower(p.CustomLootKey)
		}
		if validRustCustomKey(p.ViewmodelKey) {
			app.BindKeys["viewmodel"] = strings.ToLower(p.ViewmodelKey)
		}
	}
	app.CustomLootKey = app.BindKeys["loot"]
	app.ViewmodelKey = app.BindKeys["viewmodel"]
}
func savePrefs() error {
	keys := normalizedRustBindKeys(app.BindKeys)
	b, err := json.MarshalIndent(prefsState{Language: app.Language, Theme: app.Theme, Login: strings.TrimSpace(app.Login), UpdateFeedURL: strings.TrimSpace(app.UpdateFeedURL), CustomLootKey: strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(keys["loot"])), ViewmodelKey: strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(keys["viewmodel"])), BindKeys: keys, RustTrackedIDs: normalizeSteamIDs(app.RustTrackedIDs)}, "", "  ")
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := writeFileAtomic(prefsPath(), b, 0600); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	// Verify that the persisted identity can be read back before dismissing first-run.
	check, err := os.ReadFile(prefsPath())
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	var saved prefsState
	if err := json.Unmarshal(check, &saved); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if strings.TrimSpace(saved.Login) != strings.TrimSpace(app.Login) {
		return fmt.Errorf("saved login did not verify")
	}
	if strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(saved.ViewmodelKey)) != strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(app.ViewmodelKey)) {
		return fmt.Errorf("saved viewmodel key did not verify")
	}
	for id, key := range keys {
		if strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(saved.BindKeys[id])) != key {
			return fmt.Errorf("saved bind key %s did not verify", id)
		}
	}
	return nil
}
func applyTheme() {
	if app.Theme == "blue" {
		colBg, colSidebar, colCard, colCardLine = 0x21110A, 0x2B160D, 0x302018, 0x8A5735
		colPurple, colPurple2 = 0xFFB36A, 0xD98A42 // COLORREF BGR: vivid arctic blue
		colText, colMuted = 0xF3F7FF, 0xAAB8D2
		colGreen, colOrange = 0x75D6A0, 0x55A9FF
	} else {
		colBg, colSidebar, colCard, colCardLine = 0x180A12, 0x24101B, 0x2D1725, 0x694078
		colPurple, colPurple2 = 0xFF5B9D, 0xBE3666
		colText, colMuted = 0xFAEDF1, 0xB99AA4
		colGreen, colOrange = 0x75D6A0, 0x55A9FF
	}
	selectThemeCursor()
}
func languageButtonLabel() string {
	if app.Language == "ru" {
		return "ЯЗЫК: РУССКИЙ → ENGLISH"
	}
	return "LANGUAGE: ENGLISH → RUSSIAN"
}
func themeButtonLabel() string {
	if app.Language == "en" {
		if app.Theme == "purple" {
			return "THEME: VOID PURPLE → ARCTIC BLUE"
		}
		return "THEME: ARCTIC BLUE → VOID PURPLE"
	}
	if app.Theme == "purple" {
		return "ТЕМА: VOID PURPLE → ARCTIC BLUE"
	}
	return "ТЕМА: ARCTIC BLUE → VOID PURPLE"
}

func changeKey(c regChange) string { return c.Key + "|" + c.Name }
func changesForTweak(id int) []regChange {
	switch id {
	case idTweakGame:
		return []regChange{{`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\GameBar`, "AutoGameModeEnabled", "1"}, {`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\GameBar`, "AllowAutoGameMode", "1"}}
	case idTweakDVR:
		return []regChange{{`HKCU\System\GameConfigStore`, "GameDVR_Enabled", "0"}, {`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\GameDVR`, "AppCaptureEnabled", "0"}, {`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\GameDVR`, "AudioCaptureEnabled", "0"}, {`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\GameDVR`, "CursorCaptureEnabled", "0"}, {`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\GameDVR`, "HistoricalCaptureEnabled", "0"}}
	case idTweakTrans:
		return []regChange{{`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Themes\Personalize`, "EnableTransparency", "0"}}
	case idTweakFX:
		return []regChange{{`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Explorer\VisualEffects`, "VisualFXSetting", "2"}}
	case idTweakAnim:
		return []regChange{{`HKCU\Control Panel\Desktop\WindowMetrics`, "MinAnimate", "0"}}
	case idTweakMenu:
		return []regChange{{`HKCU\Control Panel\Desktop`, "MenuShowDelay", "0"}}
	case idTweakOverlay:
		return []regChange{{`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\GameBar`, "GameBarEnabled", "0"}, {`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\GameBar`, "ShowStartupPanel", "0"}}
	case idTweakRustGPU:
		return rustGPUPreferenceChanges()
	case idTweakMouseAccel:
		return []regChange{{`HKCU\Control Panel\Mouse`, "MouseSpeed", "0"}, {`HKCU\Control Panel\Mouse`, "MouseThreshold1", "0"}, {`HKCU\Control Panel\Mouse`, "MouseThreshold2", "0"}}
	case idTweakTaskbarAnim:
		return []regChange{{`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Explorer\Advanced`, "TaskbarAnimations", "0"}}
	case idTweakAeroPeek:
		return []regChange{{`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\DWM`, "EnableAeroPeek", "0"}}
	case idTweakListAlpha:
		return []regChange{{`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Explorer\Advanced`, "ListviewAlphaSelect", "0"}}
	case idTweakDragFull:
		return []regChange{{`HKCU\Control Panel\Desktop`, "DragFullWindows", "0"}}
	case idTweakExplorerThumbs:
		return []regChange{{`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Explorer\Advanced`, "IconsOnly", "1"}}
	case idTweakWidgets:
		return []regChange{{`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Explorer\Advanced`, "TaskbarDa", "0"}}
	case idTweakTaskView:
		return []regChange{{`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Explorer\Advanced`, "ShowTaskViewButton", "0"}}
	case idTweakTaskbarSearch:
		return []regChange{{`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Explorer\Advanced`, "SearchboxTaskbarMode", "0"}}
	case idTweakStartRecs:
		return []regChange{{`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\ContentDeliveryManager`, "SystemPaneSuggestionsEnabled", "0"}, {`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\ContentDeliveryManager`, "SubscribedContent-338388Enabled", "0"}, {`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\ContentDeliveryManager`, "SubscribedContent-338389Enabled", "0"}}
	case idTweakStartupDelay:
		return []regChange{{`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Explorer\Serialize`, "StartupDelayInMSec", "0"}}
	case idTweakRecentFiles:
		return []regChange{{`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Explorer\Advanced`, "ShowRecent", "0"}, {`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Explorer\Advanced`, "ShowFrequent", "0"}}
	case idTweakBackgroundApps:
		return []regChange{{`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\BackgroundAccessApplications`, "GlobalUserDisabled", "1"}}
	case idTweakSearchHighlights:
		return []regChange{{`HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\SearchSettings`, "IsDynamicSearchBoxEnabled", "0"}}
	case idTweakSearchSuggestions:
		return []regChange{{`HKCU\Software\Policies\Microsoft\Windows\Explorer`, "DisableSearchBoxSuggestions", "1"}}
	}
	return nil
}
func rustGPUPreferenceChanges() []regChange {
	const key = `HKCU\Software\Microsoft\DirectX\UserGpuPreferences`
	// Prefer the path stored in the rollback journal. Steam libraries can move;
	// restoring the exact old registry value is more important than current discovery.
	prefix := strings.ToUpper(key + "|")
	for savedKey := range app.TweaksBackup {
		if strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToUpper(savedKey), prefix) {
			parts := strings.SplitN(savedKey, "|", 2)
			if len(parts) == 2 && strings.EqualFold(filepath.Base(parts[1]), "RustClient.exe") {
				return []regChange{{key, parts[1], "GpuPreference=2;"}}
			}
		}
	}
	folder := strings.TrimSpace(app.RustFolder)
	if folder == "" {
		folder = findRust()
		app.RustFolder = folder
	}
	if folder != "" {
		exe := filepath.Join(folder, "RustClient.exe")
		if _, err := os.Stat(exe); err == nil {
			return []regChange{{key, exe, "GpuPreference=2;"}}
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func regValueStrict(key, name string) (regSnapshot, error) {
	// All current VOID tweaks are under HKCU. Restrict the reader to this hive
	// instead of parsing locale-dependent `reg.exe` text output.
	const prefix = `HKCU\`
	if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToUpper(key), prefix) {
		return regSnapshot{}, fmt.Errorf("unsupported registry hive: %s", key)
	}
	subkey := key[len(prefix):]
	if subkey == "" {
		return regSnapshot{}, fmt.Errorf("empty registry subkey")
	}
	subkeyPtr, err := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString(subkey)
	if err != nil {
		return regSnapshot{}, err
	}
	var h syscall.Handle
	openErr := syscall.RegOpenKeyEx(syscall.Handle(syscall.HKEY_CURRENT_USER), subkeyPtr, 0, syscall.KEY_QUERY_VALUE, &h)
	runtime.KeepAlive(subkeyPtr)
	if err := openErr; err != nil {
		if err == syscall.ERROR_FILE_NOT_FOUND {
			return regSnapshot{}, nil
		}
		return regSnapshot{}, fmt.Errorf("open registry key %s: %w", key, err)
	}
	defer syscall.RegCloseKey(h)

	valuePtr, err := syscall.UTF16PtrFromString(name)
	if err != nil {
		return regSnapshot{}, err
	}
	var valueType uint32
	var size uint32
	queryErr := syscall.RegQueryValueEx(h, valuePtr, nil, &valueType, nil, &size)
	runtime.KeepAlive(valuePtr)
	if err := queryErr; err != nil {
		if err == syscall.ERROR_FILE_NOT_FOUND {
			return regSnapshot{}, nil
		}
		if err != syscall.ERROR_MORE_DATA {
			return regSnapshot{}, fmt.Errorf("query registry value %s: %w", name, err)
		}
	}
	var data []byte
	for attempt := 0; attempt < 3; attempt++ {
		data = make([]byte, size)
		var dataPtr *byte
		if len(data) > 0 {
			dataPtr = &data[0]
		}
		err = syscall.RegQueryValueEx(h, valuePtr, nil, &valueType, dataPtr, &size)
		runtime.KeepAlive(valuePtr)
		runtime.KeepAlive(data)
		if err == nil {
			data = data[:size]
			break
		}
		if err != syscall.ERROR_MORE_DATA {
			if err == syscall.ERROR_FILE_NOT_FOUND {
				return regSnapshot{}, nil
			}
			return regSnapshot{}, fmt.Errorf("read registry value %s: %w", name, err)
		}
		if attempt == 2 {
			return regSnapshot{}, fmt.Errorf("registry value %s kept changing while it was read", name)
		}
	}

	value := ""
	switch valueType {
	case syscall.REG_DWORD:
		if len(data) != 4 {
			return regSnapshot{}, fmt.Errorf("registry DWORD %s has invalid size %d", name, len(data))
		}
		value = strconv.FormatUint(uint64(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(data)), 10)
	case syscall.REG_QWORD:
		if len(data) != 8 {
			return regSnapshot{}, fmt.Errorf("registry QWORD %s has invalid size %d", name, len(data))
		}
		value = strconv.FormatUint(binary.LittleEndian.Uint64(data), 10)
	case syscall.REG_SZ, syscall.REG_EXPAND_SZ:
		if len(data)%2 != 0 {
			return regSnapshot{}, fmt.Errorf("registry string %s has invalid UTF-16 size", name)
		}
		words := make([]uint16, len(data)/2)
		for i := range words {
			words[i] = binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(data[i*2 : i*2+2])
		}
		value = syscall.UTF16ToString(words)
	case syscall.REG_BINARY:
		return regSnapshot{Exists: true, Type: "REG_BINARY"}, nil
	default:
		return regSnapshot{Exists: true, Type: fmt.Sprintf("REG_TYPE_%d", valueType)}, nil
	}
	return regSnapshot{Exists: true, Type: registryTypeName(valueType), Value: value}, nil
}

func registryTypeName(t uint32) string {
	switch t {
	case syscall.REG_DWORD:
		return "REG_DWORD"
	case syscall.REG_QWORD:
		return "REG_QWORD"
	case syscall.REG_SZ:
		return "REG_SZ"
	case syscall.REG_EXPAND_SZ:
		return "REG_EXPAND_SZ"
	default:
		return fmt.Sprintf("REG_TYPE_%d", t)
	}
}

func regValue(key, name string) regSnapshot {
	value, err := regValueStrict(key, name)
	if err != nil {
		return regSnapshot{}
	}
	return value
}
func regMatches(c regChange) bool {
	r := regValue(c.Key, c.Name)
	wantType := "REG_DWORD"
	if c.Name == "MinAnimate" || c.Name == "MenuShowDelay" || c.Name == "MouseSpeed" || c.Name == "MouseThreshold1" || c.Name == "MouseThreshold2" || c.Name == "DragFullWindows" || strings.EqualFold(filepath.Base(c.Name), "RustClient.exe") {
		wantType = "REG_SZ"
	}
	if !r.Exists || !strings.EqualFold(r.Type, wantType) {
		return false
	}
	return r.Value == c.Desired
}
func tweakEnabled(id int) bool {
	changes := changesForTweak(id)
	if len(changes) == 0 {
		return false
	}
	for _, c := range changes {
		if !regMatches(c) {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}
func tweakButtonLabel(id int) string {
	if id == idTweakRustGPU && len(changesForTweak(id)) == 0 {
		if app.Language == "en" {
			return "RUST NOT FOUND"
		}
		return "RUST НЕ НАЙДЕН"
	}
	if tweakEnabled(id) {
		hasBackup := true
		for _, c := range changesForTweak(id) {
			if _, ok := app.TweaksBackup[changeKey(c)]; !ok {
				hasBackup = false
			}
		}
		if app.Language == "en" {
			if hasBackup {
				return "RESTORE"
			}
			return "ALREADY ON"
		}
		if hasBackup {
			return "ОТКАТИТЬ"
		}
		return "УЖЕ ВКЛЮЧЕНО"
	}
	if app.Language == "en" {
		return "ENABLE"
	}
	return "ВКЛЮЧИТЬ"
}
func validRegistrySnapshot(snap regSnapshot) bool {
	if !snap.Exists {
		return true
	}
	switch strings.ToUpper(snap.Type) {
	case "REG_DWORD":
		_, err := strconv.ParseUint(snap.Value, 10, 32)
		return err == nil
	case "REG_QWORD":
		_, err := strconv.ParseUint(snap.Value, 10, 64)
		return err == nil
	case "REG_SZ", "REG_EXPAND_SZ":
		return len(snap.Value) <= 4096 && !strings.ContainsRune(snap.Value, '\x00')
	default:
		return false
	}
}

func loadTweaksBackup() {
	app.TweaksBackup = map[string]regSnapshot{}
	app.TweaksBackupError = ""
	b, err := os.ReadFile(tweaksBackupPath())
	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
		return
	}
	if err != nil {
		app.TweaksBackupError = "Не удалось прочитать файл резервных копий твиков: " + err.Error()
		return
	}
	var values map[string]regSnapshot
	if err := json.Unmarshal(b, &values); err != nil || values == nil {
		app.TweaksBackupError = "Файл резервных копий твиков повреждён. Изменение реестра заблокировано для безопасности."
		return
	}
	for key, snap := range values {
		if !allowedTweaksBackupKey(key) {
			app.TweaksBackupError = "В backup найден неизвестный параметр реестра. Изменение реестра заблокировано."
			return
		}
		if !validRegistrySnapshot(snap) {
			app.TweaksBackupError = "В backup найден неподдерживаемый или некорректный тип/значение реестра. Изменение реестра заблокировано."
			return
		}
	}
	app.TweaksBackup = values
}
func allowedTweaksBackupKey(key string) bool {
	for _, id := range []int{idTweakGame, idTweakDVR, idTweakTrans, idTweakFX, idTweakAnim, idTweakMenu, idTweakOverlay, idTweakMouseAccel, idTweakTaskbarAnim, idTweakAeroPeek, idTweakListAlpha, idTweakDragFull, idTweakExplorerThumbs, idTweakWidgets, idTweakTaskView, idTweakTaskbarSearch, idTweakStartRecs, idTweakStartupDelay, idTweakRecentFiles, idTweakBackgroundApps, idTweakSearchHighlights, idTweakSearchSuggestions} {
		for _, change := range changesForTweak(id) {
			if strings.EqualFold(key, changeKey(change)) {
				return true
			}
		}
	}
	const gpuPrefix = `HKCU\Software\Microsoft\DirectX\UserGpuPreferences|`
	if strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToUpper(key), strings.ToUpper(gpuPrefix)) {
		parts := strings.SplitN(key, "|", 2)
		return len(parts) == 2 && strings.EqualFold(filepath.Base(parts[1]), "RustClient.exe")
	}
	return false
}
func saveTweaksBackupMap(values map[string]regSnapshot) error {
	b, err := json.MarshalIndent(values, "", "  ")
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return writeFileAtomic(tweaksBackupPath(), b, 0600)
}
func cloneTweaksBackup(src map[string]regSnapshot) map[string]regSnapshot {
	dst := make(map[string]regSnapshot, len(src))
	for k, v := range src {
		dst[k] = v
	}
	return dst
}
func setRegChange(c regChange) error {
	typ := "REG_DWORD"
	if c.Name == "MinAnimate" || c.Name == "MenuShowDelay" || c.Name == "MouseSpeed" || c.Name == "MouseThreshold1" || c.Name == "MouseThreshold2" || c.Name == "DragFullWindows" || strings.EqualFold(filepath.Base(c.Name), "RustClient.exe") {
		typ = "REG_SZ"
	}
	_, err := runHidden("reg.exe", "add", c.Key, "/v", c.Name, "/t", typ, "/d", c.Desired, "/f")
	return err
}
func restoreReg(key, name string, snap regSnapshot) error {
	if !snap.Exists {
		_, err := runHidden("reg.exe", "delete", key, "/v", name, "/f")
		if err != nil {
			// Treat an already-absent value as success, but never hide a read error.
			current, readErr := regValueStrict(key, name)
			if readErr != nil {
				return readErr
			}
			if !current.Exists {
				return nil
			}
		}
		return err
	}
	_, err := runHidden("reg.exe", "add", key, "/v", name, "/t", snap.Type, "/d", snap.Value, "/f")
	return err
}
func applyTweak(id int) error {
	if app.TweaksBackupError != "" {
		return fmt.Errorf("%s", app.TweaksBackupError)
	}
	changes := changesForTweak(id)
	if len(changes) == 0 {
		return fmt.Errorf("для этого твика не найден подходящий компонент")
	}
	if tweakEnabled(id) {
		return nil
	}
	next := cloneTweaksBackup(app.TweaksBackup)
	for _, c := range changes {
		k := changeKey(c)
		if _, ok := next[k]; ok {
			continue
		}
		snapshot, err := regValueStrict(c.Key, c.Name)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("не удалось прочитать %s: %w", c.Name, err)
		}
		if !validRegistrySnapshot(snapshot) {
			return fmt.Errorf("исходное значение %s имеет неподдерживаемый тип", c.Name)
		}
		next[k] = snapshot
	}
	if err := saveTweaksBackupMap(next); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("не удалось сохранить резервную копию: %w", err)
	}
	app.TweaksBackup = next
	app.TweaksBackupError = ""
	for _, c := range changes {
		if err := setRegChange(c); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("ошибка применения %s: %w; резервная копия сохранена", c.Name, err)
		}
	}
	if !tweakEnabled(id) {
		return fmt.Errorf("Windows не подтвердил применение параметра")
	}
	return nil
}

func toggleTweak(id int) {
	if app.TweaksBackupError != "" {
		app.Status = app.TweaksBackupError
		return
	}
	changes := changesForTweak(id)
	if len(changes) == 0 {
		if app.Language == "en" {
			app.Status = "Rust was not detected. Select the Rust folder first."
		} else {
			app.Status = "Rust не найден. Сначала выбери папку Rust."
		}
		return
	}
	if tweakEnabled(id) {
		for _, c := range changes {
			if _, ok := app.TweaksBackup[changeKey(c)]; !ok {
				app.Status = "Параметр уже был включён до VOID; исходное значение неизвестно, поэтому откат заблокирован."
				return
			}
		}
		completed := []regChange{}
		for _, c := range changes {
			if err := restoreReg(c.Key, c.Name, app.TweaksBackup[changeKey(c)]); err != nil {
				for _, done := range completed {
					_ = setRegChange(done)
				}
				app.Status = "Не удалось восстановить " + c.Name + ": " + err.Error()
				return
			}
			completed = append(completed, c)
		}
		next := cloneTweaksBackup(app.TweaksBackup)
		for _, c := range changes {
			delete(next, changeKey(c))
		}
		if err := saveTweaksBackupMap(next); err != nil {
			app.Status = "Параметры восстановлены, но backup не обновлён: " + err.Error()
			return
		}
		app.TweaksBackup = next
		app.Status = "Твик отключён. Исходные значения восстановлены."
		return
	}
	if err := applyTweak(id); err != nil {
		app.Status = "Твик не применён: " + err.Error()
		return
	}
	app.Status = "Твик включён. Исходные значения сохранены для отката."
}

func restoreAllTweaks() {
	if app.TweaksBackupError != "" {
		app.Status = app.TweaksBackupError
		return
	}
	if len(app.TweaksBackup) == 0 {
		app.Status = "Нет сохранённых твиков для отката."
		return
	}
	failed := 0
	for k, snap := range app.TweaksBackup {
		parts := strings.SplitN(k, "|", 2)
		if len(parts) != 2 {
			failed++
			continue
		}
		if err := restoreReg(parts[0], parts[1], snap); err != nil {
			failed++
		}
	}
	if failed > 0 {
		app.Status = fmt.Sprintf("Не удалось восстановить параметров: %d. Backup сохранён для повторной попытки.", failed)
		return
	}
	if err := saveTweaksBackupMap(map[string]regSnapshot{}); err != nil {
		app.Status = "Твики восстановлены, но файл backup не удалось очистить: " + err.Error()
		return
	}
	app.TweaksBackup = map[string]regSnapshot{}
	app.TweaksBackupError = ""
	app.Status = "Все твики VOID восстановлены."
}

func refreshMetrics() {
	var idle, kernel, user fileTime
	r, _, _ := pGetSystemTimes.Call(uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&idle)), uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&kernel)), uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&user)))
	if r != 0 {
		i := ft64(idle)
		k := ft64(kernel)
		u := ft64(user)
		if app.HasCPUSample {
			di := i - app.LastIdle
			dt := (k - app.LastKernel) + (u - app.LastUser)
			if dt > 0 {
				v := 100.0 * float64(dt-di) / float64(dt)
				app.CPUPercent = fmt.Sprintf("%.0f%%", math.Max(0, math.Min(100, v)))
			}
		}
		app.LastIdle = i
		app.LastKernel = k
		app.LastUser = u
		app.HasCPUSample = true
	}
	var m memoryStatusEx
	m.Length = uint32(unsafe.Sizeof(m))
	r, _, _ = pGlobalMemoryStatusEx.Call(uintptr(unsafe.Pointer(&m)))
	if r != 0 {
		app.RAMPercent = fmt.Sprintf("%d%%", m.MemoryLoad)
	}
}
func ft64(ft fileTime) uint64 { return uint64(ft.High)<<32 | uint64(ft.Low) }

func systemCommandPath(name string) string {
	root := os.Getenv("SystemRoot")
	if root == "" {
		root = os.Getenv("WINDIR")
	}
	if root != "" {
		var candidate string
		switch strings.ToLower(name) {
		case "powershell.exe":
			candidate = filepath.Join(root, "System32", "WindowsPowerShell", "v1.0", name)
		case "explorer.exe":
			candidate = filepath.Join(root, name)
		default:
			candidate = filepath.Join(root, "System32", name)
		}
		if info, err := os.Stat(candidate); err == nil && !info.IsDir() {
			return candidate
		}
	}
	return name
}

func runHidden(name string, args ...string) (string, error) {
	cmd := exec.Command(systemCommandPath(name), args...)
	cmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{HideWindow: true, CreationFlags: 0x08000000}
	out, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
	return string(out), err
}

// Package main contains Rust keybind-file helpers shared with tests.
package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"strings"
)

type rustBindSpec struct {
	ID         string
	DefaultKey string
	TitleRU    string
	DescRU     string
	TitleEN    string
	DescEN     string
	Command    string
}

func rustUtilityBindSpecs() []rustBindSpec {
	return []rustBindSpec{
		{ID: "autorun", DefaultKey: "q", TitleRU: "АВТОБЕГ", DescRU: "Вперёд с бегом; W отменяет движение.", TitleEN: "AUTO-RUN", DescEN: "Move forward while sprinting; W cancels movement.", Command: "forward;sprint"},
		{ID: "crouch", DefaultKey: "z", TitleRU: "ПРИСЕДАНИЕ", DescRU: "Переключатель приседания.", TitleEN: "CROUCH", DescEN: "Crouch toggle.", Command: "duck"},
		{ID: "jumpcrouch", DefaultKey: "n", TitleRU: "ПРЫЖОК + ПРИСЕД", DescRU: "Прыжок и приседание при удержании.", TitleEN: "JUMP + CROUCH", DescEN: "Jump and crouch while held.", Command: "+jump;+duck"},
		{ID: "loot", DefaultKey: "mouse3", TitleRU: "БЫСТРЫЙ ЛУТ", DescRU: "Удерживай и веди курсор по ячейкам инвентаря.", TitleEN: "QUICK LOOT", DescEN: "Hold while moving the cursor across inventory slots.", Command: "+hoverloot"},
		{ID: "voice", DefaultKey: "mouse5", TitleRU: "ГОЛОСОВОЙ ЧАТ", DescRU: "Голосовой чат при удержании.", TitleEN: "VOICE CHAT", DescEN: "Proximity voice while held.", Command: "+voice"},
		{ID: "console", DefaultKey: "f2", TitleRU: "КОНСОЛЬ", DescRU: "Открывает и закрывает консоль Rust.", TitleEN: "CONSOLE", DescEN: "Opens and closes the Rust console.", Command: "consoletoggle"},
		{ID: "bandage", DefaultKey: "b", TitleRU: "КРАФТ БИНТА", DescRU: "Добавляет один бинт в очередь крафта.", TitleEN: "BANDAGE CRAFT", DescEN: "Queues one bandage for crafting.", Command: `"craft.add -2072273936 1"`},
		{ID: "quickalt", DefaultKey: "h", TitleRU: "БЫСТРЫЙ ALT", DescRU: "Изменяет плавность возврата камеры через client.headlerp.", TitleEN: "QUICK ALT", DescEN: "Changes camera-return interpolation via client.headlerp.", Command: `"client.headlerp 100"`},
		{ID: "radius", DefaultKey: "l", TitleRU: "РАДИУС ВЗАИМОДЕЙСТВИЯ", DescRU: "Переключает client.lookatradius между 0, 0.2 и 10.", TitleEN: "INTERACTION RADIUS", DescEN: "Cycles client.lookatradius between 0, 0.2 and 10.", Command: "~client.lookatradius 0;client.lookatradius 0.2;client.lookatradius 10"},
		{ID: "viewmodel", DefaultKey: "f3", TitleRU: "МАСШТАБ РУК", DescRU: "Переключает стандартное и альтернативное отображение рук.", TitleEN: "VIEWMODEL SCALE", DescEN: "Toggles standard and alternate hand display.", Command: `"~graphics.vm_fov_scale true;graphics.vm_fov_scale false"`},
	}
}

func defaultRustBindKeys() map[string]string {
	keys := make(map[string]string, len(rustUtilityBindSpecs()))
	for _, spec := range rustUtilityBindSpecs() {
		keys[spec.ID] = spec.DefaultKey
	}
	return keys
}

func normalizedRustBindKeys(input map[string]string) map[string]string {
	keys := defaultRustBindKeys()
	for id, key := range input {
		if _, ok := keys[id]; ok && validRustCustomKey(key) {
			keys[id] = strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(key))
		}
	}
	return keys
}

func validateRustBindKeys(keys map[string]string) error {
	seen := map[string]string{}
	for _, spec := range rustUtilityBindSpecs() {
		key := strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(keys[spec.ID]))
		if !validRustCustomKey(key) {
			return fmt.Errorf("недопустимая клавиша для %s", spec.ID)
		}
		if previous, exists := seen[key]; exists {
			return fmt.Errorf("клавиша %s назначена одновременно для %s и %s", strings.ToUpper(key), previous, spec.ID)
		}
		seen[key] = spec.ID
	}
	return nil
}

func rustBindKey(line string) string {
	line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
	if line == "" || strings.HasPrefix(line, "//") || strings.HasPrefix(line, "#") {
		return ""
	}
	fields := strings.Fields(line)
	if len(fields) < 3 {
		return ""
	}
	if !strings.EqualFold(fields[0], "bind") && !strings.EqualFold(fields[0], "input.bind") {
		return ""
	}
	return strings.ToLower(strings.Trim(fields[1], "[]"))
}

func mergeRustUtilityBinds(original string) string {
	return mergeRustUtilityBindsWithMap(original, nil)
}

func mergeRustUtilityBindsWithLootKey(original, lootKey string) string {
	keys := defaultRustBindKeys()
	keys["loot"] = lootKey
	return mergeRustUtilityBindsWithMap(original, keys)
}

func mergeRustUtilityBindsWithKeys(original, lootKey, viewmodelKey string) string {
	keys := defaultRustBindKeys()
	keys["loot"] = lootKey
	keys["viewmodel"] = viewmodelKey
	return mergeRustUtilityBindsWithMap(original, keys)
}

// mergeRustUtilityBindsWithMap replaces only VOID-managed assignments. The original
// keys.cfg is backed up by the caller before this function is used.
func mergeRustUtilityBindsWithMap(original string, input map[string]string) string {
	keys := normalizedRustBindKeys(input)
	managed := map[string]bool{}
	for _, spec := range rustUtilityBindSpecs() {
		managed[spec.DefaultKey] = true // migrate legacy VOID defaults cleanly
		managed[keys[spec.ID]] = true
	}
	lines := strings.Split(strings.ReplaceAll(original, "\r\n", "\n"), "\n")
	kept := make([]string, 0, len(lines)+len(keys)+2)
	for _, line := range lines {
		trimmed := strings.TrimSpace(line)
		key := rustBindKey(line)
		lowerLine := strings.ToLower(trimmed)
		legacyMouse4Loot := key == "mouse4" && (strings.Contains(lowerLine, "hoverloot") || strings.Contains(lowerLine, "oldloot"))
		if managed[key] || legacyMouse4Loot || strings.Contains(lowerLine, "graphics.vm_fov_scale") ||
			strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, "// VOID OPTIMIZER — utility bind") ||
			strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, "// VOID OPTIMIZER — utility binds") ||
			strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, "// VOID OPTIMIZER — customizable utility binds") ||
			strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, "// VOID OPTIMIZER — viewmodel scale toggle") {
			continue
		}
		kept = append(kept, line)
	}
	for len(kept) > 0 && strings.TrimSpace(kept[len(kept)-1]) == "" {
		kept = kept[:len(kept)-1]
	}
	if len(kept) > 0 {
		kept = append(kept, "")
	}
	kept = append(kept, "// VOID OPTIMIZER — utility binds; restore with keys.cfg.void-backup")
	for _, spec := range rustUtilityBindSpecs() {
		kept = append(kept, "bind "+keys[spec.ID]+" "+spec.Command)
	}
	return strings.Join(kept, "\r\n") + "\r\n"
}

package main

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

func TestMergeRustUtilityBindsPreservesOtherKeys(t *testing.T) {
	original := "bind f1 consoletoggle\r\nbind x forward\r\nbind q oldcommand\r\nbind z oldcrouch\r\nbind mouse4 oldloot\r\ninput.bind mouse3 +buttons.hoverloot\r\n// keep this comment\r\n"
	got := mergeRustUtilityBinds(original)
	for _, keep := range []string{"bind f1 consoletoggle", "bind x forward", "// keep this comment"} {
		if !strings.Contains(got, keep) {
			t.Fatalf("unmanaged binds/comments were lost: %q", got)
		}
	}
	keys := []string{"q", "z", "n", "mouse3", "mouse5", "f2", "b", "h", "l", "f3"}
	for _, key := range keys {
		if strings.Count(strings.ToLower(got), "bind "+key+" ") != 1 {
			t.Fatalf("expected exactly one %s bind, got %q", key, got)
		}
	}
	for _, want := range []string{
		"bind q forward;sprint",
		"bind z duck",
		"bind n +jump;+duck",
		"bind mouse3 +hoverloot",
		"bind mouse5 +voice",
		"bind f2 consoletoggle",
		"bind b \"craft.add -2072273936 1\"",
		"bind h \"client.headlerp 100\"",
		"bind l ~client.lookatradius 0;client.lookatradius 0.2;client.lookatradius 10",
		"bind f3 \"~graphics.vm_fov_scale true;graphics.vm_fov_scale false\"",
	} {
		if !strings.Contains(got, want) {
			t.Fatalf("missing utility bind %q in %q", want, got)
		}
	}
	if strings.Contains(got, "bind mouse4 oldloot") {
		t.Fatalf("old VOID quick-loot assignment was not migrated: %q", got)
	}
}

func TestMergeRustUtilityBindsIsIdempotent(t *testing.T) {
	first := mergeRustUtilityBinds("bind f1 consoletoggle\n")
	second := mergeRustUtilityBinds(first)
	if first != second {
		t.Fatalf("merge should be idempotent\nfirst: %q\nsecond: %q", first, second)
	}
}

func TestMergeRustUtilityBindsReplacesPriorVOIDProfile(t *testing.T) {
	prior := "// VOID OPTIMIZER — utility binds; restore with keys.cfg.void-backup\nbind q forward;sprint\nbind z duck\nbind f1 consoletoggle\n"
	got := mergeRustUtilityBinds(prior)
	if strings.Count(got, "// VOID OPTIMIZER — utility binds") != 1 || strings.Count(got, "bind q ") != 1 || !strings.Contains(got, "bind f1 consoletoggle") {
		t.Fatalf("prior profile was not cleanly replaced: %q", got)
	}
}

func TestMergeRustUtilityBindsCustomViewmodelKey(t *testing.T) {
	got := mergeRustUtilityBindsWithKeys("bind f1 consoletoggle\nbind v +voice\nbind f4 old-viewmodel\n", "mouse3", "f4")
	if !strings.Contains(got, "bind f4 \"~graphics.vm_fov_scale true;graphics.vm_fov_scale false\"") {
		t.Fatalf("custom viewmodel key missing: %q", got)
	}
	if !strings.Contains(got, "bind v +voice") {
		t.Fatalf("unmanaged user bind should be preserved: %q", got)
	}
	if strings.Contains(got, "bind f4 old-viewmodel") {
		t.Fatalf("old assignment on the selected key should be replaced: %q", got)
	}
}

func TestRustBindKey(t *testing.T) {
	cases := []struct{ line, want string }{
		{"bind q forward;sprint", "q"},
		{"  BIND [Q] forward;sprint", "q"},
		{"bind mouse3 +hoverloot", "mouse3"},
		{"input.bind mouse3 +buttons.hoverloot", "mouse3"},
		{"INPUT.BIND [L] +something", "l"},
		{"// bind q ignored", ""},
		{"unbind q", ""},
		{"bind f1 consoletoggle", "f1"},
		{"bind f3 \"~graphics.vm_fov_scale true;graphics.vm_fov_scale false\"", "f3"},
	}
	for _, tc := range cases {
		if got := rustBindKey(tc.line); got != tc.want {
			t.Errorf("rustBindKey(%q) = %q, want %q", tc.line, got, tc.want)
		}
	}
}

func TestMergeRustUtilityBindsWithCustomKeysForEveryBind(t *testing.T) {
	keys := defaultRustBindKeys()
	keys["autorun"] = "f4"
	keys["crouch"] = "f5"
	keys["jumpcrouch"] = "f6"
	keys["loot"] = "mouse4"
	keys["voice"] = "f7"
	keys["console"] = "f8"
	keys["bandage"] = "f9"
	keys["quickalt"] = "f10"
	keys["radius"] = "f11"
	keys["viewmodel"] = "f12"
	if err := validateRustBindKeys(keys); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected key map validation failure: %v", err)
	}
	got := mergeRustUtilityBindsWithMap("bind f1 custom-command\nbind x keep-me\n", keys)
	for id, key := range keys {
		if !strings.Contains(got, "bind "+key+" ") {
			t.Errorf("custom key for %s was not written: %q", id, got)
		}
	}
	for _, keep := range []string{"bind f1 custom-command", "bind x keep-me"} {
		if !strings.Contains(got, keep) {
			t.Errorf("unmanaged bind was not preserved: %q", keep)
		}
	}
	if got2 := mergeRustUtilityBindsWithMap(got, keys); got2 != got {
		t.Fatalf("custom bind merge must be idempotent\nfirst: %q\nsecond: %q", got, got2)
	}
}

func TestValidateRustBindKeysRejectsDuplicates(t *testing.T) {
	keys := defaultRustBindKeys()
	keys["crouch"] = keys["autorun"]
	if err := validateRustBindKeys(keys); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("duplicate bind keys should be rejected")
	}
}

func TestMergePreservesUnrelatedMouseFourBind(t *testing.T) {
	got := mergeRustUtilityBinds("bind mouse4 +attack\nbind x +forward\n")
	if !strings.Contains(got, "bind mouse4 +attack") {
		t.Fatalf("unrelated Mouse4 bind should be preserved: %q", got)
	}
}
