package main

import (
	"strconv"
	"strings"
)

type rustCFGEntry struct {
	key   string
	value string
}

// buildRustCFGEntries is kept as a simple default for callers and tests.
func buildRustCFGEntries(quality, drawDistance int) []rustCFGEntry {
	return buildRustCFGEntriesWithDisplay(quality, drawDistance, 90, false)
}

// buildRustCFGEntriesWithDisplay creates an adaptive client profile. It only
// writes player-side graphics/performance settings and leaves controls,
// resolution, audio, networking and server variables untouched.
func buildRustCFGEntriesWithDisplay(quality, drawDistance, fov int, vsync bool) []rustCFGEntry {
	if quality < 0 {
		quality = 0
	}
	if quality > 5 {
		quality = 5
	}
	if drawDistance < 500 {
		drawDistance = 500
	}
	// Current Rust's draw-distance slider is capped at 2500 metres.
	if drawDistance > 2500 {
		drawDistance = 2500
	}
	if fov < 70 {
		fov = 70
	}
	if fov > 90 {
		fov = 90
	}

	low := quality == 0
	high := quality >= 3
	shadowMode, shadowLights, queuedFrames := "1", "0", "1"
	lodBias, parallax := "0.5", "0"
	shadowDistance, cascades, pixelLights, shadowMap, shadowPreset := "50", "1", "1", "0", "0"
	anisotropic, softParticles := "1", "False"
	grassDistance, grassQuality := "100", "0"
	antialiasing, maxGibDistance, maxGibLife, maxGibs, minGibLife := "0", "80", "4", "20", "1"
	decorQuality, treeQuality, meshQuality, entityDistance := "40", "200", "500", "2500"
	waterQuality, waterReflections, particleQuality, terrainQuality := "0", "0", "0", "0"
	planarReflections, planarCount, sssQuality := "False", "0", "0"

	if !low {
		// Mid-range profile: keep hard shadows simple and disable the costly
		// water reflections/planar reflection pass.
		shadowMode, shadowLights = "1", "1"
		lodBias, parallax = "0.75", "0"
		shadowDistance, cascades, pixelLights, shadowMap, shadowPreset = "80", "1", "1", "0", "0"
		anisotropic, softParticles = "4", "False"
		grassDistance, grassQuality = "120", "0"
		antialiasing, maxGibDistance, maxGibLife, maxGibs, minGibLife = "1", "100", "5", "30", "2"
		decorQuality, treeQuality, meshQuality, entityDistance = "55", "400", "650", "3500"
		waterQuality, waterReflections, particleQuality, terrainQuality = "1", "0", "30", "45"
	}
	if high {
		// High tier is deliberately not an ultra preset: two shadow cascades,
		// moderate reflections and reduced particles avoid wasting GPU headroom.
		shadowMode, shadowLights, queuedFrames = "2", "2", "2"
		lodBias, parallax = "1", "1"
		shadowDistance, cascades, pixelLights, shadowMap, shadowPreset = "140", "2", "2", "1", "1"
		anisotropic, softParticles = "8", "True"
		grassDistance, grassQuality = "160", "1"
		antialiasing, maxGibDistance, maxGibLife, maxGibs, minGibLife = "2", "130", "8", "45", "4"
		decorQuality, treeQuality, meshQuality, entityDistance = "80", "750", "850", "4500"
		waterQuality, waterReflections, particleQuality, terrainQuality = "1", "1", "60", "80"
		planarReflections, planarCount, sssQuality = "True", "2", "1"
	}

	bool := func(v bool) string {
		if v {
			return "True"
		}
		return "False"
	}
	vsyncValue := "0"
	if vsync {
		vsyncValue = "1"
	}

	return []rustCFGEntry{
		{"graphics.aggressiveshadowlod", bool(true)},
		{"graphics.aggressiveshadowlodwearable", bool(true)},
		{"graphics.contactshadows", bool(false)},
		{"graphics.dof", bool(false)},
		{"graphics.drawdistance", strconv.Itoa(drawDistance)},
		{"graphics.fov", strconv.Itoa(fov)},
		{"graphics.grassshadows", bool(false)},
		{"graphics.hlod", bool(true)},
		{"graphics.impostorshadows", bool(false)},
		{"graphics.jitterspread", "1"},
		{"graphics.lodbias", lodBias},
		{"graphics.maxqueuedframes", queuedFrames},
		{"graphics.parallax", parallax},
		{"graphics.renderscale", "1"},
		{"graphics.shadowlights", shadowLights},
		{"graphics.shadowmode", shadowMode},
		{"graphics.shaderlod", "214"},
		{"graphics.vsync", vsyncValue},
		{"graphics.af", anisotropic},
		{"graphicssettings.numshadowcascades", cascades},
		{"graphicssettings.pixellightcount", pixelLights},
		{"graphicssettings.shadowdistance", shadowDistance},
		{"graphicssettings.shadowmapresolution", shadowMap},
		{"graphicssettings.shadowqualitypreset", shadowPreset},
		{"graphicssettings.softparticles", softParticles},
		{"grass.displacement", bool(false)},
		{"grass.distance", grassDistance},
		{"grass.quality", grassQuality},
		{"grass.refresh_budget", "0.3"},
		{"effects.antialiasing", antialiasing},
		{"effects.ao", bool(false)},
		{"effects.bloom", bool(false)},
		{"effects.creationeffects", bool(false)},
		{"effects.lensdirt", bool(false)},
		{"effects.maxgibdist", maxGibDistance},
		{"effects.maxgiblife", maxGibLife},
		{"effects.maxgibs", maxGibs},
		{"effects.mingiblife", minGibLife},
		{"effects.motionblur", bool(false)},
		{"effects.otherplayerslightflares", bool(false)},
		{"effects.shafts", bool(false)},
		{"effects.sharpen", bool(true)},
		{"effects.showoutlines", bool(true)},
		{"effects.vignet", bool(false)},
		{"decor.quality", decorQuality},
		{"tree.quality", treeQuality},
		{"mesh.quality", meshQuality},
		{"culling.entitymaxdist", entityDistance},
		{"water.quality", waterQuality},
		{"water.reflections", waterReflections},
		{"particle.quality", particleQuality},
		{"terrain.quality", terrainQuality},
		{"reflection.planarreflections", planarReflections},
		{"reflection.planarcount", planarCount},
		{"sss.quality", sssQuality},
		{"gc.buffer_enabled", bool(true)},
	}
}

func cfgLineKey(line string) string {
	line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
	if line == "" || strings.HasPrefix(line, "//") || strings.HasPrefix(line, "#") || strings.HasPrefix(line, ";") {
		return ""
	}
	fields := strings.Fields(line)
	if len(fields) == 0 {
		return ""
	}
	return strings.ToLower(fields[0])
}

// mergeRustCFG only replaces VOID's allow-listed rendering keys. Unknown keys,
// user comments, binds and other client preferences are preserved unchanged.
func mergeRustCFG(original string, entries []rustCFGEntry) string {
	managed := make(map[string]string, len(entries))
	for _, e := range entries {
		managed[strings.ToLower(e.key)] = e.value
	}
	lines := strings.Split(strings.ReplaceAll(original, "\r\n", "\n"), "\n")
	kept := make([]string, 0, len(lines)+len(entries))
	for _, line := range lines {
		trimmed := strings.TrimSpace(line)
		if _, ok := managed[cfgLineKey(line)]; ok || strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, "// VOID OPTIMIZER — graphics profile") {
			continue
		}
		kept = append(kept, line)
	}
	for len(kept) > 0 && kept[len(kept)-1] == "" {
		kept = kept[:len(kept)-1]
	}
	if len(kept) > 0 {
		kept = append(kept, "")
	}
	kept = append(kept, "// VOID OPTIMIZER — graphics profile; restore with VOID or edit in Rust")
	for _, e := range entries {
		kept = append(kept, e.key+" \""+e.value+"\"")
	}
	return strings.Join(kept, "\r\n") + "\r\n"
}

FILE: rust_cfg_test.go

package main

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

func TestBuildRustCFGEntriesHasBroadUniqueGraphicsProfile(t *testing.T) {
	entries := buildRustCFGEntries(0, 1500)
	if len(entries) < 55 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 40+ supported graphics settings, got %d", len(entries))
	}
	seen := map[string]bool{}
	for _, e := range entries {
		key := strings.ToLower(e.key)
		if seen[key] {
			t.Fatalf("duplicate CFG key %q", key)
		}
		seen[key] = true
		allowedPrefixes := []string{"graphics.", "graphicssettings.", "grass.", "effects.", "decor.", "tree.", "mesh.", "culling."}
		allowed := false
		for _, prefix := range allowedPrefixes {
			if strings.HasPrefix(key, prefix) {
				allowed = true
				break
			}
		}
		if !allowed {
			t.Fatalf("non-graphics key escaped allow-list: %q", key)
		}
	}
	for _, removed := range []string{"graphics.quality", "graphics.shadowdistance", "graphics.shadowcascades", "graphics.shadowquality", "bind", "input.bind", "global.god", "player.infiniteammo"} {
		if seen[removed] {
			t.Fatalf("deprecated or unsafe key was included: %q", removed)
		}
	}
}

func TestBuildRustCFGEntriesClampsInputsAndScalesSettings(t *testing.T) {
	low := buildRustCFGEntries(-5, 1)
	balanced := buildRustCFGEntries(2, 1600)
	high := buildRustCFGEntries(9, 9000)
	find := func(entries []rustCFGEntry, key string) string {
		for _, e := range entries {
			if e.key == key {
				return e.value
			}
		}
		return ""
	}
	if find(low, "graphics.drawdistance") != "500" || find(high, "graphics.drawdistance") != "2500" {
		t.Fatal("draw distance was not clamped")
	}
	if find(low, "grass.quality") != "0" || find(balanced, "grass.quality") != "1" || find(high, "grass.quality") != "2" {
		t.Fatal("grass settings did not follow performance tiers")
	}
	if find(low, "graphicssettings.shadowdistance") != "50" || find(high, "graphicssettings.shadowdistance") != "180" {
		t.Fatal("shadow distance did not follow performance tiers")
	}
	if find(low, "tree.quality") != "200" || find(high, "tree.quality") != "900" {
		t.Fatal("tree LOD did not follow performance tiers")
	}
}

func TestMergeRustCFGPreservesUnknownEntriesAndIsIdempotent(t *testing.T) {
	entries := buildRustCFGEntries(0, 1500)
	original := "global.language \"en\"\r\ngraphics.fov \"75\"\r\ncustom.user.setting \"x\"\r\nbind q \"forward\"\r\n// keep comment\r\n"
	first := mergeRustCFG(original, entries)
	second := mergeRustCFG(first, entries)
	if first != second {
		t.Fatalf("merge not idempotent")
	}
	for _, keep := range []string{"global.language \"en\"", "custom.user.setting \"x\"", "bind q \"forward\"", "// keep comment"} {
		if !strings.Contains(first, keep) {
			t.Fatalf("unmanaged line lost: %q", keep)
		}
	}
	if strings.Count(strings.ToLower(first), "graphics.fov ") != 1 {
		t.Fatal("managed key should occur exactly once")
	}
}

func TestBuildRustCFGEntriesUsesSelectedDisplaySettingsAndCurrentPlayerKeys(t *testing.T) {
	entries := buildRustCFGEntriesWithDisplay(0, 4000, 120, true)
	find := func(key string) string {
		for _, entry := range entries {
			if entry.key == key {
				return entry.value
			}
		}
		return ""
	}
	if find("graphics.drawdistance") != "2500" || find("graphics.fov") != "90" || find("graphics.vsync") != "1" {
		t.Fatal("display settings were not clamped/applied")
	}
	if find("graphics.af") != "1" || find("gc.buffer_enabled") != "True" || find("water.reflections") != "0" {
		t.Fatal("expected current player-side performance keys are missing")
	}
	if find("graphicssettings.anisotropicfiltering") != "" || find("graphicssettings.enablelodcrossfade") != "" {
		t.Fatal("legacy/unconfirmed graphics key was emitted")
	}
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: rust_monitor.go
====================================================================================================
package main

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"net/http"
	"net/url"
	"regexp"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

const (
	steamRustAppID = 252490
	maxRustWatch   = 7
	steamAPIBase   = "https://api.steampowered.com"
)

var steamID64Pattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9]{17}$`)
var steamAPIKeyPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`(?i)^[0-9a-f]{32}$`)

type RustTrackedPlayer struct {
	SteamID64       string    `json:"steam_id64"`
	PersonaName     string    `json:"persona_name"`
	ProfileURL      string    `json:"profile_url"`
	AvatarFull      string    `json:"avatar_full,omitempty"`
	PersonaState    int       `json:"persona_state"`
	CurrentGame     string    `json:"current_game,omitempty"`
	RustPlaying     bool      `json:"rust_playing"`
	GameStatusKnown bool      `json:"game_status_known"`
	RustHours       float64   `json:"rust_hours,omitempty"`
	RustHours2Weeks float64   `json:"rust_hours_2weeks,omitempty"`
	HoursKnown      bool      `json:"hours_known"`
	HoursError      string    `json:"hours_error,omitempty"`
	LastLogoff      time.Time `json:"last_logoff,omitempty"`
	CheckedAt       time.Time `json:"checked_at"`
}

type rustMonitorResult struct {
	Players []RustTrackedPlayer
	Err     error
}

type steamSummariesResponse struct {
	Response struct {
		Players []struct {
			SteamID       string `json:"steamid"`
			PersonaName   string `json:"personaname"`
			ProfileURL    string `json:"profileurl"`
			AvatarFull    string `json:"avatarfull"`
			PersonaState  int    `json:"personastate"`
			GameID        string `json:"gameid"`
			GameExtraInfo string `json:"gameextrainfo"`
			LastLogoff    int64  `json:"lastlogoff"`
		} `json:"players"`
	} `json:"response"`
}

type steamOwnedGamesResponse struct {
	Response struct {
		GameCount int `json:"game_count"`
		Games     []struct {
			AppID           int `json:"appid"`
			PlaytimeForever int `json:"playtime_forever"`
			Playtime2Weeks  int `json:"playtime_2weeks"`
		} `json:"games"`
	} `json:"response"`
}

func validSteamID64(id string) bool    { return steamID64Pattern.MatchString(strings.TrimSpace(id)) }
func validSteamAPIKey(key string) bool { return steamAPIKeyPattern.MatchString(strings.TrimSpace(key)) }

// fetchRustPlayers reads only Steam's documented public profile/game-library fields.
// A missing game-library response is treated as private/hidden, never as zero hours.
func fetchRustPlayers(ctx context.Context, client *http.Client, baseURL, apiKey string, steamIDs []string) ([]RustTrackedPlayer, error) {
	apiKey = strings.TrimSpace(apiKey)
	if !validSteamAPIKey(apiKey) {
		return nil, errors.New("нужен корректный Steam Web API key (32 символа)")
	}
	ids := make([]string, 0, len(steamIDs))
	seen := map[string]bool{}
	for _, id := range steamIDs {
		id = strings.TrimSpace(id)
		if !validSteamID64(id) {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("некорректный SteamID64: %q", id)
		}
		if !seen[id] {
			seen[id] = true
			ids = append(ids, id)
		}
	}
	if len(ids) == 0 {
		return nil, errors.New("список наблюдения пуст")
	}
	if len(ids) > maxRustWatch {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("можно отслеживать не более %d профилей", maxRustWatch)
	}
	if client == nil {
		client = &http.Client{Timeout: 12 * time.Second}
	}
	baseURL = strings.TrimRight(strings.TrimSpace(baseURL), "/")
	if baseURL == "" {
		baseURL = steamAPIBase
	}

	q := url.Values{}
	q.Set("key", apiKey)
	q.Set("steamids", strings.Join(ids, ","))
	var summaries steamSummariesResponse
	if err := steamGETJSON(ctx, client, baseURL+"/ISteamUser/GetPlayerSummaries/v2/?"+q.Encode(), &summaries); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("Steam profile API: %w", err)
	}
	byID := make(map[string]RustTrackedPlayer, len(ids))
	for _, p := range summaries.Response.Players {
		if !validSteamID64(p.SteamID) {
			continue
		}
		player := RustTrackedPlayer{
			SteamID64: p.SteamID, PersonaName: p.PersonaName, ProfileURL: p.ProfileURL,
			AvatarFull: p.AvatarFull, PersonaState: p.PersonaState,
			CurrentGame: p.GameExtraInfo, CheckedAt: time.Now(),
		}
		if p.LastLogoff > 0 {
			player.LastLogoff = time.Unix(p.LastLogoff, 0).UTC()
		}
		gameID := strings.TrimSpace(p.GameID)
		gameName := strings.EqualFold(strings.TrimSpace(p.GameExtraInfo), "Rust")
		if gameID == fmt.Sprint(steamRustAppID) || gameName {
			player.RustPlaying = true
			player.GameStatusKnown = true
			player.CurrentGame = "Rust"
		} else if gameID != "" || p.PersonaState == 0 {
			player.RustPlaying = false
			player.GameStatusKnown = true
		}
		byID[p.SteamID] = player
	}

	out := make([]RustTrackedPlayer, 0, len(ids))
	for _, id := range ids {
		player, ok := byID[id]
		if !ok {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("Steam не вернул профиль %s: профиль может быть скрыт или ID неверен", id)
		}
		gamesQ := url.Values{}
		gamesQ.Set("key", apiKey)
		gamesQ.Set("steamid", id)
		gamesQ.Set("include_appinfo", "0")
		gamesQ.Set("include_played_free_games", "1")
		gamesQ.Set("appids_filter[0]", fmt.Sprint(steamRustAppID))
		var owned steamOwnedGamesResponse
		gamesURL := baseURL + "/IPlayerService/GetOwnedGames/v1/?" + gamesQ.Encode()
		if err := steamGETJSON(ctx, client, gamesURL, &owned); err != nil {
			player.HoursError = err.Error()
		} else {
			for _, game := range owned.Response.Games {
				if game.AppID == steamRustAppID {
					player.RustHours = float64(game.PlaytimeForever) / 60
					player.RustHours2Weeks = float64(game.Playtime2Weeks) / 60
					player.HoursKnown = true
					break
				}
			}
			if !player.HoursKnown {
				player.HoursError = "часы скрыты в настройках приватности Steam или игра не отображается"
			}
		}
		out = append(out, player)
	}
	sort.SliceStable(out, func(i, j int) bool { return out[i].PersonaName < out[j].PersonaName })
	return out, nil
}

func steamGETJSON(ctx context.Context, client *http.Client, endpoint string, dst any) error {
	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodGet, endpoint, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	resp, err := client.Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer resp.Body.Close()
	if resp.StatusCode < 200 || resp.StatusCode >= 300 {
		body, _ := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(resp.Body, 2048))
		return fmt.Errorf("HTTP %d: %s", resp.StatusCode, strings.TrimSpace(string(body)))
	}
	dec := json.NewDecoder(io.LimitReader(resp.Body, 2<<20))
	if err := dec.Decode(dst); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("ошибка чтения ответа: %w", err)
	}
	return nil
}

// rustPlayerTransition returns a notification only when Steam exposed a known state.
// Unknown/private game status must not be misreported as a player leaving Rust.
func rustPlayerTransition(previous, current RustTrackedPlayer) string {
	if !current.GameStatusKnown || !previous.GameStatusKnown || previous.RustPlaying == current.RustPlaying {
		return ""
	}
	name := current.PersonaName
	if strings.TrimSpace(name) == "" {
		name = current.SteamID64
	}
	if current.RustPlaying {
		return name + " зашёл в Rust"
	}
	return name + " вышел из Rust"
}

func rustPresenceLabel(p RustTrackedPlayer) string {
	if p.GameStatusKnown && p.RustPlaying {
		return "В RUST СЕЙЧАС"
	}
	if p.GameStatusKnown && !p.RustPlaying && p.PersonaState == 0 {
		return "НЕ В СЕТИ"
	}
	if p.GameStatusKnown && !p.RustPlaying {
		return "STEAM ONLINE · НЕ RUST"
	}
	if p.PersonaState == 0 {
		return "НЕ В СЕТИ · ДАННЫЕ ИГРЫ СКРЫТЫ"
	}
	return "STEAM ONLINE · ИГРА СКРЫТА"
}

// normalizeSteamIDs keeps the saved list valid and duplicate-free.
func normalizeSteamIDs(input []string) []string {
	seen := make(map[string]bool)
	out := make([]string, 0, len(input))
	for _, raw := range input {
		id := strings.TrimSpace(raw)
		if !validSteamID64(id) || seen[id] {
			continue
		}
		seen[id] = true
		out = append(out, id)
		if len(out) == maxRustWatch {
			break
		}
	}
	return out
}

// parseSteamIDs accepts one SteamID64 per line, or comma/semicolon separated.
func parseSteamIDs(raw string) ([]string, error) {
	parts := strings.FieldsFunc(raw, func(r rune) bool {
		return r == '\n' || r == '\r' || r == ',' || r == ';' || r == '\t' || r == ' '
	})
	seen := make(map[string]bool)
	ids := make([]string, 0, len(parts))
	for _, part := range parts {
		id := strings.TrimSpace(part)
		if id == "" {
			continue
		}
		if !validSteamID64(id) {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("некорректный SteamID64: %q — нужен идентификатор из 17 цифр", id)
		}
		if seen[id] {
			continue
		}
		seen[id] = true
		ids = append(ids, id)
		if len(ids) > maxRustWatch {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("лимит списка — %d игроков", maxRustWatch)
		}
	}
	return ids, nil
}

// rustPlayerTransitions reports only confirmed state changes between two polls.
// Hidden/private statuses and first-seen players never generate false alerts.
func rustPlayerTransitions(previous, current []RustTrackedPlayer) []string {
	before := make(map[string]RustTrackedPlayer, len(previous))
	for _, p := range previous {
		before[p.SteamID64] = p
	}
	var events []string
	for _, now := range current {
		old, ok := before[now.SteamID64]
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		if event := rustPlayerTransition(old, now); event != "" {
			events = append(events, event)
		}
	}
	return events
}

package main

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

const testSteamKey = "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef"
const testSteamID = "76561198000000001"

func TestFetchRustPlayersReadsProfileAndHours(t *testing.T) {
	srv := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		switch r.URL.Path {
		case "/ISteamUser/GetPlayerSummaries/v2/":
			if r.URL.Query().Get("key") != testSteamKey || r.URL.Query().Get("steamids") != testSteamID {
				t.Errorf("unexpected summary query: %s", r.URL.RawQuery)
			}
			fmt.Fprintf(w, `{"response":{"players":[{"steamid":"%s","personaname":"VOID_TEST","profileurl":"https://steamcommunity.com/profiles/%s","avatarfull":"https://example.test/avatar.png","personastate":1,"gameid":"252490","gameextrainfo":"Rust","lastlogoff":1700000000}]}}`, testSteamID, testSteamID)
		case "/IPlayerService/GetOwnedGames/v1/":
			if r.URL.Query().Get("steamid") != testSteamID || r.URL.Query().Get("appids_filter[0]") != "252490" {
				t.Errorf("unexpected owned games query: %s", r.URL.RawQuery)
			}
			fmt.Fprint(w, `{"response":{"game_count":1,"games":[{"appid":252490,"playtime_forever":1530,"playtime_2weeks":120}]}}`)
		default:
			http.NotFound(w, r)
		}
	}))
	defer srv.Close()

	players, err := fetchRustPlayers(context.Background(), srv.Client(), srv.URL, testSteamKey, []string{testSteamID})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(players) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("got %d players", len(players))
	}
	p := players[0]
	if !p.RustPlaying || !p.GameStatusKnown {
		t.Fatalf("expected known Rust presence: %+v", p)
	}
	if !p.HoursKnown || p.RustHours != 25.5 || p.RustHours2Weeks != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected playtime: %+v", p)
	}
	if p.PersonaName != "VOID_TEST" || p.ProfileURL == "" || p.LastLogoff.IsZero() {
		t.Fatalf("profile fields missing: %+v", p)
	}
}

func TestFetchRustPlayersDoesNotTreatHiddenHoursAsZero(t *testing.T) {
	srv := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		if r.URL.Path == "/ISteamUser/GetPlayerSummaries/v2/" {
			fmt.Fprintf(w, `{"response":{"players":[{"steamid":"%s","personaname":"PRIVATE","personastate":0}]}}`, testSteamID)
			return
		}
		fmt.Fprint(w, `{"response":{}}`)
	}))
	defer srv.Close()
	players, err := fetchRustPlayers(context.Background(), srv.Client(), srv.URL, testSteamKey, []string{testSteamID})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if players[0].HoursKnown {
		t.Fatal("hidden playtime must not be represented as 0 hours")
	}
	if players[0].HoursError == "" {
		t.Fatal("expected privacy explanation")
	}
}

func TestValidationAndWatchLimit(t *testing.T) {
	if validSteamID64("not-a-steam-id") || validSteamID64("123") {
		t.Fatal("invalid SteamID64 accepted")
	}
	if validSteamAPIKey("bad-key") {
		t.Fatal("invalid API key accepted")
	}
	ids := make([]string, maxRustWatch+1)
	for i := range ids {
		ids[i] = fmt.Sprintf("76561198000000%03d", i)
	}
	if _, err := fetchRustPlayers(context.Background(), nil, "", testSteamKey, ids); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("watch limit not enforced")
	}
}

func TestRustPlayerTransitionsIgnoreUnknownState(t *testing.T) {
	before := RustTrackedPlayer{SteamID64: testSteamID, PersonaName: "A", GameStatusKnown: true, RustPlaying: true}
	unknown := RustTrackedPlayer{SteamID64: testSteamID, PersonaName: "A", GameStatusKnown: false, RustPlaying: false}
	if got := rustPlayerTransition(before, unknown); got != "" {
		t.Fatalf("unknown state caused false event: %q", got)
	}
	after := RustTrackedPlayer{SteamID64: testSteamID, PersonaName: "A", GameStatusKnown: true, RustPlaying: false}
	if got := rustPlayerTransition(before, after); got == "" {
		t.Fatal("expected leave notification")
	}
	if got := rustPlayerTransition(after, before); got == "" {
		t.Fatal("expected join notification")
	}
}

func TestSteamRequestHonorsContext(t *testing.T) {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), time.Nanosecond)
	defer cancel()
	time.Sleep(time.Millisecond)
	var dst map[string]any
	if err := steamGETJSON(ctx, &http.Client{}, "https://example.invalid", &dst); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected canceled request")
	}
}

func TestParseSteamIDsDeduplicatesAndEnforcesLimit(t *testing.T) {
	ids, err := parseSteamIDs(testSteamID + "\n" + testSteamID + ", 76561198000000002")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(ids) != 2 || ids[0] != testSteamID {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected ids: %#v", ids)
	}
	if _, err := parseSteamIDs("not-an-id"); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("invalid SteamID64 accepted")
	}
	tooMany := ""
	for i := 0; i < maxRustWatch+1; i++ {
		tooMany += fmt.Sprintf("76561198000000%03d\n", i)
	}
	if _, err := parseSteamIDs(tooMany); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("watch list limit was not enforced")
	}
}

func TestRustPlayerTransitionsOnlyKnownChanges(t *testing.T) {
	before := []RustTrackedPlayer{{SteamID64: testSteamID, PersonaName: "A", GameStatusKnown: true, RustPlaying: true}}
	after := []RustTrackedPlayer{{SteamID64: testSteamID, PersonaName: "A", GameStatusKnown: true, RustPlaying: false}}
	got := rustPlayerTransitions(before, after)
	if len(got) != 1 || got[0] != "A вышел из Rust" {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected transition: %#v", got)
	}
	unknown := []RustTrackedPlayer{{SteamID64: testSteamID, PersonaName: "A", GameStatusKnown: false}}
	if got := rustPlayerTransitions(before, unknown); len(got) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("unknown status generated event: %#v", got)
	}
}

//go:build windows

package main

import (
	"context"
	"net/http"
	"time"
)

func startRustMonitor() {
	if app.RustMonitorBusy {
		return
	}
	if !validSteamAPIKey(app.RustSteamAPIKey) {
		app.RustMonitorError = "Нужен корректный Steam Web API Key."
		app.Status = "Введи Steam Web API Key на странице RUST+ МОНИТОРИНГ."
		invalidate()
		return
	}
	ids := normalizeSteamIDs(app.RustTrackedIDs)
	if len(ids) == 0 {
		app.RustMonitorError = "Список SteamID64 пуст."
		app.Status = "Добавь SteamID64 игроков, которых нужно отслеживать."
		invalidate()
		return
	}
	app.RustTrackedIDs = ids
	key := app.RustSteamAPIKey
	ch := make(chan rustMonitorResult, 1)
	app.RustMonitorCh = ch
	app.RustMonitorBusy = true
	app.RustMonitorError = ""
	app.RustMonitorNextCheck = time.Now().Add(2 * time.Minute)
	app.Status = "Запрашиваем публичный статус Steam и часы в Rust…"
	invalidate()
	go func() {
		ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 35*time.Second)
		defer cancel()
		client := &http.Client{Timeout: 12 * time.Second}
		players, err := fetchRustPlayers(ctx, client, steamAPIBase, key, ids)
		ch <- rustMonitorResult{Players: players, Err: err}
	}()
}

package main

import (
	"bufio"
	"context"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/sha1"
	"encoding/base64"
	"encoding/binary"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"math"
	"net"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"time"
)

// RustPlusPairing contains the four values delivered by Rust+ after the user
// pairs their own Steam account with a Rust server. PlayerToken is secret.
type VoidRadarEvent struct {
	ID        string    `json:"id,omitempty"`
	Type      string    `json:"type"`
	Title     string    `json:"title"`
	Message   string    `json:"message"`
	Player    string    `json:"player,omitempty"`
	SteamID   string    `json:"steam_id,omitempty"`
	Grid      string    `json:"grid,omitempty"`
	X         float64   `json:"x,omitempty"`
	Z         float64   `json:"z,omitempty"`
	MapSize   float64   `json:"map_size,omitempty"`
	Server    string    `json:"server,omitempty"`
	Timestamp time.Time `json:"timestamp"`
}

type RustPlusPairing struct {
	Server      string `json:"server"`
	Port        int    `json:"port"`
	PlayerID    string `json:"playerId"`
	PlayerToken string `json:"playerToken"`
}

func (p RustPlusPairing) Validate() error {
	if strings.TrimSpace(p.Server) == "" || strings.ContainsAny(p.Server, " \t\r\n/\\") {
		return errors.New("укажи IP-адрес или имя Rust-сервера из Rust+ pairing")
	}
	if p.Port < 1 || p.Port > 65535 {
		return errors.New("порт Rust+ должен быть от 1 до 65535; это не игровой порт 28015")
	}
	playerID := strings.TrimSpace(p.PlayerID)
	if _, err := strconv.ParseUint(playerID, 10, 64); err != nil || len(playerID) != 17 {
		return errors.New("Player ID должен быть SteamID64 из 17 цифр")
	}
	if _, err := strconv.ParseInt(strings.TrimSpace(p.PlayerToken), 10, 32); err != nil {
		return errors.New("Player Token должен быть числом из уведомления Rust+ Pairing")
	}
	return nil
}

func rustPlusPairingPath() string {
	root := os.Getenv("LOCALAPPDATA")
	if root == "" {
		root, _ = os.UserConfigDir()
	}
	return filepath.Join(root, "VOID Optimizer", "rustplus-pairing.json")
}

func saveRustPlusPairing(p RustPlusPairing) error {
	if err := p.Validate(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	path := rustPlusPairingPath()
	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(path), 0700); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	b, err := json.MarshalIndent(p, "", "  ")
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	tmp := path + ".tmp"
	if err := os.WriteFile(tmp, b, 0600); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := os.Rename(tmp, path); err != nil {
		_ = os.Remove(tmp)
		return err
	}
	return nil
}

func loadRustPlusPairing() RustPlusPairing {
	var p RustPlusPairing
	b, err := os.ReadFile(rustPlusPairingPath())
	if err == nil {
		_ = json.Unmarshal(b, &p)
	}
	return p
}

// RustPlusRadar uses the Rust+ Companion WebSocket protocol. It polls team
// state and map markers, then publishes detections through Rust+ Team Chat.
// It does not inject input into Rust and does not require a server-side plugin.
type RustPlusRadar struct {
	pairing RustPlusPairing
	events  chan<- VoidRadarEvent
	stop    chan struct{}
	done    chan struct{}
	mu      sync.Mutex
	conn    *rustPlusSocket
}

func StartRustPlusRadar(pairing RustPlusPairing, events chan<- VoidRadarEvent) (*RustPlusRadar, error) {
	if err := pairing.Validate(); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	r := &RustPlusRadar{pairing: pairing, events: events, stop: make(chan struct{}), done: make(chan struct{})}
	go r.run()
	return r, nil
}

func (r *RustPlusRadar) Stop() {
	select {
	case <-r.stop:
	default:
		close(r.stop)
	}
	r.mu.Lock()
	if r.conn != nil {
		_ = r.conn.Close()
		r.conn = nil
	}
	r.mu.Unlock()
	select {
	case <-r.done:
	case <-time.After(2 * time.Second):
	}
}

func (r *RustPlusRadar) emit(typ, title, message, player, steamID, grid string) {
	if r.events == nil {
		return
	}
	e := VoidRadarEvent{Type: typ, Title: title, Message: message, Player: player, SteamID: steamID, Grid: grid, Server: r.pairing.Server, Timestamp: time.Now().UTC()}
	select {
	case r.events <- e:
	default:
	}
}

func (r *RustPlusRadar) run() {
	defer close(r.done)
	for {
		select {
		case <-r.stop:
			return
		default:
		}
		conn, err := dialRustPlus(r.pairing)
		if err != nil {
			r.emit("connection_error", "Rust+ не подключён", err.Error(), "", "", "")
			if !waitOrStop(r.stop, 8*time.Second) {
				return
			}
			continue
		}
		r.mu.Lock()
		r.conn = conn
		r.mu.Unlock()
		r.emit("connected", "VOID Radar подключён к Rust+", "Подключение к Rust+ успешно. Проверяю команду и события.", "", "", "")
		r.monitorConnection(conn)
		r.mu.Lock()
		if r.conn == conn {
			r.conn = nil
		}
		r.mu.Unlock()
		_ = conn.Close()
		r.emit("disconnected", "Связь с Rust+ потеряна", "Пробую переподключиться…", "", "", "")
		if !waitOrStop(r.stop, 5*time.Second) {
			return
		}
	}
}

func waitOrStop(stop <-chan struct{}, d time.Duration) bool {
	t := time.NewTimer(d)
	defer t.Stop()
	select {
	case <-stop:
		return false
	case <-t.C:
		return true
	}
}

func (r *RustPlusRadar) monitorConnection(c *rustPlusSocket) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	info, err := c.getInfo(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		r.emit("connection_error", "Не удалось прочитать сервер Rust+", err.Error(), "", "", "")
		return
	}
	mapSize := float64(info.MapSize)
	if mapSize < 1000 {
		r.emit("connection_error", "Rust+ не вернул размер карты", "Не могу точно определить квадрат смерти без размера карты сервера.", "", "", "")
		return
	}
	team, err := c.getTeamInfo(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		r.emit("connection_error", "Rust+ не отдал состав команды", err.Error(), "", "", "")
		return
	}
	if len(team) == 0 {
		r.emit("waiting_team", "Ожидание команды Rust", "Войди в команду Rust на этом сервере — тогда появятся тиммейты.", "", "", "")
	}
	previous := make(map[string]RustTeamMember, len(team))
	for _, m := range team {
		previous[m.SteamID] = m
	}
	knownMarkers := make(map[uint32]bool)
	markersInitialized := false
	lastMarkers := time.Time{}
	for {
		if !waitOrStop(r.stop, 3*time.Second) {
			return
		}
		fresh, err := c.getTeamInfo(ctx)
		if err != nil {
			return
		}
		for _, cur := range fresh {
			old, existed := previous[cur.SteamID]
			if existed {
				if old.IsAlive && !cur.IsAlive {
					posX, posY := cur.X, cur.Y
					if posX == 0 && posY == 0 {
						posX, posY = old.X, old.Y
					}
					grid := rustPlusGrid(posX, posY, mapSize)
					msg := fmt.Sprintf("[VOID] %s погиб! Квадрат: %s", safeRustChatName(cur.Name), grid)
					if err := c.sendTeamMessage(ctx, msg); err != nil {
						return
					}
					r.emit("teammate_death", "Тиммейт погиб: "+safeRustChatName(cur.Name), msg, cur.Name, cur.SteamID, grid)
				} else if !old.IsOnline && cur.IsOnline {
					msg := "[VOID] " + safeRustChatName(cur.Name) + " подключился к серверу"
					if err := c.sendTeamMessage(ctx, msg); err != nil {
						return
					}
					r.emit("team_join", "Тиммейт подключился", msg, cur.Name, cur.SteamID, "")
				} else if old.IsOnline && !cur.IsOnline {
					msg := "[VOID] " + safeRustChatName(cur.Name) + " вышел с сервера"
					if err := c.sendTeamMessage(ctx, msg); err != nil {
						return
					}
					r.emit("team_leave", "Тиммейт вышел", msg, cur.Name, cur.SteamID, "")
				}
			}
			previous[cur.SteamID] = cur
		}
		if time.Since(lastMarkers) >= 15*time.Second {
			markers, markerErr := c.getMapMarkers(ctx)
			if markerErr == nil {
				for _, marker := range markers {
					if marker.ID == 0 || knownMarkers[marker.ID] {
						continue
					}
					knownMarkers[marker.ID] = true
					if !markersInitialized {
						continue
					}
					grid := rustPlusGrid(marker.X, marker.Y, mapSize)
					msg := rustMarkerChatText(marker, grid)
					if msg == "" {
						continue
					}
					if err := c.sendTeamMessage(ctx, msg); err != nil {
						return
					}
					r.emit("world_event", "Событие Rust: "+markerLabel(marker.Type), msg, "", "", grid)
				}
				markersInitialized = true
				lastMarkers = time.Now()
			}
		}
	}
}

func safeRustChatName(name string) string {
	name = strings.TrimSpace(name)
	name = strings.Map(func(r rune) rune {
		if r < 0x20 || r == 0x7f {
			return -1
		}
		return r
	}, name)
	if name == "" {
		return "Тиммейт"
	}
	if len([]rune(name)) > 32 {
		name = string([]rune(name)[:32])
	}
	return name
}

func rustPlusGrid(x, y, mapSize float64) string {
	if mapSize < 1000 || math.IsNaN(mapSize) || math.IsInf(mapSize, 0) {
		return "?"
	}
	if math.IsNaN(x) || math.IsNaN(y) || math.IsInf(x, 0) || math.IsInf(y, 0) {
		return "?"
	}
	cells := int(math.Round(mapSize/1024.0)) * 7
	if cells < 7 {
		cells = 28
	}
	if cells > 70 {
		cells = 70
	}
	if x < 0 {
		x = 0
	}
	if x >= 1 {
		x = math.Nextafter(1, 0)
	}
	if y < 0 {
		y = 0
	}
	if y >= 1 {
		y = math.Nextafter(1, 0)
	}
	col := int(math.Floor(x * float64(cells)))
	row := int(math.Floor((1 - y) * float64(cells)))
	if row >= cells {
		row = cells - 1
	}
	return rustGridColumn(col) + strconv.Itoa(row+1)
}

func rustGridColumn(n int) string {
	if n < 0 {
		n = 0
	}
	var out string
	for n >= 0 {
		out = string(rune('A'+n%26)) + out
		n = n/26 - 1
	}
	return out
}

func markerLabel(t uint32) string {
	switch t {
	case 4:
		return "CH-47"
	case 5:
		return "Cargo"
	case 6:
		return "Locked Crate"
	case 8:
		return "Patrol Helicopter"
	default:
		return "событие"
	}
}
func rustMarkerChatText(m RustMapMarker, grid string) string {
	switch m.Type {
	case 4:
		return "[VOID] CH-47 замечен. Квадрат: " + grid
	case 5:
		return "[VOID] Cargo Ship замечен. Квадрат: " + grid
	case 6:
		return "[VOID] Locked Crate обнаружен. Квадрат: " + grid
	case 8:
		return "[VOID] Patrol Helicopter замечен. Квадрат: " + grid
	default:
		return ""
	}
}

// ---- Minimal Rust+ protobuf + RFC6455 WebSocket client (no third-party DLLs). ----
type rustPlusSocket struct {
	conn        net.Conn
	reader      *bufio.Reader
	playerID    uint64
	playerToken int32
	seq         uint32
	mu          sync.Mutex
}

type RustTeamMember struct {
	SteamID, Name        string
	X, Y                 float64
	IsOnline, IsAlive    bool
	SpawnTime, DeathTime uint32
}
type rustPlusInfo struct {
	Name    string
	MapSize uint32
}
type RustMapMarker struct {
	ID, Type uint32
	X, Y     float64
}

func dialRustPlus(p RustPlusPairing) (*rustPlusSocket, error) {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 12*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	addr := net.JoinHostPort(strings.Trim(strings.TrimSpace(p.Server), "[]"), strconv.Itoa(p.Port))
	conn, err := (&net.Dialer{Timeout: 10 * time.Second}).DialContext(ctx, "tcp", addr)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("не удалось открыть Rust+ соединение (%s:%d): %w", p.Server, p.Port, err)
	}
	fail := func(e error) (*rustPlusSocket, error) { _ = conn.Close(); return nil, e }
	keyBytes := make([]byte, 16)
	if _, err := rand.Read(keyBytes); err != nil {
		return fail(err)
	}
	key := base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(keyBytes)
	host := net.JoinHostPort(strings.Trim(strings.TrimSpace(p.Server), "[]"), strconv.Itoa(p.Port))
	if strings.Contains(p.Server, ":") && !strings.HasPrefix(p.Server, "[") {
		host = "[" + p.Server + "]:" + strconv.Itoa(p.Port)
	}
	request := "GET / HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: " + host + "\r\nUpgrade: websocket\r\nConnection: Upgrade\r\nSec-WebSocket-Key: " + key + "\r\nSec-WebSocket-Version: 13\r\nOrigin: https://companion-rust.facepunch.com\r\n\r\n"
	if _, err := io.WriteString(conn, request); err != nil {
		return fail(err)
	}
	reader := bufio.NewReader(conn)
	_ = conn.SetReadDeadline(time.Now().Add(10 * time.Second))
	resp, err := http.ReadResponse(reader, &http.Request{Method: http.MethodGet})
	if err != nil {
		return fail(fmt.Errorf("ошибка WebSocket handshake: %w", err))
	}
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusSwitchingProtocols {
		return fail(fmt.Errorf("сервер Rust+ отклонил WebSocket: HTTP %d (порт должен быть Rust+ app.port, а сервер должен разрешать прямое Rust+ подключение)", resp.StatusCode))
	}
	accept := resp.Header.Get("Sec-WebSocket-Accept")
	sum := sha1.Sum([]byte(key + "258EAFA5-E914-47DA-95CA-C5AB0DC85B11"))
	if accept != base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(sum[:]) {
		return fail(errors.New("Rust+ вернул неверный WebSocket handshake"))
	}
	playerID, _ := strconv.ParseUint(strings.TrimSpace(p.PlayerID), 10, 64)
	token64, _ := strconv.ParseInt(strings.TrimSpace(p.PlayerToken), 10, 32)
	_ = conn.SetReadDeadline(time.Time{})
	return &rustPlusSocket{conn: conn, reader: reader, playerID: playerID, playerToken: int32(token64)}, nil
}

func (s *rustPlusSocket) Close() error {
	if s == nil || s.conn == nil {
		return nil
	}
	// Closing the TCP connection is enough here and avoids racing a close-frame
	// write against a request currently using the same socket.
	return s.conn.Close()
}

func (s *rustPlusSocket) writeFrame(opcode byte, payload []byte) error {
	if s.conn == nil {
		return net.ErrClosed
	}
	var h []byte
	h = append(h, 0x80|(opcode&0x0f))
	n := len(payload)
	switch {
	case n < 126:
		h = append(h, 0x80|byte(n))
	case n <= 65535:
		h = append(h, 0x80|126, byte(n>>8), byte(n))
	default:
		h = append(h, 0x80|127)
		var b [8]byte
		binary.BigEndian.PutUint64(b[:], uint64(n))
		h = append(h, b[:]...)
	}
	mask := make([]byte, 4)
	if _, err := rand.Read(mask); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	h = append(h, mask...)
	masked := make([]byte, n)
	for i := range payload {
		masked[i] = payload[i] ^ mask[i%4]
	}
	if _, err := s.conn.Write(h); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	_, err := s.conn.Write(masked)
	return err
}

func (s *rustPlusSocket) readFrame() (byte, []byte, error) {
	var h [2]byte
	if _, err := io.ReadFull(s.reader, h[:]); err != nil {
		return 0, nil, err
	}
	opcode := h[0] & 0x0f
	masked := h[1]&0x80 != 0
	n := uint64(h[1] & 0x7f)
	if n == 126 {
		var b [2]byte
		if _, err := io.ReadFull(s.reader, b[:]); err != nil {
			return 0, nil, err
		}
		n = uint64(binary.BigEndian.Uint16(b[:]))
	}
	if n == 127 {
		var b [8]byte
		if _, err := io.ReadFull(s.reader, b[:]); err != nil {
			return 0, nil, err
		}
		n = binary.BigEndian.Uint64(b[:])
	}
	if n > 2<<20 {
		return 0, nil, errors.New("Rust+ frame превышает 2 МБ")
	}
	var mask [4]byte
	if masked {
		if _, err := io.ReadFull(s.reader, mask[:]); err != nil {
			return 0, nil, err
		}
	}
	payload := make([]byte, int(n))
	if _, err := io.ReadFull(s.reader, payload); err != nil {
		return 0, nil, err
	}
	if masked {
		for i := range payload {
			payload[i] ^= mask[i%4]
		}
	}
	return opcode, payload, nil
}

func (s *rustPlusSocket) request(ctx context.Context, commandField int, nested []byte) ([]byte, error) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	select {
	case <-ctx.Done():
		return nil, ctx.Err()
	default:
	}
	s.seq++
	req := pbVarint(nil, 1, uint64(s.seq))
	req = pbVarint(req, 2, s.playerID)
	req = pbVarint(req, 3, uint64(int64(s.playerToken)))
	req = pbBytes(req, commandField, nested)
	message := pbBytes(nil, 1, req)
	_ = s.conn.SetWriteDeadline(time.Now().Add(8 * time.Second))
	if err := s.writeFrame(2, message); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	_ = s.conn.SetWriteDeadline(time.Time{})
	for {
		_ = s.conn.SetReadDeadline(time.Now().Add(12 * time.Second))
		op, data, err := s.readFrame()
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		switch op {
		case 8:
			return nil, errors.New("Rust+ закрыл соединение")
		case 9:
			if err := s.writeFrame(10, data); err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			continue
		case 10:
			continue
		case 1, 2:
		default:
			continue
		}
		respSeq, response, parseErr := findResponse(data)
		if respSeq != s.seq {
			continue
		}
		if parseErr != nil {
			return nil, parseErr
		}
		return response, nil
	}
}

func (s *rustPlusSocket) getInfo(ctx context.Context) (rustPlusInfo, error) {
	b, err := s.request(ctx, 8, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return rustPlusInfo{}, err
	}
	var out rustPlusInfo
	fields, e := pbFields(b)
	if e != nil {
		return out, e
	}
	for _, f := range fields {
		if f.num == 6 && f.wire == 2 {
			inner, _ := pbFields(f.data)
			for _, q := range inner {
				if q.num == 1 && q.wire == 2 {
					out.Name = string(q.data)
				}
				if q.num == 5 && q.wire == 0 {
					out.MapSize = uint32(q.v)
				}
			}
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}
func (s *rustPlusSocket) getTeamInfo(ctx context.Context) ([]RustTeamMember, error) {
	b, err := s.request(ctx, 11, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return parseRustTeamInfo(b)
}
func (s *rustPlusSocket) getMapMarkers(ctx context.Context) ([]RustMapMarker, error) {
	b, err := s.request(ctx, 18, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return parseRustMapMarkers(b)
}
func (s *rustPlusSocket) sendTeamMessage(ctx context.Context, message string) error {
	msg := pbBytes(nil, 1, []byte(message))
	_, err := s.request(ctx, 13, msg)
	return err
}

// protobuf fields are read without generated code; unknown fields are skipped.
type pbField struct {
	num  int
	wire int
	v    uint64
	data []byte
}

func pbVarint(dst []byte, field int, value uint64) []byte {
	dst = appendUvarint(dst, uint64(field<<3))
	return appendUvarint(dst, value)
}
func pbBytes(dst []byte, field int, value []byte) []byte {
	dst = appendUvarint(dst, uint64(field<<3|2))
	dst = appendUvarint(dst, uint64(len(value)))
	return append(dst, value...)
}
func appendUvarint(dst []byte, v uint64) []byte {
	for v >= 0x80 {
		dst = append(dst, byte(v)|0x80)
		v >>= 7
	}
	return append(dst, byte(v))
}
func consumeUvarint(b []byte, i *int) (uint64, error) {
	var v uint64
	for shift := uint(0); shift < 64; shift += 7 {
		if *i >= len(b) {
			return 0, io.ErrUnexpectedEOF
		}
		c := b[*i]
		*i++
		v |= uint64(c&0x7f) << shift
		if c < 0x80 {
			return v, nil
		}
	}
	return 0, errors.New("protobuf varint overflow")
}
func pbFields(b []byte) ([]pbField, error) {
	out := []pbField{}
	i := 0
	for i < len(b) {
		key, e := consumeUvarint(b, &i)
		if e != nil {
			return nil, e
		}
		f := pbField{num: int(key >> 3), wire: int(key & 7)}
		if f.num <= 0 {
			return nil, errors.New("invalid protobuf field")
		}
		switch f.wire {
		case 0:
			f.v, e = consumeUvarint(b, &i)
			if e != nil {
				return nil, e
			}
		case 1:
			if i+8 > len(b) {
				return nil, io.ErrUnexpectedEOF
			}
			f.data = b[i : i+8]
			i += 8
		case 2:
			n, er := consumeUvarint(b, &i)
			if er != nil {
				return nil, er
			}
			if n > uint64(len(b)-i) {
				return nil, io.ErrUnexpectedEOF
			}
			f.data = b[i : i+int(n)]
			i += int(n)
		case 5:
			if i+4 > len(b) {
				return nil, io.ErrUnexpectedEOF
			}
			f.data = b[i : i+4]
			i += 4
		default:
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("unsupported protobuf wire type %d", f.wire)
		}
		out = append(out, f)
	}
	return out, nil
}
func findResponse(raw []byte) (uint32, []byte, error) {
	outer, e := pbFields(raw)
	if e != nil {
		return 0, nil, e
	}
	for _, f := range outer {
		if f.num == 1 && f.wire == 2 {
			fields, er := pbFields(f.data)
			if er != nil {
				return 0, nil, er
			}
			var seq uint32
			var body []byte
			var apiErr string
			for _, q := range fields {
				if q.num == 1 && q.wire == 0 {
					seq = uint32(q.v)
				}
				if q.num >= 6 && q.num <= 17 && q.wire == 2 {
					body = appendUvarint(body, uint64(q.num<<3|2))
					body = appendUvarint(body, uint64(len(q.data)))
					body = append(body, q.data...)
				}
				if q.num == 5 && q.wire == 2 {
					ef, _ := pbFields(q.data)
					for _, v := range ef {
						if v.num == 1 && v.wire == 2 {
							apiErr = string(v.data)
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if apiErr != "" {
				return seq, nil, errors.New(apiErr)
			}
			return seq, body, nil
		}
	}
	return 0, nil, errors.New("Rust+ response block is missing")
}
func parseRustTeamInfo(body []byte) ([]RustTeamMember, error) {
	fields, err := pbFields(body)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var teamData []byte
	for _, f := range fields {
		if f.num == 9 && f.wire == 2 {
			teamData = f.data
			break
		}
	}
	if teamData == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("Rust+ не вернул TeamInfo; проверь, что персонаж состоит в команде")
	}
	teamFields, err := pbFields(teamData)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := []RustTeamMember{}
	for _, f := range teamFields {
		if f.num != 2 || f.wire != 2 {
			continue
		}
		mf, e := pbFields(f.data)
		if e != nil {
			return nil, e
		}
		var m RustTeamMember
		for _, q := range mf {
			switch {
			case q.num == 1 && q.wire == 0:
				m.SteamID = strconv.FormatUint(q.v, 10)
			case q.num == 2 && q.wire == 2:
				m.Name = string(q.data)
			case (q.num == 3 || q.num == 4) && q.wire == 5:
				v := math.Float32frombits(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(q.data))
				if q.num == 3 {
					m.X = float64(v)
				} else {
					m.Y = float64(v)
				}
			case q.num == 5 && q.wire == 0:
				m.IsOnline = q.v != 0
			case q.num == 6 && q.wire == 0:
				m.SpawnTime = uint32(q.v)
			case q.num == 7 && q.wire == 0:
				m.IsAlive = q.v != 0
			case q.num == 8 && q.wire == 0:
				m.DeathTime = uint32(q.v)
			}
		}
		if m.SteamID != "" {
			out = append(out, m)
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}
func parseRustMapMarkers(body []byte) ([]RustMapMarker, error) {
	fields, err := pbFields(body)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var markerData []byte
	for _, f := range fields {
		if f.num == 13 && f.wire == 2 {
			markerData = f.data
			break
		}
	}
	if markerData == nil {
		return nil, nil
	}
	list, err := pbFields(markerData)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := []RustMapMarker{}
	for _, f := range list {
		if f.num != 1 || f.wire != 2 {
			continue
		}
		fs, e := pbFields(f.data)
		if e != nil {
			return nil, e
		}
		var m RustMapMarker
		for _, q := range fs {
			switch {
			case q.num == 1 && q.wire == 0:
				m.ID = uint32(q.v)
			case q.num == 2 && q.wire == 0:
				m.Type = uint32(q.v)
			case (q.num == 3 || q.num == 4) && q.wire == 5:
				v := math.Float32frombits(binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(q.data))
				if q.num == 3 {
					m.X = float64(v)
				} else {
					m.Y = float64(v)
				}
			}
		}
		if m.ID != 0 {
			out = append(out, m)
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

package main

import (
	"encoding/binary"
	"math"
	"testing"
)

func TestRustPlusPairingValidation(t *testing.T) {
	good := RustPlusPairing{Server: "127.0.0.1", Port: 28017, PlayerID: "76561198000000000", PlayerToken: "12345"}
	if err := good.Validate(); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("valid pairing rejected: %v", err)
	}
	bad := good
	bad.Port = 28015
	if err := bad.Validate(); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("a valid app port should pass basic validation: %v", err)
	}
	bad = good
	bad.PlayerID = "12345"
	if err := bad.Validate(); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("invalid SteamID64 accepted")
	}
	bad = good
	bad.PlayerToken = "not-a-number"
	if err := bad.Validate(); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("invalid token accepted")
	}
}

func TestRustPlusGrid(t *testing.T) {
	if got := rustPlusGrid(0, 1, 4000); got != "A1" {
		t.Fatalf("northwest grid: got %s", got)
	}
	if got := rustPlusGrid(0, 0, 4000); got != "A28" {
		t.Fatalf("southwest grid: got %s", got)
	}
	if got := rustPlusGrid(1, 1, 4000); got != "AB1" {
		t.Fatalf("right edge grid: got %s", got)
	}
	if got := rustPlusGrid(0.5, 0.5, 0); got != "?" {
		t.Fatalf("missing map size should not guess a square: got %s", got)
	}
}

func TestRustPlusProtobufTeamInfo(t *testing.T) {
	member := []byte{}
	member = pbVarint(member, 1, 76561198000000000)
	member = pbBytes(member, 2, []byte("Tester"))
	var fx, fy [4]byte
	binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(fx[:], math.Float32bits(0.25))
	binary.LittleEndian.PutUint32(fy[:], math.Float32bits(0.75))
	member = append(member, byte(3<<3|5))
	member = append(member, fx[:]...)
	member = append(member, byte(4<<3|5))
	member = append(member, fy[:]...)
	member = pbVarint(member, 5, 1)
	member = pbVarint(member, 7, 0)
	member = pbVarint(member, 8, 42)
	team := pbBytes(nil, 2, member)
	response := pbBytes(nil, 9, team)
	got, err := parseRustTeamInfo(response)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(got) != 1 || got[0].Name != "Tester" || !got[0].IsOnline || got[0].IsAlive || got[0].DeathTime != 42 {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected parsed member: %#v", got)
	}
}

func TestRustPlusMarkerText(t *testing.T) {
	m := RustMapMarker{ID: 1, Type: 5, X: 0.3, Y: 0.4}
	if got := rustMarkerChatText(m, "G14"); got != "[VOID] Cargo Ship замечен. Квадрат: G14" {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected marker message: %q", got)
	}
	m.Type = 99
	if got := rustMarkerChatText(m, "G14"); got != "" {
		t.Fatalf("unknown marker should be ignored: %q", got)
	}
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: update_url.go
====================================================================================================
// Package main contains validation for the embedded and per-user updater feed.
package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"net/url"
	"strings"
)

// Publisher sets this once before distributing a public build. With a real
// HTTPS feed embedded here, every user can receive updates without entering a URL.
// It intentionally remains empty until the app has an actual release host.
const publisherUpdateFeedURL = ""

func validateUpdateFeedURL(feed string) (string, error) {
	feed = strings.TrimSpace(feed)
	if feed == "" {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("update server URL is not configured in this build")
	}
	u, err := url.Parse(feed)
	if err != nil || u.Scheme != "https" || u.Host == "" || u.User != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("update feed must be a valid HTTPS URL")
	}
	return u.String(), nil
}

func effectiveUpdateFeedURL(savedURL, embeddedURL string) (string, error) {
	feed := strings.TrimSpace(savedURL)
	if feed == "" {
		feed = strings.TrimSpace(embeddedURL)
	}
	return validateUpdateFeedURL(feed)
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: update_url_test.go
====================================================================================================
package main

import "testing"

func TestValidateUpdateFeedURL(t *testing.T) {
	cases := []struct {
		name string
		url  string
		ok   bool
	}{
		{name: "https", url: "https://updates.example.net/void/update.json", ok: true},
		{name: "trim spaces", url: "  https://updates.example.net/update.json  ", ok: true},
		{name: "http rejected", url: "http://updates.example.net/update.json", ok: false},
		{name: "no host", url: "https:///update.json", ok: false},
		{name: "credentials rejected", url: "https://user:pass@updates.example.net/update.json", ok: false},
		{name: "empty rejected", url: "", ok: false},
	}
	for _, tc := range cases {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			got, err := validateUpdateFeedURL(tc.url)
			if tc.ok && err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("expected valid URL, got error: %v", err)
			}
			if !tc.ok && err == nil {
				t.Fatalf("expected invalid URL, got %q", got)
			}
			if tc.ok && got == "" {
				t.Fatal("validated URL is empty")
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestEffectiveUpdateFeedURL(t *testing.T) {
	const embedded = "https://publisher.example/releases/update.json"
	got, err := effectiveUpdateFeedURL("", embedded)
	if err != nil || got != embedded {
		t.Fatalf("expected embedded publisher URL, got %q, err %v", got, err)
	}
	saved := "https://custom.example/update.json"
	got, err = effectiveUpdateFeedURL(saved, embedded)
	if err != nil || got != saved {
		t.Fatalf("saved URL should take precedence, got %q, err %v", got, err)
	}
	if _, err := effectiveUpdateFeedURL("", ""); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("an absent publisher URL must not pretend updates are configured")
	}
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: version.go
====================================================================================================
package main

import (
	"strconv"
	"strings"
)

func versionParts(v string) ([3]int, bool) {
	var out [3]int
	v = strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(strings.TrimSpace(v), "v"))
	parts := strings.Split(v, ".")
	if len(parts) != 3 {
		return out, false
	}
	for i, part := range parts {
		if part == "" {
			return out, false
		}
		for _, ch := range part {
			if ch < '0' || ch > '9' {
				return out, false
			}
		}
		n, err := strconv.Atoi(part)
		if err != nil || n < 0 || n > 999999 {
			return out, false
		}
		out[i] = n
	}
	return out, true
}

func isNewerVersion(latest, current string) bool {
	a, okA := versionParts(latest)
	b, okB := versionParts(current)
	if !okA || !okB {
		return false
	}
	for i := range a {
		if a[i] != b[i] {
			return a[i] > b[i]
		}
	}
	return false
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: version_test.go
====================================================================================================
package main

import "testing"

func TestIsNewerVersion(t *testing.T) {
	cases := []struct {
		latest, current string
		want            bool
	}{
		{"1.7.1", "1.7.0", true},
		{"1.8.0", "1.7.9", true},
		{"1.9.0", "1.8.0", true},
		{"1.9.0", "1.9.0", false},
		{"2.0.0", "1.99.99", true},
		{"2.0.0", "1.9.9", true},
		{"2.0.0", "2.0.0", false},
		{"1.7.0", "1.7.0", false},
		{"1.6.9", "1.7.0", false},
		{"v1.8.0", "1.7.0", true},
		{"latest", "1.7.0", false},
		{"1.7", "1.7.0", false},
		{"1.-1.0", "1.7.0", false},
		{"1.+2.0", "1.7.0", false},
	}
	for _, tc := range cases {
		if got := isNewerVersion(tc.latest, tc.current); got != tc.want {
			t.Errorf("isNewerVersion(%q, %q) = %v, want %v", tc.latest, tc.current, got, tc.want)
		}
	}
}

func TestVersionParts(t *testing.T) {
	got, ok := versionParts(" v12.34.56 ")
	if !ok || got != [3]int{12, 34, 56} {
		t.Fatalf("versionParts returned %v, %v", got, ok)
	}
	if _, ok := versionParts("1.2.3.4"); ok {
		t.Fatal("versionParts accepted too many components")
	}
}
